paṇṭit tamiḻa nākarikamum paṇpāṭum

patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.




cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭamoḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceyta
toru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etirpārppataṟ
killi. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintu
koḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil 
tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
*  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṅkilattil eḻutiya cāṉṟitaḻiṉ tamiḻākkam.

mukavuri
ivvulakil ettaṉiyō nākarikaṅkaḷ collap
paṭiṉum, aviyellām kīḻi nākarikam, mēli nākarikam eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum. ivviraṇṭuḷ, muṉṉatu muntiyatu; tamiḻaratu: piṉṉutu pintiyatu; āṅkilaratu.
cēmcu vāṭṭu (james Watt) eṉṉum āṅkilēyar 1765 ām āṇṭu nīrāvic cūḻcciyattik kaṇṭupiṭittatiliruntu, taṟkāla nākarikam toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṟku muntiyatellām muṟkāla nākarikamākum. iṉṟu pukivaṇṭi pala nāṭukaḷiṟ ceyyappaṭiṉum, mutaṉ mutalāyc ceyyappeṟṟa āṅkila
nāṭṭu vaṇṭiyē avaṟṟiṟkellām mūlamākum. atu pōṉṟē. nāṟṟiciyilum palvēṟu nāṭukaḷil paḻaṅkāla nākarikam irun
tiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟ kellām tamiḻa nākarikamē mūlamākum.
nīrāviyum miṉṉāṟṟaluṅ koṇṭu ceyyappeṟum poṟi (Machine) viṉiyum cūḻcciya (Engine) viṉiyum ikkāla nākarikameṉṟum; kiyālum vilaṅkālumē ceyyappaṭṭa vēlippāṭu muṟkāla nākarikam eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
cilar, taṟpukaḻcci paṟṟi, nākarikam eṉṉum colli eḷitākavum kuṟukiya nōkkōṭum āḷvar. iṭa vēṟupāṭum moḻi vēṟupāṭum paṟṟi nākarikam palvēṟu vakippaṭṭu
viṭātu. ovvoru nāṭṭaracāṭciyum oru taṉinākarikamaṉṟu. mēṉāṭukaḷuḷ mutaṉmutal nākarikamaṭintatu ekiptu (Egypt). ataṉ toṭakkam ki.mu. 4000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ, ataṟkum muntiyatu tamiḻ nākarikam. ataṉ tōṟṟam ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭu
kaṭkumuṉ. ākavē, tamiḻ nākarikamē ulakil mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyatākum. tamiḻiṉ mutupaḻantoṉmiyum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṉum uṇmiyum iti valiyuṟuttum. 
iṅṅaṉamiruppavum āriyac cūḻcciyālum āriya varukikkup piṟpaṭṭa mūvēntariṉ pētimiyālum, pal
vakippaṭṭa koṇṭāṉmāriṉ (viyāpurikaḷiṉ) kāṭṭik koṭuppālum, tamiḻ nākarikam mēṉāṭṭārkkut terinta aḷavukūṭat tamiḻarkkut teriyātu maṟiyuṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. ivviraṅkattakka nilimi tamiḻariṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku muṭṭuk kaṭṭiyāyiruttalāl, iti nīkkutaṟ poruṭṭu in nūli eḻutat tuṇintēṉ. iṟṟit tamiḻaruḷ nūṟṟiṟ keṇpatiṉmar taṟkuṟikaḷum tāymoḻiyuṇarcci yillātavarumāyiruppaṉum, pututtalimuṟiyāka muḷiviṭṭuk kiḷarnteḻum tamaḻa māṇavamaṇikaḷēṉum, itik karuttūṉṟip paṭittut tam muṉṉōriṉ nākarikap paṇpāṭṭip putukkip pōṟṟik kāppārāka.
innūl āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻanākarikattiyē kūṟavatāyiruppiṉum, mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum aḻintum aḻiyuṇṭum pōṉamiyālum, muṟkāla nākarikat toṭarcciyē piṟkāla nākarikamumātalālum, ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷellām piṟkāla nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟum kalveṭṭuk kaḷiṉiṉṟumē kāṭṭappeṟum eṉa aṟika.
innūli iyaṉṟa virivil cīruñ cevviyumāka acciṭttanta cīvaṉ accakarttārkku neñcāra naṉṟi kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
	allum pakalum aruṇā calameṉṉum
nalliṟiyaṉ meyppu navinīṅka - ollum
vakiyāl tiruntiya vāṟaṉṟō innūl
takiyāl viḷakkun taki.

paṇṭit tamiḻa 
nākarikamum paṇpāṭum



muṉṉuri
1. nākarikam eṉṉum colviḷakkam
nākarikam eṉpatu nakara makkaḷiṉ tiruntiya vāḻkki. nākarikam eṉṉum col nakarakam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripākum. (nakar + akam = nakarakam. nakarakam - nakarikam - nākarikam) ellā nāṭṭilum māntar mutal mutal nakara niliyilēyē nākarikamaṭintuḷḷaṉar. ataṉāl nakarap peyariṉiṉṟu nākarikap peyar tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. ciṟṟūrkaṭkum nakaraṅ
kaṭkum evvaḷavō toṭarpēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa ikkālattum, nākarikamillātavaṉ nāṭṭup puṟattāṉ eṉṟum paṭṭik kāṭṭāṉ eṉṟum iḻittuk kūṟutal kāṇka. nakarappati vāḻnar eṉṉum col nākarikamuḷḷōrik kuṟikkum ilakkiya vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka.
āṅkilattilum, nākarikattik kuṟikkum ilattīṉac col nakarap peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatē. L. Civitas, city or city - CIVIS citizen, L. civilis - E. civil - civilize.
nakaraṅkaḷ mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu uḻavut toḻiṟkuc ciṟanta maruta nilattilēyē. uḻavut toḻilum niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppum ūrpperukkamum nākarikam tōṉṟuvataṟkup peritum tuṇi ceytaṉa. uḻavut toḻilāl vēḷāṇmiyum, patiṉeṇ pakkat toḻilkaḷum, piṟatoḻil ceyvārkkum pōtiyavuṇavum, vāṇikamum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. niliyākak kuṭiyiruppataṉāl uḻavaṉ kuṭiyāṉavaṉ eṉṉap peṟṟāṉ. ilvāḻvāṉ eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷu varāṟ ciṟappittuc collap peṟṟavaṉum uḻavaṉē. ilvāḻ vāṉik kuṟikkum husbondi (house-dweller) eṉṉum paḻa nārvēyac colliṉiṉṟu uḻu allatu payir cey eṉṟu muṉpu poruḷpaṭṭa husband eṉṉum ēval viṉiccollum, uḻavaṉik kuṟikkum husbandman eṉṉum peyarc collum, uḻavut toḻilik kuṟikkum husbandry eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarum, āṅkilattil tōṉṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu. niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppāl oḻukkap poṟuppum, ūrp perukkamum āṭciyamippum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, maruta nilamum uḻavut toḻilum eṅṅaṉam nākarikattit toṟṟuvittaṉa eṉpatu teḷivākum.
nakar eṉṉum col, mutaṉ mutal, oru vaḷamaṉiyi allatu māḷikiyiyē kuṟittatu.
nakar = 1. vaḷamaṉi.
koḷakkoḷak kuṟi kaṭāk kūḻuṭi viyaṉakar              (puṟam. 70)
2. māḷiki
pāḻi yaṉṉa kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉakar 		      	  (akam. 15)
māḷiki aracaṉukkē ciṟappākavuriyatātalāl, nakar eṉṉum col araṇmaṉiyiyum aracaṉ maṉi pōṉṟa iṟivaṉ kōyiliyum piṉpu kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu.
nakar = 1 araṇmaṉi,
muricukeḻu celvar nakar   				 puṟam. 127)
nitituñcu viyaṉakar 			        (cilap. 27:200)
2. kōyil.
mukkaṭ celvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē 		  (puṟam. 6)
uttara kōcamaṅki miṉṉēṟu māṭa viyaṉmāḷikipāṭi (tiruvācakam, 16, 3)
eṉpatāl, kōyilukkum māḷikip peyaruṇmi aṟiyalām.
ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷ naṭipeṟum maṇṭapam māḷiki pōṉṟiruttalāl, tiruttakka tēvar nakar eṉṉum colli maṇṭapam eṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭār.
aṇinakar muṉṉi ṉāṉē			            (cīvaka. 701)
nakar eṉṉum col maṉiyik kuṟittalālēyē, maṉi, il, kuṭi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōl iṭavāku peyarāy maṉiviyiyum kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu.
nakar = maṉivi
varuvirun tōmpit taṉṉakar viḻiyakkūṭi 		(kalit. 8)
ciṟanta ōviya vēlippāṭamintu cippic cuṇṇāmpuc cāntiṉāl tīṟṟappeṟṟu veḷḷiyaṭikkappaṭṭa kāriccuvark kaṭṭaṭam, maṇcuvark kūri vīṭṭōṭu oppu nōkkumpōtu, mika viḷaṅkit tōṉṟalāl, māḷiki nakar eṉṉappaṭṭatu. nakutal viḷaṅkutal. naku - nakal - nakar. veṇpalliyum poṉmaṇi yaṇikalattiyum mukamalarcciyākiya cirippiyum uṇarttum, naki eṉṉum colli nōkkuka. naku - naki.
	oḷiyārmuṉ oḷḷiya rātal veḷiyārmuṉ
	vāṉcuti vaṇṇaṅ koḷal
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar kūṟṟāl, cippiccuṇṇāmpuc cāntiṉ veṇmic ciṟappu uṇarappaṭum.
māḷiki mutalil aracaṉukkē yuriyatāyiruntatiṉālum, aracaṉ vāḻum ūr pērūrāyiruntatiṉālum, nakar eṉṉum col ciṉiyāku peyarāyk kōnakariyum pērūriyuṅ kuṟittatu. iṉṟum, kūri vīṭukaḷ niṟinta oru nāṭṭuppuṟattūruṭaṉ kāri vīṭukaḷ niṟinta oru ciṟṟūr allatu pērūri oppu nōkkiṉāl, piṉṉatu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟuvatu veḷḷiṭi maliyām. paṇṭik kālattiṟ pērūrkaḷē kāri vīṭukaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉa.
neṭunakar viṉipuṉi nallil  		                (puṟam. 23)
eṉpatil, nakar eṉṉum col pērūrik kuṟittatu.
nakar eṉṉum col, taṉi māḷikiyiyum ataṉiyuṭiya pērūriyuṅ kuṟittatiṉāl, immayakki nīkkum poruṭṭu, pērūri maṭṭum kuṟittaṟku ikaravīṟu koṇṭa nakari eṉṉum col eḻuntatu. nakari (māḷikiyi) yuṭiyatu nakari. ikaram iṅkuk kāṭik kaṇṇi, nāṟkāli eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl oṉṟi uṭimiyi uṇarttum oṉṟaṉ pālīṟu. iṉṟu nakar eṉṉum col ulaka vaḻakkil māḷikiyi allatu vaḷamaṉiyi uṇarttāmiyāl, mēṟkūṟiya mayakkiṟku iṭamilli.
paṭṭi eṉpatu māṭṭup paṭṭiyuḷḷa ciṟṟūrik kuṟittatu pōṉṟē, nakar eṉpatum, māḷikiyuḷḷa pērūrik kuṟittatu. pati, eṉṉum collum vīṭṭiyum nakariyum kuṟittal kāṇka. nakar eṉṉum col, am eṉṉum perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟu nakaram eṉṟākum. atuvum taṉi māḷikiyiyum pērūriyum kuṟikkum. am īṟu perumip poruḷuṇarttali, illam, niliyam, matiyam (muḻu nilā), viḷakkam, mutaliya coṟkaḷiṟ kāṇka.
nakaram = 	1. araṇmaṉi (yāḻ. aka.)
		2. kōyil
mēḻivala ṉuyartta veḷḷi nakaramum 	         (cilap. 14 : 9)
viṇṭunakaram - viṇṇakaram = tirumāl kōyil.
3. pērūr
nakar, nakari, nakaram eṉṉum muccolvaṭivum, mutalil aracaṉ vāḻum talinakariyum avaṉ ceṉṟu taṅkum kōnakariyum kuṟittu, piṉpu ellāp pērūrkaḷiyum potuppaṭac cuṭṭalāyiṉa. ataṉāl, aracaṉ irukkum allatu taṅkum nakarit tali allatu kō eṉṉum aṭi koṭuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
kōnakar etirkoḷa 			      (cilap. 27:255)
aracaṉukkum teyvattiṟkum orupuṭi yoppumi yiruttalāl, kōyil eṉṉum coṟpōṉṟē kōnakar eṉṉum collum iruvar irukkiyiyum kuṟikkum.
kōyil = 1. araṇmaṉi.
kōyil maṉṉaṉik kuṟukiṉaḷ 		        (cilap. 20:47)
(kō = aracaṉ, il = vīṭu)
2. teyvap paṭimiyirukki.
kōnakar = kōyil.
māyōṉ kōnakar eṭṭum 	    (kantapu. tirunakarap. 88)
perunakari nakaram eṉṟu colliṉum pōtum. āyiṉ, avvilakkaṇam iṉṟu aṟiyappaṭāmiyāl, mānakar eṉṟu colla vēṇṭiyatākiṉṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam nākarikam eṉṉum coṟku mūlamāṉa nakar eṉṉum 
col tūya tamiḻāyiruppiṉum, nakara (Nagara) eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tirivākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akara mutaliyil (akarātiyil) kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷatu. itu tamiḻariṉ aṟiyāmiyiyum tamiḻp pērāciriyariṉ aṭimit taṉattiyumē uṇarttum. nakar eṉṉum mūla vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyililli. ataṉ tirivāṉa nakaram, eṉṉum vaṭivē, vēruntūrum moḻipporuṭ karaṇiyamumiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi yil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. nakari eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil (nakarī eṉṟu īṟu nīṇṭu) vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṉ īṟṟiṟku aṅkut taṉip poruḷilli.
nakar eṉṉum colliṟku viḷaṅkiya veṇmiyeṉpatē vērpporuḷeṉpati, veṇpallik kuṟikkum nakār eṉṉum colliyum; māḷikiyiṉ viḷaṅkiya tōṟṟam ataṉ veṇ cāntiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa teṉpati,
putuppiṟi yaṉṉa cuticey māṭattu		     (378)
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟaṭiyiyum 	
       varuṅkuṉṟa moṉṟurit tōṉtilli yampala vaṉmalayat
	tiruṅkuṉṟa vāṇar iḷaṅkoṭi yēyiṭar eytalemmūrp
	paruṅkuṉṟa māḷiki nuṇkaḷa pattoḷi pāyanummūrk
	karuṅkuṉṟam veṇṇiṟak kañcukam ēykkum kaṇaṅkuḻiyē  (15)
eṉṉum tirukkōvic ceyyuḷiyum; nōkkit teḷika. iṉi, naki eṉṉum col palliyum muttiyum kuṟittaliyum nōkkuka.
nakaram eṉṉum teṉ collip pōṉṟē ataṉiṉṟum tirin
taminta nākarikam eṉṉum teṉ colliyum, vaṭavar kaṭaṉkoṇṭu vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅki varuvatuṭaṉ, kaṭukaḷavum uṇmiyum naṉṟi
yaṟivumiṉṟi avaṟṟi vaṭacollēyeṉṟu valittum varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻi oru taṉimoḻiyaṉṟeṉpatum, kuṟinta pakkam aintiliru pakuti tamiḻeṉpatum, eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu  eṉṉum nūlil veḷḷiṭi maliyāy viḷakkappeṟum. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
2. paṇpāṭu eṉṉum col viḷakkam
paṇpaṭuvatu paṇpāṭu. paṇpaṭutal cīrppaṭutal, allatu tiruntutal. tiruntiya nilattip paṇpaṭṭa allatu paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭa nilameṉṟum, tiruntiya tamiḻip paṇpaṭṭa centamiḻ (taṉippāṭal) eṉṟum, tiruntiya vuḷḷattip paṇpaṭṭa vuḷḷa meṉṟum, colvatu vaḻakkam.
paṇ eṉṉum peyarc coṟku mūlamāṉa paṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉic collum, ciṟappāka āḷappeṟum pōtu, palvēṟu viṉikaḷit tiruntac ceytaliyum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷic cevviyāy amittaliyum, kuṟikkum.
paṇṇutal = 1. nilattit tiruttutal.
(paṇṇappaṭṭa marutanilam paṇṇi.)
2. ūrtiyit takutip paṭuttutal.
pūtanūl yāṉiyoṭu puṉitēr paṇṇavum	      	 (puṟam. 12)
3. cuvaṭittal (alaṅkarittal)
paṭṭamo ṭilaṅkal paṇṇi  	        	     (cūḷā. kalyā. 14)
4. iciyala kamittal.
paṇṇal parivaṭṭaṇi yārāytal		    (cīvaka. 657, uri)
5. paṇ amittal.
marutam paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ	       (malipaṭu. 534.)
6. camittal.
pālu mitaviyum paṇṇātu peṟukuvir	      (malipaṭu. 417.)
cey eṉṉum viṉic colliṉiṉṟu, tirunta allatu aḻakāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, cey eṉṉum nilappeyarum ceyyuḷ eṉṉum iyaṟṟamiḻppāṭṭiṉ peyarum tōṉṟiyiruppatu pōṉṟē; paṇ eṉṉum viṉic colliṉiṉṟum, tirunta allatu iṉitāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, paṇṇi eṉṉum nilap peyarum paṇ eṉṉum icit tamiḻ amippiṉ peyarum; tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
paṇpāṭu pala poruṭku uriyatēṉum, nilamum makkaḷ uḷḷamum paṟṟiyē peruvaḻakkākap pēcap peṟum. āṅkilattilum culture eṉṉum peyarc col ciṟappāka nilap paṇpāṭṭiyum uḷap paṇpāṭṭiyum kuṟippatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. cultivate eṉṉum viṉiccollum aṅṅaṉamē. ivviru vakip paṇpāṭṭuḷḷum, makkaḷit taḻuviya uḷap paṇpāṭē ciṟappākac koḷḷavum collavum peṟum.
cēlaṅ kallūri mēṉāḷ mutalvar pērā. irāmacāmik kavuṇṭar kuṭumpam paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻk kuṭumpattiṟkum, tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻit talimiyākap pōṟṟum ceṭṭikuḷam paṇpaṭṭa vūrukkum, tali ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
uḷḷam paṇpaṭuvatu perumpālum kalviyālātalāl, paṇpāṭu kalvi mikutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tamiḻil taṉmiyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, iyalpu eṉpatu iyaṟkiyāṉa taṉmiyiyum, paṇpu eṉpatu paṇpaṭuttappeṟṟa nalla taṉmiyiyum, kuṟikkum.
3. nākarikattiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭu
nākarikam eṉpatu tiruntiya vāḻkki. atu ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum tamakkē payaṉpaṭuttuvatu. paṇpāṭu eṉpatu tiruntiya oḻukkam. atu ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭuttuvatu.
ilakkaṇap piḻiyiṉṟip pēcuvatum, ellāvakiyilum tuppuravāyiruppatum, kāṟṟōṭṭamuḷḷatum uṭal nalattiṟkēṟṟatu māṉa vīṭṭiṟ kuṭiyiruppatum, naṉṟāyc camittu uṇpatum, piṟarukkut tīṅku ceyyāmiyum, nākarikak kūṟukaḷām; eḷi yāriṭattum iṉitākap pēcuvatum, putitāy vanta oḻukkamuḷḷa ayalāri viruntōmpuvatum, irappōrkkiṭuvatum, iyaṉṟavari piṟarukkutavuvatum, koḷkiyum māṉamuṅkeṭiṉ uyiri viṭuvatum, paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷām. curuṅkac colliṉ uḷḷattiṉ cemmi paṇpāṭum, uḷḷattiṟkup puṟampāṉa uṇavu, uṭi, uṟiyuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cemmi nākarikamum ākum. ākavē, ivaṟṟi muṟiyē akanākarikam, puṟanākarikam eṉak koḷḷiṉum poruntum.
māḷikiyiṟ kuṭiyiruppatum, paṭṭāṭi yaṇivatum, aṟucuvi yuṇṭi yuṇpatum, iṉṉiyaṅkiyil allatu pukivaṇṭi mutal vakuppiṟ celvatum, vēlikkārari vittāḷvatum, poṟikaḷik koṇṭu viṉi ceyvatum, iṉpamāyp poḻutu pōkkuvatum, celva vāḻkkiyēyaṉṟi nākarika vāḻkki yākā.
	neṭiya moḻitalum kaṭiya ūrtalum
	celvam aṉṟutaṉ ceyviṉip payaṉē
	cāṉṟōr celvam eṉpatu cērntōr
	puṉkaṉ añcum paṇpiṉ
	meṉkaṭ celvam celvameṉ patuvē
eṉṟu naṟṟiṇiyum (210 : 5-9) kūṟutal kāṇka. neṭiya moḻital  = aracarāl mārāyam peṟappaṭutal. kaṭiya ūrtal = virinta celaviṉi
yuṭiya kutiri yāṉi tēr mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mēl ēṟic cellutal. celvam aṉṟu = nākarikam aṉṟu. meṉkaṭ celvam = kaṇṇōṭṭam eṉṉum paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇam.
nākarikattiṉum uyarnta niliyē paṇpāṭāyiṉum, nākarikamiṉ ṟiyum paṇpāṭuṇṭu. kāṭṭil taṅkum muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻumuṉivar āṭiyiṉṟiyum nīrāṭātum maṇṇil iruppar; āyiṉum, avar paṇpāṭṭil taliciṟantavarāvar. karavātu karintu iṉattōṭu uṇṇum kākkiyum, vēlāṟ kuttiya viṭattum vālāṭṭum naṉṟiya ṟivuḷḷa nāyum, oru kālattil orē peṭiyōṭu kūṭivāḻum vāṉap paṟaviyiṉamum, nākarikamillā aḵṟiṇi yāyiṉum paṇpāṭṭil makkaḷiṉum viñciyaviyē.
	makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum (marapiyal, 33),
	karuṅkaṭ ṭākkali perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉak
	kimmi yuyyāk kāmar manti
	kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kiḷimutaṟ cērtti
	ōṅkuvari yaṭukkattup pāyntuyir cekukkum
	cāral nāṭa
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuṭ pakutiyum (69), iṅkuk karutat takkaṉa.
paṇṭik kālattil, nākarikam eṉṉum colliyē paṇpā ṭeṉṉum poruḷilum āṇṭaṉar. ataṉālēyē, paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇamāṉa kaṇṇōṭṭatti nākarikam eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
	peyakkaṇṭum nañcuṇ ṭamivar nayattakka
	nākarikam vēṇṭu pavar			           (kuṟaḷ 580)
tiruntiya nili eṉṉum potuk karuttil, nākarikamum paṇpāṭum oṉṟātal kāṇka.
paṇpāṭu nākarikattiṉum ciṟantatāyiṉum, nāṭṭilum nakarilum kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyil vāḻpavar nākarikamiṉṟi yiruttal kūṭātu. kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyil nākarikamiṉṟiyiruppavarkkup paṇpāṭum iruttal muṭiyātu. ūriliruppavar ulakappaṟṟi nīttavarēṉum, uṭal nīṅkum vari nīrccīliyāvatu aṇital vēṇṭum.
4. intiya nākarikam tamiḻaratē yātal
vēta āriyar mēliyāciyāviṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālam ki. mu. 2000 - 1500. aṉṟu avar kaṉṟukālikaḷi ōṭṭit tiriyum nāṭōṭikaḷāyum, mulli nākarika niliyiṉarāyumē yiruntaṉar. avarukku ilakkiyamumilli; eḻuttumilli. avar pēciya moḻi kirēkkattiṟku iṉamāyum paḻampāracīkattiṟku mika neruṅkiyatāyum colvaḷamaṟṟatāyum iruntatu. intiyāviṟ kālūṉṟiya piṉṉarē, avar irukku eṉṉum tam mutal vētatti yāttaṉar. avvēta moḻi vaṭintiyap pirākirutattiyum tiraviṭattiyum taḻuviyateṉpatu atil ekara okarak kuṟiliṉmi yālum pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṇmiyālum teriyavarukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum aṟiyāta varalāṟṟā ciriyar, āriya vētakkālattiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟit toṭaṅkum tavaṟāṉa vaḻakkam iṉṉum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ vēta āriyattiṟku muntiyatāyiruppa tōṭu vētap peyarkaḷē tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ tiripākavumirukkiṉṟaṉa.
vētam : viḻittal = pārttal, kāṇutal, aṟital.
		viḻi = aṟivu, ōti (ñāṉam).
		tēṟār viḻiyilā māntar 		 (tirumantiram, 177)
		viḻi - L.Vide - Vise; Skt.Vid - Veda.
		o.nō: kuḻal - kuṭal; oṭi - oci.
curuti: 	ceviyuṟutal = kēṭṭal
		ceviyuṟu - cru - cruti = kēḷvi.
		mutaṟkālattil eḻutappeṟātu kēṭṭēyaṟiyappaṭṭu				vanta āriya maṟi.
maṇṭalam: mul - muṉ - muṉi = vil.
		mul - mur - muri. murital = vaḷital.
	muri - mūri = vaḷivu.
		mur - muru - muṟuku. muṟukutal = vaḷinta kātaṇi.
	mur - muru - muṟuku. muṟukutal = vaḷital; tirukutal.
	muṟuku - muṟukku = tirukal, tirukiya tiṉpaṇṭam.
	muṟukku - muṟukkam.
	muṟu - muṟṟu - muṟṟuki = nakaric cūḻki.
	muṟu - muṟi - miṟi = vaḷivu.
	muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = vaḷital,
	muṟi - maṟi. maṟital = vaḷital, maṭaṅkutal.
		mul - (muḷ) - muṇṭu = uruṭci,tiraṭci.
	muṇṭu - muṇṭi = uruṇṭi, muṭṭi.
muṇṭi viḷipaḻam 			           (patiṟ. 60:6)
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭi
muḷ - (muṇ) - muṇam - muṇaṅku. muṇaṅkutal = uḷ vaḷital.
muṇam - muṭam = vaḷivu.
muṭam - muṭaṅku. muṭaṅkutal = vaḷital.
muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. muṭaṅku - muṭakku = maṭakku.
muṭam - (muṭal) - muṭali = kuṟaṭu, uruṇṭi.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutul = vaḷital.
maṇṭu - maṇṭi, maṇṭiyiṭal = muḻaṅkāli maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam, vaṭṭam. nāṭṭuppakuti, kālappakuti, nūṟ pakuti.
koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali, maṇṭalittal = ceyyuḷiṉ ellā aṭikaḷum aḷavottu varutal. īṟu toṭaṅkiyil (antātiyil) mutalum īṟum oṉṟittu varutal.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam =	1.vaṭṭam
				2.vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi.
				3.vaṭṭamāṉa cuṭar.
				(ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ)
				4.nilap pakuti.
maṇṭilat tarumiyum			 (tol.akat.41)
				5.aḷavotta aṭikaḷik koṇṭa 					   ceyyuḷ.
a - i. alam-ilam.
o.nō. aṉam - iṉam. e.ṭu.paṭṭaṉam - paṭṭiṉam.
intiya āriyar piṟkālattil tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭi vaḻakki allatu pottaka moḻiyi amittu, atiṟ paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭaṉar. itu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkappeṟum.
tamiḻar teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ. tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu muḻukip
pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭattil 50,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ.  tamiḻ nākarikam tōṉṟiyatu 20,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ. tamiḻilakkaṇa vilakkiyam tōṉṟitu ki.mu.10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ. itaṉ viḷakkatti eṉ`tamiḻ varalāṟu eṉṉum nūluṭ kāṇka.
intiya nākarika aṭippaṭi tamiḻ nākarikamē. āyiṉum, iṉṟu atu aṟiyappaṭāmaliruppataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam): āriyavaru kikku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiyam aṉittum aḻintum aḻikkap paṭṭum pōṉamiyum, āriyak kulap piriviṉiyāl tamiḻar pala vakiyilum tāḻttappaṭṭiruppatum; ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāka, intiyak kalikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum moḻi peyarttu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷē iṉṟiruppatum, avaṟṟiyellām mutaṉūlkaḷē yeṉṟu vaṭavar valippatum, kulavariciyilum kalvittuṟiyilum āriyar uyarttappaṭṭiruppatumē.
oru tiruṭaṉ tāṉ tiruṭiya poruḷit taṉateṉṟē valippiṉum, apporuḷiṉ varalāṟu atu piṟaṉatu eṉpati meyppippatupōl, tamiḻ varalāṟu, tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu ākiya mūṉṟum, intiya nākarikam tamiḻaratē eṉpati aiyantiripaṟa nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  āyiṉum, ivvaralāṟṟi mēliyar aṟiyār. tamiḻarum ituvari avarukku aṟivittilar.  iṟṟit tamiḻil vaṭacoṟkaḷum tamiḻilakkiyattil vaṭanūl muṟikaḷum āriyak karuttukaḷum mikatiyākap pukuttap
paṭṭiruppatum, kōyil vaḻipāṭu, camaṟkirutattil pirāmaṇap pūcāriyarāl naṭipeṟṟu varuvatum, tamiḻariṉ iyaṟpeyar perumpālum vaṭacollā yiruppatum, tamiḻik kāṭṭikkoṭukkum tannalattār talimip patavi tāṅkikkoṇṭu tamiḻap potu makkaṭkut teriyātapaṭi tamiḻ nākarikatti maṟippatum, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟi mēliyarkku meypōṟ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
	poyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colvaṉ miyiṉāl
	meypōlummē meppōlummē.
	meyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colamāṭ ṭāmiyāl
	poypō lummē poypō lummē.
eṉṉum veṟṟivēṟki veṇ centuṟikaṭkēṟpa, āriyar tamiḻar nilikaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa.  āyiṉum potuviyalpiṟku vilakkāka kilpeṭṭu cilēṟṟar (Gilbert Slater) eṉṉum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka mēṉāṭ poruḷiyal nūṟ pērāciriyar, tam intiya nākarikattil tiraviṭak kūṟu (Dravidian Element in Indian culture) eṉṉum nūlil,
tirāviṭar iṅṅaṉam moḻiyil āriyap paṭuttap paṭṭapōtu, āriyar nākarikap paṇpāṭṭil tiraviṭappaṭuttap peṟṟaṉar (“While the Dravidians were thus Aryanised in language the Aryans were Dravidised in culture.”) eṉṟu naṭunilit tīrppuk kūṟiyiruppatu, tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum makiḻntu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu.  ittīrppip piṉvarum nūl valiyuṟuttal kāṇka.
intiya nākarikam maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka nākarikamē intiya āriyarateṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku, vētakālatti aḷaviṟantu muṉ taḷḷi vikkum muyaṟciyoṉṟu iṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. aṟiyappaṭṭa tampiyiṉ akavi (vayatu) uyarttik kūṟappaṭiṉ, aṇṇaṉiṉ akavi tāṉē uyartal kāṇka.
5.intiya nākarikam āriyara teṉak kāṭṭak   			  kiyāḷappaṭum vaḻikaḷ.			
1	paḻantamiḻnāṭākiya kumarikkaṇṭa varalāṟṟi maṟittalum maṟuttalum.
2.	pāṇṭiyar niṟuviya muttamiḻk kaḻaka uṇmiyi maṟuttal.
3.	tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟi vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum vētakkālatti ṉiṉṟum toṭaṅkal.
4.	teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷākiya tamiḻari vantēṟi kaḷākavum kalaviyiṉamākavuṅ kāṭṭal.
5.	tamiḻ muṉṉūlkaḷiyum mutaṉūlkaḷiyum piṉṉūl kaḷākavum vaḻi nūlkaḷākavum kāṭṭal.
6.	kumarikkaṇṭa iṭappeyarkaḷiyum teyvap peyarkaḷiyum mūvēntar kuṭippeyarkaḷiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭal.
7.	kaṭṭukkatikaḷiyum āriyac coṟkaḷiyum pukutti, iruperun tamiḻc camayaṅkaḷākiya civaneṟiyiyum tirumāl neṟiyiyum āriya vaṇṇamākkalum, tamiḻik kōyil vaḻi pāṭṭiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷalum.
8.	mūvēntar pētimiyāl āriyam vēruṉṟiya kaṭikkaḻakak kāla nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu āriyac cārpāṉa cāṉṟu kāṭṭal.
9.	tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkiḷiyeṉṟu ayalār karutumāṟu, aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaṭkellām valintum nalintum vaṭacoṉ mūlaṅ kāṭṭal.
10. vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum, vētakkāla āriyap pūcāriyarāl pukuttappaṭṭa ēmāṟṟuk karuttukkaḷiyum, piṟapput toṭarpāṉa kulappiri viṉiyiyum, toṭarntu pōṟṟal.
11.	camayaccārpāṉa coṟpoḻivukaḷāṟ potu makkaḷi aṟiyāmiyil amiḻttutal.
12.	uṇmi kūṟum tamiḻp pulavarkku aluvaṟ pataviyillāvāṟu  ceytal.
ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkatti eṉ `tamiḻ varalāṟu' tamiḻar varalāṟu' `tamiḻar matam' eṉṉum nūlkaḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.
6.kumarik kaṇṭa iṭap peyarum mūvēntar kuṭip peyarum
iṭappeyar: 
kumari
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭum teṉ māvāriyil 
muḻukippōṉa perunilamumāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi yaṭuttu, paṉimali (imayam)pōlum oru māperu malit toṭar iruntatu.  ataṉ peyar kumari. ataṉālēyē muḻukippōṉa nilamum kumarik kaṇṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
	paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
	kumarikkōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa             (cilap.11:19-20)
kumari eṉpatu kāḷiyiṉ peyar. kāḷi tamiḻar teyvaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iṟavātavaḷ allatu eṉṟum iḷamiyāyiruppavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil, kāḷiyik kumari eṉṟaṉar.
kummutal tiraḷtal. kummal - kummali = paruttavaḷ. kum - kumal - kumar = tiraṭci, tiraṇṭa kaṉṉippeṇ, kaṉṉimi.
kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭa iḷiñaṉ. iḷiñaṉāṉa murukaṉ. kumar - kumari = tiraṇṭa iḷiñi(kaṉṉi).
maṇapparuvamāṉa iḷami varumpōtu uṭal tiraḷutal iyalpu. poliyum paruva vilaṅkukaḷiyum muṭṭiyiṭum paruvap paṟavi kaḷiyum viṭi eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. viṭittal paruttal.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kumāra, kumārī eṉa nīṭṭi, muṟiyē, makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiṟ ciṟappāy āḷvatuṭaṉ kumāra eṉpatik ku + māra eṉap pirittu, mariyātavaṉ eṉṟu poruntap poyttalākap poruḷ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ivviru coṟkaṭkum iḷamip poruḷē ciṟantiruttali, vaḻakku nōkkiyum, kōṭic cēlikku oru veḷḷi, kumarip peṇṇukku oru piḷḷi eṉṉum paḻamoḻi nōkkiyum, uṇarka.
iḷamikkuppiṉ mūppum cākkāṭum nērvatāl iḷamik karuttiṉṟē aḻiyāmik karuttut tōṉṟum. evvaḷavu uṭaluram peṟṟavarāyiṉum nakiccuviyum uvamiyum paṟṟiyaṉṟik kiḻavaṉ kiḻaviyik kumaraṉ kumariyeṉṉum vaḻakka miṉmi kāṇka.
kumarik kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyil, kumari eṉa oru pēriyāṟu miruntatu.
matiri-maturi
maturi eṉpatu kumari malit toṭariṟ piṟantu kiḻakku mukamāy ōṭik kīḻ kaṭaliṟ kalantatum, kaṅki pōlum periyatumāṉa paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkariyil aminta pāṇṭiyariṉ mutal talinakarum talikkaḻaka irukkiyumākum.
pāṇṭiyar tiṅkaḷ  kulattārākaliṉ, tam kula mutalākak karutiya matiyiṉ peyarāl, tam mutal talinakarkku matiri eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. atu piṉṉar maturi eṉattirintatu.
o.nō: kuti - kutiri,etirki - etuki.
kumarimali muḻukumuṉpō muḻukiya piṉpō, kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē, kaṇṇapirāṉ vāḻnta maturiyiyum amittaṉar. ataṟku appeyariṭṭatu, avartam muṉṉōr iṭatti niṉivu kūrtaṟporuṭṭākum. kaṇṇapirāṉ oru tiraviṭa maṉṉaṉē eṉpatu, eṉ tamiḻar varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkap peṟum.
kaṇṇapirāṉ kālamākiya pāratak kālattil viki maturiyilli. ātalāl, nāvalantēyattil iraṇṭāvatu ēṟpaṭṭatum vaṭamaturi yeṉap paṭṭatum kaṇṇaṉ maturiyē. ataṉ peyar annāṭṭu moḻikkēṟpa matrā eṉap piṉṉart tirintatu.
civaperumāṉ taṉ caṭimuṭiyiluḷḷa matiyiṉiṉṟu matuvip poḻinta iṭam maturi eṉpatu, tolkatikkaṭṭu.
viki maturiyic cārnta tirumaruta muṉṟuṟiyāl maturip peyar vanta teṉpatu, pēraṉ peyarāl pāṭṭaṉ peyar peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum kūṟṟi okkum. maturiyeṉṟu mutaliṟ peyar peṟṟatu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkariyatu, eṉpati maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu.
kaṉṉi
muḻukippōṉa kumarik kaṇṭam, teṉ kōṭiyil kumari eṉṉum malit toṭariyum vaṭakōṭiyil kumari eṉṉum pēriyāṟṟiyum koṇṭiruntami, muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyira aṭiyiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭatu kumariyāṟē.
parataṉ eṉṉum māvēntaṉiṉ makaḷākiya kumariyiṉ peyarāl, peyar peṟṟatu kumarik kaṇṭam eṉpatum, tolkatikkaṭṭē.
kumari, kaṉṉi eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷātalāl, kumariyāṟu kaṉṉiyeṉavumpaṭum.
	maṉṉu māli veṇkuṭiyāṉ
	 	vaḷiyāc ceṅkō latuvōccik
	kaṉṉi taṉṉip puṇarntālum
		pulavāy vāḻi kāvēri
eṉṉum cilappatikārak kāṉal varippāṭal, kumariyāṟṟik kaṉṉi yeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṉṉi eṉpatum kāḷiyiṉ peyarē.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum col, makkaḷiṉattup peṇṇik kuṟikkum pōtu, pūppaṭintu maṇamākāta peṇṇik kuṟikkum. oru peṇ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇamākātirukkalāmātalāl, iḷaṅkaṉṉi kaṉṉi
kiyeṉappaṭuvāḷ. ki eṉpatu oru kuṟumipporuḷ piṉṉoṭṭu (Diminutive suffix).
o.nō: kuṭi(vīṭu) - kuṭiki (ciṟuvīṭu) - kuṭici.
pūppaṭiyāta ciṟumiyiyum maṇamāṉa peṇṇiyum, kaṉṉi yeṉṟu collum vaḻakkamilli. kaṉṉi kaḻital, kaṉṉiyaḻital, kaṉṉiyaḻittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum collik kaṉyā eṉṟum, kaṉṉiki eṉṉum collik kaṉyakā eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tirittu, ciṟumi, makaḷ eṉṟa poruḷkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvar. ataṟkēṟpa, kaṉ (tikaḻ), kaṉa (ciṟu) eṉpavaṟṟi vērākak kāṭṭuvar.
tikaḻtalik kuṟikkum kaṉṉeṉum col valintu poruttu vatākum. ciṟumiyik kuṟikkum kaṉa eṉṉum col pūppaṭinta peṇṇiṟkup poruntātu. mikac ciṟiyatik kuṉṉiyeṉpatu tamiḻmarapu. kul - kuṉ - kuṉṉi. kul - kuḷ - kuṟu, naṉṉiyum kuṉṉiyum eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭuvaḻakku. kaṉ - kuṉ - kaṉ (kana).
kaṉṉutal eṉpatu paḻuttalik kuṟikkum ōr aruntamiḻc col. veppattiṉalāvatu aḻuttattiṉālāvatu uḷḷaṅkiyilum uḷḷaṅ
kālilum arattaṅkaṭṭic civantuviṭṭāl ati arattaṅ kaṉṉutal eṉpar. kaṉi(paḻam) eṉṉum col kaṉṉi (paḻuttatu) eṉpataṉ tokuttalē. naku eṉṉum mutaṉili vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉapiṉ, naki eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar mutaṉiliyāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatē kaṉi eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum. pūppu eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē, kaṉṉutal eṉpatum nilittiṇikkuriya tāyiruppatum, mature eṉṉum āṅkila vaḻakkum, iṅkuk karutattakkaṉa.
mūvēntar kuṭippeyar
paḻaṅkuṭi eṉṉum coṟku, cēra cōḻa paṇṭiyar pōlap paṭippukkālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭu varuṅkuṭi eṉṟu, parimēlaḻakar urittaṟ kēṟpa,
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
eṉṟu tolkāppiyarum (cey.78) mūvēntar kuṭittoṉmi yiyum ciṟappiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
mutaṟkālattil muṭiyaṇiyum urimi cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkkē yiruntatāl, avar talimiyāyirunta kālamellām kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiṟku muṉṉarēyē. kuṟunila maṉṉar palar mūvēntark kaṭaṅkā miyi, kaṭikkaḻakap paṉuvalkaḷum pāṭalkaḷum terivikkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉ. kaḷappāḷar (kaḷappirar) āṭciyum pallavar āṭciyum piṟamoḻiyāḷar āṭciyum iṭiyiṭṭiṭiyiṭṭu vantuviṭṭaṉa.
āriyar varumuṉ, nāvalantēyam muḻutum mūvēntar nēraṭi āṭcik
kuṭpaṭāviṭiṉum atikārattiṟkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. mati, katiravaṉ, neruppu ākiya muccuṭariyum, muṟiyē, pāṇṭiyarum cōḻarum cērarumē tamkula mutalākak koṇṭiruntaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ
mūvēntaruḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉ muntiyavaṉ eṉpatu,
	paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
	kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
	vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamum koṇṭu
	teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi
eṉṉum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟṟut (cilap.10:18-21)terivikkum. itaṉālum, paḻiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa iru kuṟu nila maṉṉar iruntatālum, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarip paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirippar. āyiṉ kāḷiyik kuṟikkum pāṇṭi allatu, pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu appeyar vantatākak koḷvatē mikap poruttamām.
pāṇṭi = erutu (paripā.20:17 kuṟippu). pāṇṭi - pāṇṭiyam =  erutu,``ceñcuvaṟ pāṇṭiyam'' (peruṅ. uñcik 38:32). pāli nilattalivaṉum vayavaṉum (vīraṉum) kāḷiyeṉap paṭutal iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
maṭuttavā yellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ iṭarppā ṭuṭittu eṉṉum kuṟaḷum (624) iṅku nōkkattakkatām.
pāratap pōrkku muntiyē pāṇṭiyar iruntatāl pāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum collip pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkku mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu, varalāṟṟaṟivillār kūṟṟām. pāṇṭava kuravar kuṭiyāṉa parata marapu. teṉṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟa pāṇṭiyar kuṭik kiḷiyē. itaṉ viḷakkatti eṉ tamiḻar marapu eṉṉum nūluṭ kāṇka.
	vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
	cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
	teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
	naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭittu.
eṉṟār auviyār.
cōḻa nāṭṭiṟkup puṉalnāṭu, vaḷanāṭu eṉṟum, cōḻaṉukku vaḷavaṉ eṉṟum peyar. cōṟeṉpatu neṟ cōṟē. col = nel. col - coṉṟi, cōṟu.
col iyaṟkiyākavō mikutiyākavō viḷintatu cōḻa nāṭeṉappaṭṭatu. collākkat tirivil lakaram ḻakaramāvatu iyalpē.
o.nō: kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāḻakam = karumi.
kil - kīḻ - tōṇṭu. kel - kēḻal = tōṇṭum paṉṟi.
cul (vaḷi) - cūḻ.
tul (poruntu) - tōḻam - tōḻaṉ. pul (tuḷi)- pūḻi. pol (piḷa) - pōḻ.
tacarataṉiṉ muṉṉōrākac collappaṭṭa mucukuntaṉ, cipi mutaliyōr vaṭa nāṭṭiyum āṇṭa cōḻa māvēntarē.
	veṟṟivēl maṉṉaṟ kuṟṟati oḻikkeṉat
	tēvar kōmāṉ ēvaliṟ pōnta
	kāvaṟ pūtattuk kaṭikeḻu pīṭiki
eṉpatu (cilap. 5:65-67), mucukuntaṉiyum,
	tūṅkeyil eṟinta toṭittōṭ cempiyaṉ
	viṇṇavar talivaṉi vaṇaṅkimuṉ niṉṟu
	maṇṇakat teṉṟaṉ vāṉpati taṉṉuḷ
	mēlōr viḻiya viḻākkōḷ eṭutta
	nālēḻ nāḷiṉum naṉkiṉi tuṟikeṉa
	amarar talivaṉ āṅkatu nērntatu''      	   (aṇimē. 1: 4-9)
	kūrukirp paruntiṉ ēṟukuṟit torīit
	taṉṉakam pukka kuṟunaṭip puṟaviṉ
	taputi yañcic cīri pukka
	variyā īki uravōṉ maruka'' 		        (puṟam. 43: 5-8)
eṉpaṉa,cipiyiyum avaṉ vaḻiyiṉariyum uṟiyūrilum pukārililu miruntāṇṭa cōḻa vēntarākavē kuṟippāyuṇarttutal kāṇka. cipi eṉpataṉ tamiḻ vaṭivam cimpi allatu cempi eṉṟiruntirukkalām. varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya kālattil, cōḻaṉukkup paṉimalivariyum āṭciyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal teṟkē nīṇṭirunta kumarik kaṇṭam paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāyiruntati nōkkum pōtu, paṉimalivari cōḻaṉāṭci paraviyiruntatu viyappākat tōṉṟātu. vēṅkaṭattiṟku vaṭakkiluḷḷa koṭuntamiḻnāṭu vaṭuka nāṭāyt tirintapiṉ, annāṭṭuc cōḻar kuṭiyiṉar cōṭar eṉappaṭṭār.
cēraṉ
mēṟpuṟamāyiṉum kīḻppuṟamāyiṉum, cēranāṭu muḻuvatum perumpālum mēṟkut toṭarcci kiḻakkut toṭarcci malikaḷiṉ aṭivārac carivē. maliyaṭivāram cāral eṉappaṭum.
cāral nāṭa cevviyi yākumati  		         (kuṟun. 18:2)
 cāral nāṭa naṭunāḷ  				(kuṟun. 9)
 cāral nāṭa vāralō eṉavē  		       (kuṟun. 141:8)
eṉpaṉa, malippakka nāṭṭic cāral nāṭu eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
cāral - cēral - cēralaṉ - cēraṉ. maliyaraṇik kuṟikkum kuṟumpu eṉṉum coṟpōl, cēral eṉpatum iṭavāku peyarāy aracaṉi uṇarttum.
cēralaṉ - kēraḷaṉ
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉilum ciṟanta māvali eṉṉum cēra vēntaṉiṉ āṭci, vēmpāy (Mumbay)  variyum paraviyiruntatu.
I. paṇṭit tamiḻ nākarikam
1. moḻi
potumakkaḷ amippu: oru nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ nākarika niliyik kāṭṭuvatu avar tāy moḻiyē. ennāṭṭilum, moḻi potumakkaḷ amippeṉṟum ilakkiyam pulamakkaḷ amippeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṭukuṟi yetuvumiṉṟi oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷum nuṇporuṭ pākupāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum niṟintu, pakuttaṟiviṟ kotta ciṟanta ilakkaṇam amintu, ellāk karuttukkaḷiyum  taṉ coṟkaḷālēyē teḷḷat teḷivākat terivippatu taliciṟanta moḻiyām.
muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭat teṉ kōṭiyaṭuttu, nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum uṇavuvaḷamum poṉ vaḷamum maṇi vaḷamum niṟintirunta paḻantamiḻnāṭṭil, vāḻntuvanta mutaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ ikkālap pulavariṉum ciṟanta nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṉa rātaliṉ, kāṭci, karuttu eṉṉum iruvakip poruḷkaḷiyum kūrntu nōkkiyum nuṇukiyārāyntum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpiṟ kēṟpa aḻakiya poruṭ (karaṇiyak) kuṟikaḷākavē ellāc coṟkaḷiyum vēṟupaṭuttal, oṉṟu paṭuttal, iṉappaṭuttal eṉṉum mummuṟiyil amittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vēṟupaṭuttal
nālvakiyili :
cila pūṇṭukaḷilum, ceṭi koṭikaḷilum, vāḻi, mā, puḷi mutaliya maraṅkaḷilum, kiḷi nuṉiyiṟ kāmpuṭaṉ taṉittum aṭarntum perumpālum muṭṭi vaṭivil melliyatāyiruppatu ili.
pullilum nel, varaku pōṉṟa payirkaḷilum, nuṉiyilum taṇṭiyoṭṭiyum īṭṭipōl oṭuṅki nīṇṭu curacurappāyiruppatu tāḷ.
karumpilum, cōḷam, nāṇal pōṉṟa peruntaṭṭikaḷilum, nuṉiyilum  kaṇuvoṭṭiyum peruntāḷāka ōṅki maṭintu toṅkuvatu tōki.
teṉṉi, paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ pōl, nuṉiyiyum aṭiyiyum oruṅkē oṭṭiyē taṉittaṉi maṭṭiyōṭu tōṉṟi, viciṟi vaṭivil allatu tūvuvaṭivil muraṭāyiruppatu ōli.
aivaki malar nili:
tōṉṟum nili arumpu; malarat toṭaṅkum nili pōtu;
malarnta nili malar; kīḻē viḻunta nili vī; vāṭic civanta nili cemmal.
arumpu, moṭṭu, muki, mokkuḷ eṉpaṉa, parumaṉ paṟṟiya vevvēṟu arumpu vakikaḷik kuṟippaṉa.
mūvakik kāyppunili :
iḷaṅkāy piñcu ; mutirntatu kāy; paḻuttatu paḻam allatu kaṉi.
āṅkilam mikunta vaḷarcciyuṟṟu, ēṟattāḻa mūṉṟilakkam coṟkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉum iṉṉum atil kāyik kuṟikkac col amiyavilli; paḻukkāta paḻam eṉṟu vaṭṭa vaḻiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.
cila vakip piñcukaṭkuc ciṟappup peyarumuḷa. teṉṉi, paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ piñcu kurumpi; mām piñcu vaṭu; palāppiñcu mūcu; vāḻip piñcu kaccal;
iṅṅaṉamē nilittiṇiyiṉ (tāvarattiṉ) ellāvuṟuppu kaḷiyum, nuṇpākupāṭu ceytu, vevvēṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭit tamiḻp potumakkaḷ.
iṭaṅkar (alligator), karām (gavial), mutali (crocodile) eṉa mūvakiyāka mutalikaḷ vakukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kutiri mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷukku muṉpē arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu kumari nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. atu ayalnāṭṭu vilaṅkēṉum, atip palvēṟu vakippaṭutti vevvēṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
puravi, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṉṉi, kutiri, pari, kantukam eṉṉum eṇvakik kutirikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappilakkaṇamum, parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa (naripari. 87-94).
pāṇṭiyaṉ kutiri kaṉavaṭṭam; cōḻaṉ kutiri kōram; cēraṉ kutiri pāṭalam; kuṟunila maṉṉar kutiri kantukam.
uṉṉi, kaṇṇukam, kalimā, kiḷḷi, kuntam, kūntal, kokku, koyyuḷi, kōṭi, kōṇam, turaṅkam, tūci, pāymā, mā, maṇṭilam, vayamā mutaliyaṉa kutiriyiṉ potup peyarkaḷ. maṭṭam eṉpatu ciṟuvakik kutiri. taṭṭu eṉpatu nāṭṭuk kutiri. maṭṭattiṉum ciṟuvaki iḷamaṭṭam. tēci eṉpatu peruṅ kutiri.
kutiri eṉpatu oru ciṟappu vakiyiṉ peyarākak kūṟappaṭṭirup piṉum, ulaka vaḻakkiṟ potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkuvatu aḵtoṉṟē. kutippatu kutiri. kutittal tāṇṭutal. kūṟṟaṅ kutittaluṅ kikūṭum eṉṉum kuṟaḷaṭiyi nōkkuka. (kuṟaḷ. 269) .
uṇmi, vāymi, meymmi eṉṉum muccollum, uḷḷam, vāy, mey (uṭampu) eṉṉum mukkaraṇattiyum muṟiyē taḻuviṉa vākac collappaṭuvatuṇṭu. ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟu poruṭkaraṇiyamum uṇṭu.
collutaliṉ vakikaḷik kuṟikka. ēṟattāḻa nāṟpatu coṟkaḷ tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. avi yāvaṉa :-
col                          ciṟappup poruḷ
1. 	acittal 	- 	aciyaḻuttattuṭaṉ collutal. aciyaḻuttam (accent)
2. 	aṟital 	-	aṭittu (vaṉmiyāy maṟuttu)c collutal.
3. 	icittal 	- 	ōci vēṟupāṭṭuṭaṉ collutal.
4. 	iyamputal 	- 	icikkaruvi yiyakkic collutal.
5. 	urittal 	- 	aruñcoṟku allatu ceyyuṭkup poruḷ 					collutal.
6. 	uḷaṟutal 	- 	oṉṟirukka oṉṟic collutal.
7. 	eṉṉutal	 - 	eṉṟu collutal.
8. 	ōtutal 	- 	kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.
9. 	kattutal	 - 	kuraleḻuppic collutal.
10.	karital 	- 	aḻittuc collutal.
11.	kaḻaṟutal 	-	kaṭintu collutal.
12. 	kiḷattal 	- 	iṉṉateṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuc collutal.
13.	kiḷattutal 	- 	kuṭumpa varalāṟu collutal.
14.	kuyilutal, 
	kuyiṟṟu 	- 	kuyilpōl iṉkuraliṟ collutal.
15. 	kuḻaṟutal 	- 	nāt taḷarntu collutal.
16. 	kūṟutal 	- 	kūṟupaṭuttic collutal.
17. 	cāṟṟutal 	- 	palaraṟiyac collutal.
18. 	cepputal 	- 	viṉāviṟku viṭi collutal.
19. 	navilutal 	- 	nāviṉāl olittup payilutal.
20. 	nutalutal 	- 	oṉṟic collit toṭaṅkutal.
21. 	nuvalutal 	-	nūliṉ nuṇporuḷ collutal.
22. 	noṭittal 	- 	kati collutal.
23. 	pakartal 	-	paṇṭaṅkaḷip pakuttu vili collutal.
24. 	paṟital 	- 	maṟi (irakaciyam) veḷippaṭuttic collutal.
25. 	paṉṉutal 	- 	niṟutti niṟuttic collutal.
26. 	paṉuvutal 	- 	ceyyuḷiṟ pukaḻntu collutal.
27. 	pukalutal 	-	virumpic collutal.
28. 	pulamputal 	- 	taṉakkuttāṉē collutal.
29. 	pēcutal 	- 	oru moḻiyiṟ collutal.
30. 	poḻital 	- 	iṭiviṭātu collutal.
31. 	māṟutal 	- 	uriyāṭṭil māṟic collutal.
32. 	miḻaṟṟutal 	- 	maḻalipōl iṉimiyāyc collutal.
33. 	moḻital 	- 	coṟkaḷit teḷivākap palukkic collutal.
34. 	valattal 	- 	kēṭpār maṉattip piṇikkac collutal.
35. 	viṭutal 	- 	meḷḷa veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
36. 	vitattal 	- 	ciṟappāy eṭuttuc collutal.
37. 	viḷḷutal 	- 	veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
38. 	viḷattutal 	- 	(vivarittuc) collutal.
39. 	viḷamputal 	- 	ōr aṟivippic collutal.
oruvaṉiṭattil oṉṟik kēṭkum pōtu, tāḻntavaṉ, ī eṉṟum, ottavaṉ, tā eṉṟum, uyarntavaṉ, koṭu eṉṟum, collavēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻmarapu.
oṉṟupaṭuttal
yāṉikkum tēṉ vaṇṭiṟkum mukattiṉ muṉ ōr uṟiñci (proboscis ) uḷḷatu. inta oppumiyik kaṇṭa mutaṟkālat tamiḻar, iraṇṭiṟkum tumpi eṉappotup peyar iṭṭaṉar. tūmpu - tumpu - tumpi = tūmpu (kuḻāy) pōṉṟa uṟuppiyuṭiyatu.
iṉappaṭuttal
oru poruḷi, uṭampiṉ allatu uṟuppiṉ vaṭivil, otta vēṟu poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭa paṇṭit tamiḻar, muṉṉataṉ peyarkku aṭi koṭuttup piṉṉavaṟṟiṟkiṭṭu, avaṟṟiyellām ōriṉap paṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.ṭu: vēmpu, kaṟivēmpu, nāyvēmpu, nilavēmpu, nīrvēmpu.
vēmpip piṟavaṟṟōṭu oppunōkki nalla vēmpu eṉṟaṉar.
iṭukuṟiyiṉmi
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē 
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (640). āṉāl, pala coṟkaṭṭup pārttamaṭṭil poruḷ tōṉṟātu. avaṟṟi āḻntu ārāya vēṇṭum. itaṉiyē,
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (877) kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
palā, paṉi, poṉ, maram mutaliyavaṟṟi moḻi nūlaṟivum collārāycciyumillā ilakkaṇa vuriyāciriyar, iṭukuṟi yeṉṟu kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.
palā parutta paḻattiyuṭiyatu. pal - paru - parumi.
o. nō: cil - ciṉ - ciṟu. ciṉmi = ciṟumi.
ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻam tūṅki yāṅku  	            (kuṟun. 18).
paṉi karukkumaṭṭiyuṭiyatu. pal - paṉ = aṟuvāṭpal, paṉṉaṟuvāḷ, paṉvittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka. paṉ - paṉi = kūriya paṟpōṉṟa karukkuḷḷatu.
poṉ poṟpu allatu polivuḷḷatu. pol-poli-polivu.  pol - poṟpu = aḻaku. pol - polam - polaṉ. pol-poṉ. poṟṟa = aḻakiya (cīvaka. 270). pollā = aḻakillāta (auviyār) poṟṟatu = polivuṟṟatu (cīvaka. 649).
maram uṇarcciyaṟṟatu. kāl uṇarcciyaṟṟāl, marattup pōy
viṭṭatu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. uṇarcciyaṟṟavaṉi marampōliruk kiṟāṉ eṉpar. matamatappu = uṇarcciyiṉmi, timir. matam - maram. matattal = marattal.
o. nō: viti - viri.
iṭukuṟiyillāmalē oru perun tāymoḻiyi ākkiyatu, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻi pulattic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
iyaṟkic collākkam
tamiḻil ellāc coṟkaḷum iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil amintavi. vaṭamoḻiyil āeṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭāl etirpporuḷ 
viṉiccoṟkaḷi amittuk koḷvar.
e.ṭu: kaccati = celkiṟāṉ. ākaccati = varukiṟāṉ. tattē = 
koṭukkiṟāṉ, ātattē = eṭukkiṟāṉ. ittakiya ceyaṟkiyamippu 
tamiḻil illi.
tūymi
kumarik kaṇṭattil tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅka
villi. veḷināṭṭiliruntu poruḷkaḷ variṉ, avaṟṟiṟku uṭaṉuṭaṉ tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa.
karumpu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu atikamāṉiṉ muṉṉōrāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu.
 amararp pēṇiyum āvuti yaruttiyum
	aruppeṟaṉ marapiṟ karumpivaṭ ṭantum
	nīraka virukki yāḻi cūṭṭiya
	toṉṉili marapiṉniṉ muṉṉōr pōla'' 
eṉṟu auviyār pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka. (puṟam. 99) cīṉa nāṭṭi vāṉavar nāṭeṉpatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. karuppu niṟamāṉatu karumpu.
miḷakāy amerikkāviṉiṉṟu vantatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. miḷaku + kāy = miḷakāy. miḷakupōṟ kāramuḷḷa kāy miḷakāy. iv
vaḻakkip piṉpaṟṟiyē, uruḷikkiḻaṅku, pukiyili, nilak kaṭali, vāṉkōḻi mutaliya peyarkaḷum piṟkālattiṟ ēṟpaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
ilakkaṇa amippu
irutiṇi: poruḷkaḷiyellām, pakuttaṟivuḷḷatu, pakuttaṟivillatu eṉap pakuttatu tamiḻp potumakkaḷē. kāḷiyum āvum aḵṟiṇiyil āṇpālum peṇpālumāyiṉum, kāḷi vantāṉ, āvu vantāḷ eṉṟu yārum collum vaḻakkamilli. kāḷi vantatu, āvu vantatu eṉṟu iraṇṭiyum oṉṟaṉpāliṟ kūṟuvatē marapu.
murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi vantāḷ eṉṟu āṇpāl peṇpāl (pakuttaṟivuḷḷa) makkaḷukkē vakukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulakiṟ pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷum iṟivaṉumē uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum, piṟa vellām uyiruḷḷaviyāyiṉum illaviyāyiṉum tāḻnta vakuppē
yeṉṟum, ēṟkeṉavē potu makkaḷ vakutta iru vakuppiṟkum, ilakkaṇiyar muṟiyē uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟu peyar maṭṭum iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu, ēṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa moḻiyamitikaḷuḷ nallavaṟṟi maṭṭum koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkup peyariṭṭuk kāṭṭum muṟiyēyaṉṟi, putitāka ēṟpaṭṭa pulavar paṭippaṉṟu. pakuttaṟivi aḷaviyākak koṇṭu poruḷkaḷip pakutta iḵtoṉṟē, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ akakkaraṇa vaḷarcciyik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
ōriyaloḻuṅku
yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ; nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ; nīṉ, nīm, nīṅkaḷ; tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ eṉṉum mūviṭap pakarap peyarkaḷum;
avaṉ		ivaṉ		uvaṉ		evaṉ
avaḷ		ivaḷ		uvaḷ		evaḷ
avar		ivar		uvar		evar
atu		itu		utu		etu
avi		ivi		uvi		evi
eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyar viṉāp peyarkaḷum; oṉṟumutal toḷḷā
yirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatu varippaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, koṇṭuḷḷa ōriyalamippi vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇamuṭiyātu.
 poruṭpāl
āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl īṟupaṟṟiya pālamippiṉṟi, poruḷkaḷiṉ āṇmi peṇmiyum orumi paṉmiyum paṟṟiya pālamippē tamiḻiluḷḷatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil, maṉiviyik kuṟikkum tāra, pāryā, kaḷattira eṉṉum muccoṟkaḷuḷ, mutalatu āṇpāl; iṭiyatu peṇpāl; kaṭiyatu alippāl. pottakattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, kiranta āṇpāl; cruti' peṇpāl; pustaka' alippāl. itiṉiṉṟu ammoḻiyiṉ oḻuṅki aṟintu koḷka.
pālicivu
tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarkaḷil, eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyum perum
pālum tiṇipāl eṇ iṭam ottēyirukkum. tiṇipāl eṇ iṭamayakkam iruppiṉum teḷivāyt teriyum.
vaṭamoḻiyil, tat kaccati' eṉpatu avaṉ pōkiṟāṉ, avaḷ pōkiṟāḷ, atu pōkiṟatu, eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōṉṟa 
coṟṟoṭaramippu tamiḻil illi.
pulamakkaḷ amippu
urinaṭi, ceyyuḷ eṉa moḻinaṭi iruvakip paṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ ceyyuḷ pulamakkaḷ amippākum. atu pāṭṭum pāvum eṉa iru vakiyām. urinaṭi paṇpaṭṭu icip pāṭṭākavum, icip pāṭṭu paṇpaṭṭuc ceyyuḷākavum, tiruntum. ākavē, ceyyuḷē moḻiyiṉ ucca niliyām.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar, ceyyuṭ kaliyiṉ koṭu muṭiyēṟi, aṟuvaki veṇpākkaḷiyum, nālvaki akavaṟpākkaḷiyum, 
nālum aintum āṟumāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiyuṭiya nālvakik kalip
pākkaḷiyum, iruvaki vañcippākkaḷiyum, yāttiruntaṉar. 
yāttal = kaṭṭutal.
veṇpāvum kalippāvum pōṉṟa ceyyuḷ vakikaḷi, vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇṭalaritu.
e. ṭu : 			veṇpā			
	ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
	ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
	miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
	taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ'' 
kalippā
(taravu)
	aritāya aṟaṉeyti aruḷiyōrk kaḷittalum
	peritāya pakiveṉṟu pēṇārit teṟutalum
	purivamar kātaliṟ puṇarcciyum tarumeṉap
	piriveṇṇip poruḷvayiṟ ceṉṟanama kātalar
	varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay valippalyāṉ kēeḷiṉi;
(tāḻici)
1 
	aṭitāṅkum aḷaviṉṟi aḻalaṉṉa vemmiyāṟ
	kaṭiyavē kaṉaṅkuḻāay kāṭeṉṟār akkāṭṭuḷ
	tuṭiyaṭik kayantali kalakkiya ciṉṉīrip
	piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅ kaḷiṟeṉavum urittaṉarē;
2
	iṉpattiṉ ikantorīi ilitīnta ulaviyāl
	tuṉpuṟūum takiyavē kāṭeṉṟār akkāṭṭuḷ
	aṉpukoṇ maṭappeṭi aciiya varuttatti
	meṉciṟaka rālāṟṟum puṟaveṉavum urittaṉarē;
3
	kaṉmici vēyvāṭak kaṉikatir teṟutalāl
	tuṉṉarūum takiyavē kāṭeṉṟār akkāṭṭuḷ
	iṉṉiḻal iṉmiyāṉ varuntiya maṭappiṇikkut
	taṉṉiḻalik koṭuttaḷikkum kaliyeṉavum urittaṉarē;
(taṉic col) 
	eṉa vāṅku,
	iṉinala muṭiya kāṉam ceṉṟōr
	puṉinalam vāṭṭunar allar maṉivayiṟ
	palliyum pāṅkot ticittaṉa
	nalleḻil uṇkaṇum āṭumāliṭaṉē. (kalit. 11)
itu, kāṭṭu vaḻiyāyt tolivāṉa iṭattiṟkup poruḷ tēṭac ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉār, kāṭṭiluḷḷa āṇyāṉi peṇ yāṉikkum, āṇ puṟā peṇ puṟāviṟkum, āṇ māṉ peṇ māṉiṟkum kāṭṭum aṉpi ēṟkeṉavē taṉakkuc colliyirut talāl, avaṟṟi nēriṟ kaṇṭapiṉ nīṇṭanāḷ vēṟṟiṭattil taṅkiyirātu virintu varuvāreṉṟum, ataṟkēṟṟa naṟkuṟikaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, maṉivi taṉ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.
itu nāluṟuppaminta nērici yottāḻicik kali. aiyuṟup
paminta ampōtaraṅka vottāḻicik kaliyum, āṟuṟup paminta vaṇṇaka vottāḻicik kaliyum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka. kātaliyum kaṭavuḷiyum vaṇṇittup pāṭuvataṟku avaṟṟilum ciṟanta ceyyuḷ vaki etirkālattilum irukka muṭiyātu. ampōtaraṅkam, vaṇṇakam eṉṉum iraṇṭum teṉ coṟkaḷē.
ampōtaraṅkam
ammutal - poruntutal, oṭṭutal.
am = (oṭṭum) nīr. amtāḻ caṭiyār'' (veṅkikkō. 35)
am - ām = nīr. āmiḻi aṇimali (kalit. 48)
am - ampu = nīr, kaṭal ampēḻum  (tiruppu. 32)
o.nō: kum - kumpu, cem - cempu. vem - vempu.
taraṅku = 1. īṭṭi pōṉṟa kuttuk karuvi.
2. kariyik kuttum ali.
taraṅkāṭun taṭanīr'' 				     (tēvā. 463, 1)
taraṅku - taraṅkam = ali. nīrttaraṅka neṭuṅkaṅki'' 
(periyapu. taṭuttāṭ. 165).
taraṅkampāṭi - ali yiriyum oru kaṭaṟkariyūr.
ō eṉpatu oru cāriyi.
am eṉpatu ap eṉṟu tirintum, ampu eṉpatu tiriyātum, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum.
vaṇṇam
vaḷ - vaḷi. vaḷittal = eḻututal, varital.
uruvap palpū orukoṭi vaḷii  		    (neṭunal. 113).
vaḷ - vari. varital = eḻututal, ōviyam varital.
varittal = eḻututal, cittirameḻututal.
vari = eḻuttu.
vari + aṇam - varaṇam = eḻuttu, eḻutum niṟam, niṟam paṟṟiya kulam.
varaṇam - varṇa (va).
vari eṉṉum colliṉ poruḷ, ataṟku mūlamāṉa vaḷ (vaṇ) eṉṉum aṭiyālum uṇarttap peṟum.
vaḷ - vaṇ - vaṇṇam = eḻuttu, niṟam, vaki, ōcivaki, uruṭṭu vaṇṇam, ciṟappōcip pāṭṭu.
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam - varṇaka (va).
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇa (pirākirutam).
vaṇṇikaṉ - varṇika (va) = eḻuttāḷaṉ.
vaṇṇam eṉpatu varaṇam eṉpataṉ tiripaṉṟu; vaḷ eṉṉum aṭic colliṉ tiripēyākum.
o.nō: tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṇṇi = tiraṇṭa mēṭu.
tiḷ - tiru - tiraḷ - tiraḷi - tiraṇi. tiṇṇi, tiraṇi eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē vēriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ. āyiṉ, tiṇṇi eṉpatu tiraṇi eṉpataṉ tiripaṉṟu; tiṇ eṉṉum aṭippaṭic colliṉiṉṟu tirintatu. iṅṅaṉamē vaṇṇamum varaṇamum eṉka. muṟkālattil toḻiṟ peyarāyirunta pala coṟkaḷ, piṟkālattil tuṇiviṉi cērntum cērātum viṉi mutaṉilikaḷāka vaḻaṅku
kiṉṟaṉa. e.ṭu: kaḷavu cey, naki. nilam pala paṭiyāy oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟu amivatupōl, coṟkaḷum amikiṉṟaṉa. tiḷ, vaḷ eṉṉum īraṭikaḷum collākkattil ottiruttalip piṉvaruñ coṟkaḷāṟ kāṇka.
tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṇṇam - tiṇṇakam.
	     - tiṇ - tiṇṭu - tiṇṭi. tiṇ - tiṇar.
	     - tiṭṭu - tiṭṭam, tiṭṭi.
vaḷ - vaṇ - vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam.
	     vaṇ - vaṇṭu - vaṇṭi. vaṇ - vaṇar.
	     - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam, vaṭṭi.
varaṇam - varaṇi. varaṇittal = vaṇṇattāl varitalpōṟ collāṟ pukaḻtal. allatu virittuk kūṟutal.
varaṇi - varaṇaṉi. varuṇam, varuṇi, varuṇaṉi, eṉpaṉa vaṭacollip piṉpaṟṟiya vaḻu vaṭivaṅkaḷ. varaṇam eṉpatē pāṇṭi
nāṭṭulaka vaḻakku.
varaṇi - varṇa (va.). varaṇaṉi - varṇaṉā(va.)
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇi = varaṇi. vaṇṇi -vaṇṇaṉi = varaṇaṉi.
aṭippaṭic col tamiḻil maṭṭum amintiruppatum, vaṭamoḻiyil mēṟpaṭic collē amintiruppatum, avaṟṟiṉ muṉmi piṉmiyik kāṭṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, kākkik kākā kūki''  eṉṉum ōreḻuttup pāṭṭum, ceṉṉi mukamāṟuḷatāl eṉṉum kāḷamēkam mummaṭi yiraṭṭuṟalum, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavar toḻuvūr-vēlāyuta mutaliyār pāṭiya patiṉpaṅki (tacapaṅki), patiṟṟup patiṉpaṅki (catapaṅki) eṉṉum collaṇi
kaḷum, aruṇakiriyār pāṭiya tiruppukaḻ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum,paṭṭiṉattār pāṭiya uṭaṟkūṟṟu vaṇṇamum, akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷil varum uḷḷuṟi yuvamiyum, pōṉṟa aruñcuviyiṉpak kūṟukaḷ vēṟemmoḻiyilakkiyattilum kāṇak kiṭiyā.
	muṟañcevi maṟippāypu muraṇceyta puliceṟṟu
	maṟantalik koṇṭa nūṟṟuvar talivaṉik
	kuṟaṅkaṟut tiṭuvāṉpōl kūrnuti maṭuttataṉ
	niṟañcāṭi muraṇtīrnta nīḷmarup peḻilyāṉi
	mallari maṟañcāytta mālpōltaṉ kiḷināppaṇ
	kalluyar naṉañcāral kalantiyalum nāṭakēḷ.  (kali-52)
eṉṉum 52-ām kalittokic ceyyuḷiṉ taravu, yāṉiyiṉ cevi maṟivil piṉṉiruntu vantu pāynta puliyi anta yāṉi ciṉantatu, kātalaṉiṉ kaḷavoḻukkattippaṟṟip paḻitūṟṟiya ayalārik kaṭinta
tākavum; yāṉi puliyik kutti māṟupāṭu tīrntatu kātalaṉ kātaliyi maṇantu koṇṭu ayalāri vāyaṭakkuvatākavum; yāṉi taṉ iṉattiṉ naṭuvē ulāvit tirital, kātalaṉ taṉ uṟaviṉaruṭaṉ kūṭiyiruntu illaṟam naṭattuvatākavum, uḷḷuṟip poruḷpaṭat tōḻi kūṟiya kūṟṟāka amintiruttal kāṇka.
paṇṭit tamiḻar ceyyuṭkaliyiṟ ciṟantiruntataṉāl, ceyyuḷukkum nūṟpāviṟkum poruḷkūṟum uri uṭpaṭa, ellā ilakkiyattiyum ceyyuḷilēyē iyaṟṟiyiruntaṉar.
	pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
	aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
	.....................................................ah¥ã‹ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar'' 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (cey.78) kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. tuppuravu
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ viṭikāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ, ūrukku veḷiyē ceṉṟu kālikkaṭaṉ kaḻittu, āṟṟil allatu kālvāyil allatu kuḷattil kāl kaḻuvuvar. āṟṟiṟkup pōtal, kāl vāykkup pōtal, kuḷattiṟkup pōtal, kollikkup pōtal, veḷikkup pōtal eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakku iti yuṇarttum. ittakiya vaḻakkum oṉṟukkup pōtal. iraṇṭukkuk pōtal eṉṉum iṭakkaraṭakkalum tamiḻar nākarikattik kāṭṭum.
kalvit toḻilāḷar, kāvalar, vaṇikar, mutaliya uṭaluḻip pillā vakuppār kāliyilum; uḻavar, kittoḻilāḷar, kūlikkārar mutaliya uḻippāḷi vakuppār māliyilum nāḷtōṟum kuḷittu vantaṉar. aḻukkit tēyppataṟku āṭavar pīrkkaṅkūṭṭip payaṉpaṭuttuvatuṇṭu. peṇṭir mañcaḷ, cuṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟit tēyttuk kuḷippar.
cuṇṇam eṉpatu palavaki naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷic cērttu iṭikkum poṭi. ati iṭittu vippatu paṇṭik kālattu iḷamakaḷirkkup peru vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. cuṇṇamiṭikkum pōtu pāṭṭup pāṭuvar. icceyal māṇikkavācakar maṉattik kavarntatiṉāl, tiruppoṟcuṇṇam' eṉṉum tiruvācakap pāṭal tilliyil aruḷic ceyyappeṟṟatu.
araṇmaṉiyil vāḻum araca makaḷirum, māḷikikaḷil vāḻum celvap peṇṭirum, mātavi pōlum nāṭakak kaṇikiyarum, kōṭṭam, turukkam, takaram, akil, cantaṉam eṉṉum aivaki viriyum (naṟumaṇap poruḷum); nāval allatu pūvanti, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, āl, aracu, atti, itti, muttakkācu allatu karuṅkāli, māntaḷir eṉṉum pattuvakit tuvarum; ilavaṅkam, paccili, kaccōlam, ēlam, nākaṇam, kōṭṭam, nākām, matāvarici, takkōlam, naṉṉāri, veṇkōṭṭam, kācaṟi (kattūri), vēri, ilāmiccam, kaṇṭil veṇṇey, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, tuttam, vaṇṇak kaccōlam, arēṇukam, māñci, cayilēkam, puḻuku, puṉṉi naṟuntātu, puliyukir, caraḷam, tamālam, vakuḷam, patumukam, nuṇṇēlam, koṭuvēri eṉṉum muppattiruvaki. ōmālikiyum; ūṟavitta naṉṉīriṟ kuḷittu vantaṉar.
āṭavar aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) kāriyum (caṉiyum), peṇṭir cevvāy veḷḷiyum, oḻuṅkāy eṇṇey tēyttut tali muḻukiṉar. kuḷippu nīrāṭṭu eṉṟum, talimuḻukku neyyāṭṭu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. neyyāṭṭil eṇṇeyiyum uṭampaḻukkiyum pōkka, cīyaṟkāy ariyal, arippu ariyal, ucilit tūḷ, pāci (pacci)p payaṟṟumā, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
cīyaṟkāy allatu cīkkāy eṉpatu, aḻukkip pōkkuṅ kāy eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu; cikikkāy eṉpataṉ marūu aṉṟu.
cīttal = 1. tuṭittal.
meṉpūñ cemmaloṭu naṉkalañ cīppa  	    ( maturik. 685).
2.pōkkutal.
iruḷcīkkuñ cuṭarēpōl  		   (kalit. 100: 24).
3. tuppuravākkutal (cūṭā).
	ūrkkum aṇittē poyki poykikkuc
	cēyttum aṉṟē ciṟukāṉ yāṟē
	kūḻik kērmaṉ koṇarkañ cēṟum  
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷ (113), paṇṭit tamiḻ makaḷir    ūrukkaṇittāṉa kuṭinīrniliyiṟ kuḷikkāmal caṟṟu appāluḷḷa     kāṭṭāṟṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu kuḷittatiyum, kūntalaḻukkup pōkkak kaḷimaṇ eṭuttuc ceṉṟatiyum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
iru pālārum kuḷittavuṭaṉum talimuḻukiyavuṭaṉum, veḷutta tuvarttu, muṇṭiṉāl taliyiyum uṭampiyum tuvartti, veḷuttāṭi allatu puttāṭi yuṭuppar. māṟṟāṭi yillāviṭiṉ, muṉpu aṇin
tirunta āṭiyit tuvittuk koḷvar.
peṇṭir kārakiṟ pukiyālum cantaṉak kaṭṭip pukiyālum tam kūntaliṉ īram pularttuvar.
kāliyiṟ kuḷippiṉum kuḷiyāviṭiṉum, pal tulakkāmal oruvarum uṇpatilli.
cōṟuṇpavar, vāyum mukamum kikālum kaḻuviyapiṉ, tuppuravāṉa iṭattil taṭukkil, allatu palakiyil amarntu cappaḷittiruntu (cammaṇaṅkūṭṭi uṭkārntu), kaḻuviya vāḻi iliyil allatu veṇkala vaṭṭilil valakkiyālēyē eṭuttu uṇpar. aracar poṟ
kalattilum celvar veḷḷik kalattilum uṇpatu vaḻakkam. peṟṟōrum peṟṟa ciṟu piḷḷikaḷumā yiruntāloḻiya, orē kalattil allatu iliyiṟ palar uṇpatilli. uṇṭeḻuntapiṉ, uṇṭa iṭam ili
yakaṟṟit taṇṇīr allatu cāṇa nīr teḷittut tuppuravākkappeṟum. iliyil viṭṭa miccilip peṟṟōrum maṉiviyarum irappōrum tavirap piṟar uṇṇār. cila uṇavu vakiyāl ēṟpaṭum vāy nāṟṟattip pōkkutaṟku, naṟumaṇac carakki vāyiliṭutal, veṟṟili tiṉṉutal, mārpiṟ cantaṉam pūcutal mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāḷvar. ivaṟṟic cerimāṉattiṉ poruṭṭeṉṟu colvatu muṇṭu.
 viṭintavuṭaṉ peṇṭir muṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ cāṇanteḷittu vīṭu vācalip perukkik kōlamiṭuvar. cevvāyum veḷḷiyum tappātu vīṭu muḻutum āviṉ cāṇattāl meḻukuvar. āṇṭiṟkoru muṟiyum tirumaṇam nikaḻum pōtum, vīṭu muḻutum oṭṭaṭi pōkki veḷḷiyaṭittuc cemmaṇ kōlamiṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṇṭiṟkoru muṟiyeṉṟatu poṅkaṟ
paṇṭiki. poṅkaṟku muntiṉa nāḷ, iṉṟupōl aṉṟum vīṭṭiluḷḷa aḻukkuk kantalkaḷiyum utavāp poruḷkaḷiyum koḷutti viṭuvar. atōṭu taṅkaḷip piṭitta pīḻi pōyviṭṭatu eṉṉuṅ karuttil, atip pōki eṉṟaṉar. pōkutal pōtal. pōkiyatu pōki. ac colli vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) peyarākak koṇṭu, avaṉi nōkkic ceyyum viḻā eṉpatu poruntātu. ti piṟantāl vaḻi piṟakkum  eṉṉum paḻamoḻi, pōki eṉṉuñ coṟporuḷi valiyuṟuttum.
vāṭaki vīṭukaḷiṟ putuk kuṭi pukumpōtum, oṭṭaṭi pōkki veḷḷiyaṭikkap peṟum.
veḷic ceṉṟu vanta vīṭṭārum veḷiyārum,  vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukumuṉ  pātattik kaḻuviviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu oḻukka neṟi.
veppamillāta nāḷilum vēḷiyilum muḷḷillāta iṭattilum kūṭa, vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷic celliṉ, aṭiyil maṇṇum mācum paṭātapaṭi ceruppaṇintē celvar uyarntōr. ceruppu vācaṟku veḷiyē allatu vācalaṇṭitāṉ viṭappeṟum. ek karaṇiyam paṟṟiyum uḷḷē koṇṭu cellap paṭuvatilli.
piḷḷi peṟṟa vīṭṭiṟku ēḻu nāḷum, mātaviṭāy vantavaḷukkup paṉṉiru nāḷum, iḻavu vīṭṭiṟkup patiṉintu nāḷum tīṭṭām.
tīṭṭuḷḷa peṇṭir 
kalantoṭā makaḷir''  			            (puṟam. 299) 
eṉappaṭuvar.
3.ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉ mutal uṇavi nākarikamāyc camittuṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.  ēṉiya nāṭṭārellām, tam nāṭṭil viḷinta  uṇa vup poruḷkaḷip pacciyākavum cuṭṭum veṟumiyāy avittum uṇṭu
vanta kālattil uṇavic cōṟum kaṟiyum eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, nellariciyic cōṟākac camittum, kaṟi allatu kuḻampu vakikaḷi , cuviyūṭṭuvaṉavum uṭampi valuppaṭuttuvaṉavum nōy varātu taṭuppaṉavumāṉa palavaki maruntuc carakkukaḷi ucili (macāli) yākac cērttu ākkiyum, uyarvāka uṇṭu vanta perumi tamiḻaṉatākum.
kāy vakikaḷik kāmpu kaḷintu cīva vēṇṭupavaṟṟic cīvik kaḻuviyum; pittamuḷḷavaṟṟi avittiṟuttum; mañcaḷ, miḷaku allatu  miḷakāyvaṟṟal, cīrakam, kottumalli, tēṅkāy mutaliyavaṟṟi mi
pōl arittuk kalantum; iñci, veṅkāyam veḷḷippūṇṭu, peruṅkāyam, karāmpū(karuvāppū), karuvāp paṭṭi, ēlam, cōmpu mutaliyavaṟṟi vēṇṭumaḷavu cērttum; ventapiṉ puḷi allatu elumiccañcāṟu viṭavēṇṭiyavaṟṟiṟku viṭṭum; kaṭuku, kaṟivēppili mutaliyaṉa tūvi āviṉ neyyil tāḷittum, mutaṉ mutaṟ kuḻampu kāyccappeṟṟatu tamiḻakattilēyē.  tāḷippu ilakkiya vaḻakkil kuy eṉappaṭum.  kuyyuṭi yaṭicil  (puṟam.127).
ulakil ariciyuḷḷa tavacaṅkaḷiṟ palavakiyuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ nellaricic cōṟupōl vēṟoṉṟum cuviyāyk kuḻampoṭu porutu
vatilli.  tamiḻakattilēyē, kāṭik kaṇṇiyarici, kutiri vāliyarici, cāmiyarici, tiṉiyarici, varakarici eṉa aivaki yarici yuḷa.  avi nellaricikkīṭāka.  kampum cōḷamum kañcuyum kūḻumē  camittaṟkutavum. kēḻvarakāl kaḷiyum kūḻumtāṉ ākka muṭiyum.  kōtumi, vāṟkōtumi pōṉṟa piṟa nāṭṭut tavacaṅkaḷ (tāṉiyaṅkaḷ), appamum (roṭṭiyum) ciṟṟuṇṭiyumtāṉ aṭavum cuṭavum ēṟkum.
nellariciyuḷḷum palavakiyuḷa.  avaṟṟuḷ taliciṟantatu campā.  atuvum aṟupatu vakippaṭṭatām.  avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantaṉa cīrakac campāvum ciṟumaṇic campāvum.
paccarici, puḻuṅkalarici eṉṉum iru vakiyuḷ, tamiḻar virumpiyatu piṉṉatē.  atu cūṭṭit taṇippataṉāl veppanāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟatākum; kuḻampiṉ cuviyiyum mikuttuk kāṭṭum.  ataṟkāvē, ataṟkuriya taṉi muyaṟciyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum valuk kuṟiviṟkuk kaṟi vakikaḷālum neyyālum īṭu ceyyap peṟum.
	iruṅkā ḻulakki yirumpumukan tēytta
	 avippumāṇ arici amaliveṇ cōṟu  (ciṟupāṇ. 193-4)
eṉpatu, tūya veḷyiy arici tīṭṭappaṭṭatit terivikkum.
potuvāka, kālic ciṟṟuṇṭiyum naṇpakal mālip pēruṇṭiyum āka mūvuṇṭikaḷē paṇṭit tamiḻar uṇṭu vantaṉar.
kāliyuṇavum naṇpakaluṇavum, uḻavarkkum kūliyāḷarkkum paḻañcōṟum paḻaṅkaṟiyum ūṟukāyumākum.  putuk kaṟiyāyiṉ neruppil vāṭṭiyatum tuviyalumāyirukkum.  irāvuṇavē avarukkup putuc camiyalākum.
	meṉpulattu vayaluḻavar
	vaṉpulattup pakaṭuviṭṭuk
	kuṟumuyaliṉ kuḻic cūṭṭoṭu
	neṭuvāḷip palluviyaṟ
	paḻañcōṟṟup pukavarunti
eṉpatu puṟam (395)
uḻavar maṅkala viṉināḷum viḻā nāḷum viruntu nāḷum pōṉṟa ciṟappu nāḷilaṉṟi mūvēḷiyum nellaricic cōṟuṇ patilli.  varaku, cāmi, kampu, cōḷam, kēḻvaraku mutaliya avvakkālattu viḷinta tavacattiyē avvakkālattup pakaluṇavāy uṇpar.
illattilum niḻalilumiruntu vēli ceyvōrellām, perum
pālum, kāliyiṟ paḻiyatum naṇpakal māliyiṟ cuṭucōṟum, uṇpar.  aracarum celvarum ellā nāḷum, piṟarellām ciṟappunāḷum kāliyiṟ palakāramum poṅkalum pōṉṟa cuṭu ciṟṟuṇṭi vakikaḷi uṇpar.
iruvēḷip pēruṇṭikaḷuḷ, tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantatu naṇpakalatē.  kuḻampuc cōṟum, miḷaku nīrccōṟum, mōr allatu tayirc cōṟum eṉa, mukkaṭaviyuḷḷatu pēruṇṭi.  ati aṟucuvi yuṇṭi yeṉpatu vaḻakku.  mukkaṉikaḷuḷ ētēṉumoṉṟu iṉippum, pākaṟkāy allatu cuṇṭi
vaṟṟal kacappum, kāravaṭi uṟippum, puḷikkaṟiyum tayirum ūṟukāyum puḷippum, mātuḷaṅkāy allatu kaccal  tuvarppum, uppēṟi uvarppum, ākum.  ittakiya uṇṭi, tamiḻariṉ cuvi mutircciyiyum taliciṟanta nākarikattiyum maruttuva aṟiviyum uṭalnala vuṇarcciyiyum oruṅkē kāṭṭum.  aṟucuviyuḷḷum tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantavi iṉippum puḷippum ākum. itu veppa nāṭṭiyalpu.
	muḷitayir picinta kāntaḷ melviral
	kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaḻāa tuṭīik
	kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ kuyppuki kamaḻat
	tāṉtuḻan taṭṭa tīmpuḷip pākar
	iṉiteṉak kaṇavaṉ uṇṭaliṉ
	nuṇṇitiṉ makiḻntaṉ ṟoṇṇutaṉ mukaṉē.

tamiḻar viruntuḷ taliciṟantatu tirumaṇa viruntu. atiṟ patiṉeṇ
vakik kaṟiyum, kaṉṉalum (pāyacam) paṭikkappeṟum.  pati ṉeṇ vakik kaṟikaḷ: aviyal(uviyal), kaṭiyal, kummāyam, kūṭṭu (ventāṇam), tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, poriyal, vaṟuval, puḷikkaṟi, paccaṭi (āṇam), appaḷam, tuviyal, ūṟukāy, vaṟṟal, uḻuntu vaṭi, kāravaṭi, tēṅkuḻal, mukkaṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpaṉa.
kummāyam kuḻiya venta paruppu, allatu payaṟu.  ventāṇam (venta āṇam) eṉpatu iṉṟu veñcaṇam eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu.  iti vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kaṭaṉkoṇṭu vyañcaṉa eṉṟu tirittum, vya+añcaṉa eṉṟu pirittum, uttikkup poruntāta poruḷ kūṟiyum, vaṭa collākak kāṭṭuvar.
tiṇmi nilipaṟṟi nīr, cāṟu, tīyal, iḷaṅkuḻampu, kuḻampu, kūṭṭu eṉak kuḻampu palavakippaṭum,  cōṟum, carukkarip poṅkal (akkāraṭali, akkāra vaṭicil), veṇpoṅkal, miḷakup poṅkal, neyppoṅkal, tēṅkāyc cōṟu, pāṟpoṅkal, kaṭumpuc cōṟu ūṉcōṟu (pulavu), ūṉṟuviyaṭicil eṉap palavakippaṭum.
ūṉ cōṟu allatu pulavu paṇṭik kālattil tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟip piṉpu vaḻakkaṟṟatu; mukammatiyar vantapiṉ avariṭamiruntu putitāyk kaṟṟuk koṇṭataṉṟu.
	maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum miviṭi vīḻppavum
	aṭṭāṉ ṟāṉāk koḻuntuvi yūṉcōṟum
	peṭṭāṅ kīyum peruvaḷam paḻuṉi

	pularāp paccili yiṭiyiṭupu toṭutta

	cemmaṟ ṟammaniṉ vemmuṉi yirukki
eṉṟum (33), varum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷik kāṇka.
	amiḻtaṉa marapiṉ ūṉṟuvi yaṭicil
eṉpatu (puṟam.360:17), pulaviṉ ciṟappu vakiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟicci vakikaḷuḷ uṭumpiṟicciyit tali ciṟantatākat tamiḻar koṇṭami, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāṟkāṭi, arikkōḻi, kālāṭu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl teriya varukiṉṟatu.
	uṭumpiḻu taṟutta oṭuṅkāḻp paṭalic
	cīṟiṉ muṉṟil kūṟucey tiṭumār
	koḷḷi vitta koḻuniṇa nāṟṟam

iṅṅaṉam iruvaki vaḻakkilum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varum ittūya tamiḻc colli, vaṭamoḻiyil vruṣa eṉṟu vari peyarttu. appeyarppiyē teṉ colliṉ mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.

neyyiṟ poritta kaṟiyit tamiḻarellārum virumpi yuṇṭaṉar. veḷḷarip piñcik kuṟukkēyaṟuttatāl tōṉṟum viti pōṉṟa veḷiṟiya naṟu neyyiyē avar payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
	aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy vāḷpōḻn tiṭṭa
	kāḻpōl nalviḷar naṟuney
eṉṟu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēvi kūṟutal (puṟam.246) kāṇka.
poritta kaṟiyōṭu uṇpataṟkup pulavaricic cōṟupōl nīṇṭa parukkic cōṟṟiyē virumpiṉar. nīṇṭa parukki kokkiṉ viral pōliruntalāl kokkukir nimiral eṉappaṭṭatu.
	maṇṭiya kaṇṭa māṉvaṟik karuṉi
	kokkukir nimiral okkal āra 		(puṟam. 398: 24-5)
tamiḻakattil, mutalil, tuṟaviyarē pulāluṇavi nīkki vantaṉar. tiruvaḷḷuvar,`aruḷuṭimi `pulāṉ maṟuttal kollāmi eṉṉum mūvatikāraṅkaḷiyum, tuṟavaṟaviyalil vittiruppatum kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār paṭiyalum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa. camaṇam tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vanta piṉṉarē, illaṟattārum, ciṟappākac civaneṟiyar, pulāluṇavi nīkkat talippaṭṭaṉar.  ataṉāl, marakkaṟiyuṇavu civam eṉappaṭṭatu.
marakkaṟi yuṇaviṉarāyiṉum ūṉuṇaviṉarāyiṉum, pāl, tayir, mōr, veṇṇey, ney eṉṉum āṉinti mikap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.
naṟuneyk kaṭali vicippa 			       (puṟam.120:14)
kaṟi porikkak kāycciya neyyuliyōcikku matayāṉiyiṉ perumūcciyum, kotikkiṉṟa neyyil tuḷḷi vēkum kaṟiyōcikku āḻnta niṟi kuḷattil perumaḻit tuḷikaḷ viḻum ociyiyum paṇṭippulavar uvami kūṟiyiruppatu mika makiḻntu pārāṭṭattakkatu.

 neṭunīra niṟikayattup 
	paṭumārit tuḷipōla
	neytuḷḷiya vaṟi 			        (puṟam.386:13)
yāṉikkuk koṭukkum kavaḷamum neyyil mitittut tiraṭṭap paṭṭatiṉāl, neymmiti eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.  camiyal vakik kellām āviṉ neyyiyē akkālattil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.  nalleṇṇey eṉṉum eḷ ney talimuḻukkiṟku maṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
aracarum maṟavarum pāṇarum kūttarum porunarum pulavar cilarum uḻavarum uḻippāḷiyarum, kuṭippu vakikaḷuḷ kaḷḷiyē ciṟantākak koṇṭaṉar. kaḷ eṉṉuñ col, veṟinīr, patanīr (teḷivu allatu paṉañcāṟu) maṭṭu (carpattu), tēṉ eṉṉum nālvakikiyuṅ kuṟikkum. inta nāṉkiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar paṇṭit tamiḻar.  ivaṟṟuḷ veṟinīr vakiyē vaḷḷuvarāl kaḷḷuṇṇāmi eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kaṇṭikkappaṭṭatu.
veṟinīr iyaṟkik kaḷḷum ceyaṟkik kaḷḷum eṉa iruvakittu. paṉaṅkaḷḷum teṉṉaṅkaḷḷum īccaṅ kaḷḷum iyaṟki; aricic cōṟṟu nīrip puḷikka vittu arikkappaṭṭa ariyalum, mēṉāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu puṭṭikaḷil vanta matukkaḷḷum ceyaṟki.
yavaṉar, naṉkalanta tanta taṇkamaḻ tēṟal	          (puṟam.56:18)
veppa nāṭākiya tamiḻakattil, kāliyiliruntu katiravaṉ aṭiyum vari kāṭṭil kaṭuveyilil varuntiyuḻikkum uḻavarkku, uḻippu nōvip pōkkavum nīr vēṭkiyit taṇikkavum, veṟippum kuḷircciyum puḷippumāṉa piṭippu vēṇṭiyatā yiruntatu. ataṉāl, avarkkeṉṟu taṉik kaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu.
kaḷamark karitta viḷiyal veṅkaḷ 
eṉpatu (puṟam.212:2) itiyuṇarttum.
kaḷḷiṉ puḷippiyum veḷippiyum mikukka, atik kaṇṇāṭik kalaṅkaḷilum mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykaḷilum iṭṭu mūṭip paṉṉāḷ maṇṇiṟ putittu vippatum vaḻakkam. attakiya kaḷḷiṉ kaṭumikkut tēṭkoṭṭum pāmpuk kaṭiyum uvami kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nilamputip paḻuṉiya maṭṭiṉ tēṟal        	             (puṟam.120)

tēṟal=teḷintakaḷ. tōppi=neṟkaḷ.
iṉkaṭuṅkaḷ (puṟam.80) eṉpataṉālum, pūkkamaḻ tēṟal (porunar.157) eṉpataṉālum, ceyaṟkikkaḷ iṉimiyum maṇamum ūṭṭap peṟṟiruntami aṟiyalām.
nāṭpaṭāta iyaṟkik kaḷḷi uṭaluḻip pāḷikaḷ veṟi yuṇṭākāvāṟu aḷavāka uṇpatāṟ kēṭilli yeṉpatum naṉṟeṉpatum, ārāycciyāḷar karuttu.  vēṉiṟ kālattiṟkuk kuḷircciyāṉa paṉaṅkaḷḷiyum mārik kālattiṟku veppamāṉa teṉṉaṅ kaḷḷiyum, iyaṟki vakuttiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

tamiḻakattiṟ paḻaṅkālattilēyē necavu tōṉṟi viṭṭateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  vayavar yōvāṉ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) tam`mokoñcō-tārōvum cintu nākarikamum (Mohenjo-Daro and the Indus Civilization) eṉṉum nūlil ki.mu.3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ mēṉāṭu

parutti toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭil viḷintu varukiṉṟatu.  mūtāṭṭiyarum kim peṇṭirum paṇṭik kālattil mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilkaḷuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu nūlnūṟṟal. ataṉāl avar paruttip peṇṭir eṉappaṭṭār.

nūṟkum cakkaram kiyiṉāṟ cuṟṟappaṭuvataṉāl,atu kiyiṟāṭṭu allatu kiyiṟāṭṭi, allatu kiyiṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṟu collap paṭum. iṟāṭṭu eṉṉum collē, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutaliyil illi. irāṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻūuc col maṭṭum, tēṉkūṭu eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷatu.
iṟāṭṭu, iṟāṭṭi, iṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṉum mūvaṭivum, intiyil rahaṭ allatu rahaṇṭṭā eṉṉum vaṭivil nūṟkuṅ karuviyik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, inticcollē tamiḻc coṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉi akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
