ikkālak kāñcipurap paṭṭuccēli pōṉṟē, akkālap paṭṭāṭiyum muṉṟāṉi yōrattil aḻakiya maṇi pōṉṟa nūṉ muṭiccukkaḷi yuṭiyatāyiruntatu.
koṭṭik kariya paṭṭuṭi nalki 		     (porunar. 155)
akkālattil, uyarkuṭi āṭavar vēṭṭiyum mēlāṭiyum, uyar kuṭip peṇṭir cēliyum kaccum (iravukkiyum), aṇintiruntaṉar.  tāḻkuṭiyāṭavar vēṭṭiyoṭu tali muṇṭum, tāḻkuṭip peṇṭir kacciṉṟicēli muṉṟāṉiyālaminta māryāppum,koṇṭi runtaṉar.  ūrāṭcit talivarum periyōrum mēlāṭi yoṭu vaṭṭat talip
pākiyum aṇintiruntaṉar.
aracarkkum araciyal atikārikaṭkum araṇmaṉi aluvalark                    kumē, caṭṭiyaṇiyum urimiyiruntatu. caṭṭi uṭampiṟkup pi pōṉṟiruppatāl, meyppi eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
	nuṇviṉik kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara
	meyppi pukku viyaṅkunaṭic celaviṟ
	kikkōṟ kollaṉi 			(cilap. 16: 106-8)
	īcaṉuṅ kaṟṟuccolvōr piṉvara vikaliṟ kūṭit
	tēcuṭic caṭṭi cātti
eṉṉum perum paṟṟap puliyūr nampi kūṟṟu piṟkālattēṉum, caṭṭi eṉṉum col muṟkālattē kañcuka mākkaḷ eṉṉum cilappatikārac coṟku ``caṭṭiyiṭṭa piratāṉikaḷ eṉṟu aruñcollurikārar 
varintiruttal kāṇka. pēṭik kōlattārum aracakulap peṇṭirum, vaṭṭuṭi eṉṉum oruvakic ciṟappuṭiyi, ariyiṉiṉṟu muḻan
tāḷaḷavāka akkālattaṇintiruntaṉar. (maṇi: 3:122; peruṅ 3.4:122).
vēṭar,ariyiṉiṉṟu aṭit toṭiyaḷavāka, kāḻam eṉṉum oru vaki yuṭiyi aṇintiruntami kāḻamiṭṭa kuṟaṅkiṉaṉ eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl (kamparā.kaṅkip. 32) teriyavarukiṉṟatu. atu callaṭam pōṟ kuṟukiya arikkāṟ caṭṭi pōlum!
veyiṟ kālattilum vaṟaṇṭa iṭattilum cellum veḷip pōkkarum vaḻip pōkkarum,illaṟattārāyiṉ toṭutōl eṉṉum ceruppum, tuṟaviyarāyiṉ mitiyaṭi eṉṉum pātak kuṟaṭum, aṇintu ceṉṟaṉar.
caṭṭiyiṭṭum mēṉāṭṭārpōṟ pātakkūṭu (boots) aṇintum kāṭṭuvaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟatākap perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaṇikar, veḷināṭṭār pōl terikiṉṟatu.
	aṭiputi yaraṇam eytip paṭampukku
	...............................cl«ão¤ jṭakkiXṭātmpalar	          (bparumpāṇ69-76)
aoòijuz« = aṭiyi maṟikkiṉṟa pātak kāppu. paṭam = caṭṭi.
5.aṇi
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, ciṟanta vakiyil virumpiya aḷavu aṇikalam ceyvataṟkēṟṟa poṉ, veḷḷi, muttu, maṇi mutaliya karuvikaḷum tērnta poṟkollarum, aṇipavarum, iruntaṉar.
ikkālattum, ulakil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭikkumiṭam āttirēliyāvum teṉṉāppirikkāvumākum.  ivi muḻukippōṉa paḻantamiḻakamāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattic cērntavi.  ātalāl kumarināṭṭilum tamiḻarkku ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭittirukka vēṇṭum.
kumarik kaṇṭam muḻukiya piṉpum, koṅku nāṭṭil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭittatu. atu koṅkup poṉ eṉak kalveṭṭiṟ ciṟappāyk kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟu taṅkaccuraṅkamuḷḷa kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) koṅku nāṭṭic cōntatē. annāṭṭil tōṉṟum kāviriyāṟu, paṇṭināḷiṟ poṉ maṇalik koḻitta taṉāl poṉṉi yeṉap peṟṟatu.  koṅku nāṭu paṇṭit tamiḻa kattiṉ oru pakutiyākum.
maturiyilirunta veḷḷiyampalamum tilliyilirunta poṉṉampalamum, paṇṭittamiḻakattiṉ veḷḷi poṉ vaḷattik kāṭṭum.
mutaṟkālattil ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭittataṉāl poṉ ṉālēyē kācaṭikkappaṭṭatu.  ataṉāl, kāciṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi, tātu (ulōka) vaki kaṭkum poṉ eṉpatu potup peyarāyiṟṟu. veṇpoṉ = veḷḷi, cempoṉ = cempu, karumpoṉ = irumpu.  poṉ = tātu (metal). itaṉāl, tamiḻakattil iruppuk kālattiṟku muṉ poṟkālam oṉṟu iruntatō eṉa aiyam eḻukiṉṟatu.
kaṭik kaḻakak kālap pulavar cilarkku aḷikkappaṭṭa poṟ paricilē, akkālattup poṉvaḷattik kāṭṭap pōtumāṉatu.
kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār, kaḷaṅ kāyk kaṇṇinār muṭic cēral mītu patiṟṟuppattiṉ 4-ām pattip pāṭi, nāṟpatu nūṟāyiram poṉṉum avaṉ āṇṭattiṟpākamum peṟṟār.  poṉ eṉpatu poṟkācu.
kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār, āṭu  kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ mītu patiṟ. 6-ām pattip pāṭi, aṇikalaṉukkeṉṟu oṉpatu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum peṟṟār.
kapilar, celvakkaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ mītu patiṟ.7-ām pattip pāṭi, nūṟāyiram poṟkācum avaṉ  mali mītēṟikkaṇṭu koṭutta nāṭum peṟṟār.
aricil kiḻār, takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēralirum poṟi mītu patiṟ. 8-ām pattip pāṭi, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram poṟkācum āṭciyurimiyi maṟuttu amiccurimiyum peṟṟār.
peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār, kuṭakkō iḷañ cēralirumpoṟi mītu patiṟ. 9-ām pattip pāṭi, muppattīrāyiram poṟkācum palvakip paricilum peṟṟār.
kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār, karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ mītu paṭṭiṉap pāliyip pāṭi, patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ peṟṟār.
patiṟṟuppattil mutaṟpattum 10-ām pattum iṉṟiṉ miyāl,avaṟṟip pāṭiya pulavar peṟṟa paricum teriyavilli.
mātavipōl āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiya nāṭakak kaṇikiyiṉ māli vili āyirat teṇ kaḻañcu poṉṉāka matikkappaṭṭi runtatu.
	kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar kiviṉi muṟṟiya
	nuṇviṉik kollar			   (cilap.16:105-6)
	mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiram kaṭṭiya
	pattik kēvaṇap pacumpoṟ kuṭiccūl
	cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉi yellām
	poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ nōkkik
	kōpperun tēvik kallati yiccilampu
	yāppuṟa villi yeṉa			   (cilap. 117-122)
	palavuṟu kaṇṇuḷ cilakōl avirtoṭi	         (kalit. 85:7)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷ akkālattu aṇikala viṉittiṟattik kāṭṭum.
	malipayanta maṇiyum kaṭaṟupayanta poṉṉum
	kaṭalpayanta katirmuttamum		   (puṟam 377; 16-7)
eṉpatu tamiḻakattiṉ poṉmaṇi muttu vaḷattik kāṭṭum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭic cērnta kīḻik kaṭal muttu, toṉṟu toṭṭu ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟatu.
	viḷintu mutirnta veṇmuttiṉ
	ilaṅkuvaḷi iruñcērik
	caṭkoṇṭik kuṭippākkattu
	naṟkoṟkiyōr nacipporuna	             (maturik.135-138)
	maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum
	koṟkiyamperuntuṟi muttiṉ aṉṉa	       (akam. 27:8-9)
	ivartiri tanta īrṅkatir muttam
	kavarnaṭip puravik kālvaṭut tapukkum
	naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟki muṉṟuṟi	  (akam.130: 9-11)
	viṉinavil yāṉi viṟaṟpōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ
	pukaḻmali ciṟappiṟ koṟki muṉṟuṟi
	avirkatir muttamoṭu valampuri corintu	   (akam. 201: 3-5)
	ilaṅkirum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki
	valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉtimiṟ paratavar
	olitalip paṇilam ārppak kalleṉap
	kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa iḻitarum	(akam,350:10-13)

	kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaluḻta laṉṟiyum īṇṭunīr
	muttuppaṭu parappiṟ koṟki muṉṟuṟi. 	          (naṟ.23:5-6)
teṉkaṭal muttum kuṇakaṭal  tukirum 	     (paṭṭiṉap. 189).
aṇikalattiṟkuriya poṉ, iyaṟkiyākat tūyatum, puṭamiṭap paṭṭut tūyatākkap peṟṟatum, eṉa iruvaki.  ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ōṭṭaṟṟa poṉ eṉappaṭum.
ōṭṭaṟṟa cempoṉ pōlē 		         (īṭu, 1,10,9)
māṟṟaṟiyāta ceḻum pacum poṉ
eṉṟu irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ kūṟiyatum ituvē.
puṭamiṭappaṭṭatu māṟṟuyarnta poṉ eṉappaṭum.  pattari māṟṟut taṅkam ciṟanta teṉak kūṟuvatu ulakavaḻakku. tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ itip pattumāṟṟuttaṅkam eṉpar.
pattumāṟṟut taṅka mākkiyē paṇikoṇṭa 	            (ciṉmayā.7)
aparañci eṉṉum āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟup poṉṉum uḷḷatāka nūlkaḷ kūṟum,
āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟiṉ aparañci 	        (maccapu.tārakācurava 26).
ayōkkiyar aḻaku aparañcic cimiḻil nañcu.  
eṉṟoru piṟkālap paḻamoḻiyum uḷatu.
poṉṉiṉ māṟṟurikkuṅ kal kaṭṭaḷikkal eṉṟum, urikkum kampi uriyāṇi eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
mātavi, viralaṇi (kāṉ mōtiram), pariyakam (kāṟcavaṭi), nūpuram (cilampu), pāṭakam, cataṅki, ariyakam (pāta cālam), kuṟaṅkuceṟi (kavāṉceṟi), viriciki, kaṇṭiki (māṇikkavaḷi), tōḷvaḷi, cūṭakam, kivaḷi (poṉvaḷi), pariyakam (pācittāmaṇi, kiccari),   vālvaḷi (caṅka vaḷi, veḷḷivaḷi), pavaḻavaḷi, vāḷip pakuvāy mōtiram, maṇimōtiram, marakattāḷ ceṟi (marakatak kaṭiceṟi), caṅkili (maṟat toṭari), nuṇñāṇ, āram,kayiṟkaṭi yoḻukiya kōvi (piṉṟāli), intira nīlakkaṭippiṇi (nīlakkutampi), teyvavutti (tiruttēvi), valampuri, toyyakam (talippāḷi, pūrappāḷi), pullakam (teṉpalliyum vaṭapalli-yum) eṉa 27 vaki aṇikaḷ aṇintiruntatākac cilappatikārak kaṭalāṭu kāti kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkāta vēṟucila aṇikalam akkālattiruntaṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cūṭi allatu cūṭāmaṇi.  peṇṭir arip paṭṭiki maṭṭum mēkali, kāñci, kalāpam, parumam, viriciki, eṉa  aivakippaṭṭiruntatu.
	eṇkōvi kāñci eḻukōvi mēkali
	paṇkoḷ kalāpam patiṉāṟu-kaṇkoḷ
	parumam patiṉeṭṭu muppat tiraṇṭu
	viriciki yeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu.
eṉpatu paḻañ ceyyuḷ.
ikkāla aṇikaḷē ēṟattāḻa aimpatu vakippaṭṭiruppatāl, mutaṉmiyāṉa aṇiyiṉiyē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum.  ikkālattu arippaṭṭiki oṭṭiyāṇamākum.  iṅṅaṉamē cila aṇikaḷ vaṭivum peyarum māṟiyuḷa.
ikkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻa nākarika makaḷir pōl, akkālattil ellāt tamiḻap peṇṭirum kātu vaḷarttiruntaṉar. kātu vaḷarkkumpōtu  aṇivatu kutampiyum, vaḷarttapiṉ aṇivatu kuḻiyum kaṭippiṇiyumākum. kutampi iṉṟu kuṇukku eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
koṭuṅkuḻi tuṟantu vaṭintuvīḻ kātiṉaḷ	          (cilap. 4: 50)
	vaḷḷit tāḷpōl vaṭikā tivikāṇ
	uḷḷūṉvāṭiya uṇaṅkal pōṉṟaṉa   	     (maṇi.20: 53-4)
eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
akkālattil āṭavarum aṇikalaṉ aṇivatu peruvaḻakku.  vaṇikar kātiṟ kuṇṭalamum tōḷil (puyattil) kaṭakamum, mārpil maṇik kaṇṭikiyum, aṇintiruntaṉar. piṟa vakuppār kātiṟ kaṭukkaṉum, kiyiṟ kāppum aṇintiruntaṉar. kaṭukkaṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṟkālak kamalam (kammal) eṉṉum makaḷir kātaṇi eḻuntatu. maṟavar tōḷiṟ kaṭakamum kāliṟ kaḻalum aṇintiruntaṉar.  kaḻal piṟkālattil   veṇṭiyam eṉappaṭṭatu.  kimōtiram iru pālārkkum potuvām.
aracar, pāṇaṉukkup poṟṟāmariyum. avaṉ maṉiviyākiya pāṭiṉikku allatu viṟalikkup poṉṉaṇikalamum, paricilāka aḷittu vantaṉar.  viṟali viṟal (cattuvam) paṭa āṭupavaḷ.  viṟal uḷḷak kuṟippāl uṭampil tōṉṟum vēṟupāṭu. avi kaṇṇīr vārtal, meymmayir cilirttal mutaliyaṉa.
13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭi nāṭṭip pārkka vanta mārkkōpōlō eṉṉum veṉīcunakara vaṇikar, cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ taṉkaḻuttil vilimatikka voṇṇāta paṉmaṇi māliyum, mārpil viliyēṟap peṟṟa irumaṇiyāramum, tōḷil mūṉṟu paṉmuttak
kaṭikiyum, kāliṟ kaḻalum, kāl viralkaḷil mōtiramum aṇintirunta
tākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
	mitīr pacumpoṉ māṇṭa maṇiyaḻuttic
	ceyta teṉiṉum cerupputtaṉ kāṟkēyām
	eytiya celvatta rāyiṉum kīḻkaḷic
	ceytoḻilāṟ kāṇap paṭum.
eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷāl, paṇṭi mūvēntariṉ ceruppu ettakiya
tāyirukkum eṉpati uyttuṇaralām.
mayilukkut tōkipōlap peṇṭirkkuk kūntal aḻaku taruvatāl, akkālattu peṇṭir tam kūntali naṉṟāyp pēṇi, kuḻal, koṇṭi, paṉicci, curuḷ, muṭi eṉṉum aivakiyil aḻaku peṟa muṭittu vantaṉar. avi aimpāl eṉappaṭum. avaṟṟik kuḻal, aḷakam, koṇṭi, paṉicci, tuñci eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum; curuḷ, kural, aḷakam, tuñcu, kuḻal,koṇṭi eṉac cāmuṇṭi tēvanāyakarum; ciṟitu vēṟupaṭak kūṟuvar. kūntalukku maṇam iyaṟki eṉṉum aḷaviṟku naṟumaṇam ūṭṭuvatu orutaṉik kaliyāyiruntatu. nāṟiṅ kūntal eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap.10: 43).
peṇṭir tam kaṇṇiṟku mi yūṭṭuvatu peru vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.
	eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōṟ koṇkaṉ
	paḻikāṇēṉ kaṇṭa viṭattu.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷ (1285) itit terivikkum.  miyūṭṭuvataṉ peruvaḻak
kiṉāl, uṇkaṇ eṉṉun toṭarē miyuṇṭa kaṇṇik kuṟikkum.
irunōk kivaḷuṇkaṇ ṇuḷḷatu 		        (kuṟaḷ 1091).
miyūṭṭal kaṇṇiṟku aḻaku maṭṭumaṉṟik kuḷircciyum tarumeṉpatu, aṟiñar karuttu.
ikkālattu mēṉāṭṭup peṇṭir pōl, akkālat tamiḻp peṇṭirum tam utaṭukaṭkuc ceñcāyam ūṭṭi vantaṉar. atu avaraḻakic ciṟappittataṉāl peṇṭiraḻaki varṇikkum iṭamellām ilavitaḻc cevvāy (cilap 14:136) eṉṟum. kovvic cevvāy (tiruvāca.6:2) eṉṟum,tuppuṟaḻ toṇṭic cevvāy (cīvaka.550) eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum, kūṟuvatu pulavar vaḻakkamāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, uḷḷaṅkāṟkum cenniṟam peṟac cempañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭi vantami.
alattaka mūṭṭiya aṭi (maṇi. 6:110) eṉpatāl aṟiyalām. alattakam cempañcuk kuḻampu.
makaḷir mārpilum tōḷilum kātalar cantaṉak kuḻampāl variyum ōviyaṅkaḷum, irupālārumneṟṟiyilum kiyilum kuttuvikkum paccik kōlamum, ōviya vuṇarcciyi yaṉṟi nākarikattik kāṭṭā.
6. uṟiyuḷ
uṟiyuḷ eṉpatu kuṭiyirukkum vīṭu.
kuṟiñcinila vāṇarāṉa kuṟavar kuṉṟavar, iṟavuḷar mutaliya vakuppārum, pālinila vāṇarāṉa maṟavar, eyiṉar, vēṭar mutaliya vakuppārum, ilivēynta kuṭicikaḷilum kuṟṟilkaḷilum; mullinila vāṇarāṉa iṭiyar kūri vēynta ciṟṟilkaḷilum marutanilac ciṟṟūr vāṇarāṉa uḻavar. maṇcuvarkkūri vīṭukaḷilum; vatintārēṉum; marutanilap pērur vāṇar ēntāṉa (vacatiyāṉa) paccic ceṅkaṟ cuvark kūri vīṭukaḷilum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟcuvark kāri vīṭukaḷilum vāḻntuvantaṉar. cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭumaṇ eṉṟum cuṭumaṭ palaki eṉṟum collap paṭṭatu. paccic ceṅkal maṭpalaki yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cuṭumaṇ oṅkiyaneṭunakar varippil	 (perumpāṇ.405)
cuṭumaṭ palakipala koṇarvittu	     (periyapu.ēyarkōṉ. 49)
ciṟu ceṅkal iṭṭiki eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṇcorīi iṭṭiki tīṟṟu pavar			(paḻa.108)
iṭṭitu = ciṟitu (kuṟaḷ. 478). iṭṭimi = ciṟumi (tivā,). iṭṭiya = ciṟiya (aiṅkuṟu. 215).
iṭṭiki eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil iṣṭikā eṉṟu tirintu taṉciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu,ceṅkal eṉṟu maṭṭum poruḷpaṭum.
ciṟiyatum periyatum uyarntatum tāḻntatum allātu naṭu nikarttāṉa uṟiyuḷ, kuṭi, maṉi, il, vīṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. illam, vaḷamaṉi, māḷiki, nakar eṉpaṉa, peruñ celvar vāḻum ciṟanta uṟiyuḷik kuṟikkum. aracar vāḻum māḷiki araṇ peṟṟirukkumātalāl, araṇmaṉiyeṉap peṟum; aracaṉ maṉi eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kōyil eṉṟum collappeṟum.
kuṭi, nakar, māḷiki eṉṉum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.  āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar uṇmiyi oppukkoḷḷum nērmiyarallar.
mēṉiliyuḷḷa vīṭu māṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. atu ulaka vaḻakkil māṭi vīṭu eṉavum metti vīṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. irunili mutal eḻunili vari akkālattu māṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉakil āvivimmum eḻunilimāṭañ cērntum  (cīvaka.2840)
ovvoru māṭamum allatu māḷikiyum, cuṟṟuc cuvar, muka
maṇṭapam, talivācal, iṭikaḻi, (naṭi) muṉkaṭṭu, uḷ muṟṟam, piṉkaṭṭu, kūṭam, aṭukkaḷi (camiyalaṟi), puḻikkaṭi (kollip
puṟam), maṉikkiṇaṟu, kuḷippaṟi, calakkappuri (kakkūcu), cālakam allatu aṅkaṇam eṉṉum pakutikaḷiyuṭiya tāyiruntatu. mēṉiliyil nilāmuṟṟamiruntatu.
	vakipeṟa eḻuntu vāṉam mūḻkic
	cilkāṟ ṟicikkum palpuḻi nalil
eṉṉum maturik kāñci aṭikaṭku (357-8),
maṇṭapam kūṭam tāykkaṭṭu aṭukkaḷi eṉṟāṟ pōlum peyarkaḷip peṟutaliṉ, vakipeṟa veḻunteṉṟār eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappuri varintiruttal kāṇka:
vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku - aṅku. o.nō: vaḷi - aḷi. aṅkutal = vaḷital, cāytal, aṅku - aṅkaṇam. o.nō: cāykaṭi - cākkaṭi.
kāṟṟu varumvaḻi, kālatar, cāḷaram, palakaṇi eṉṉum peyarkaḷip peṟṟiruntatu.
kāri vīṭukaḷ ucciyil oṭu vēyntatum maccup pāviyum iruvakiyā yiruntaṉa.  ōṭum ilakkiya vaḻakkil cuṭumaṇ eṉap paṭṭatu.
	cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ 
	muṭiyara coṭuṅkum kaṭimaṉi
eṉpataṟku (cilap.14:146-7) `ōṭu vēyātu poṟṟakaṭu vēynta maṉi' eṉṟu aruñcollurikārar ōr uri varintiruppatu kavaṉikkat
takkatu.  muṭi vēntarum virumpum nāṭakak kaṇikiyar maṉikaḷātaliṉ. poṟṟakaṭu vēyntatākavum iruntirukkalām.  kāñci nakariṟ palar kūṭutaṟkuriya potu ampalamum poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatāka maṇimēkali kūṟutal kāṇka.
cāliyuṅ kūṭamum tamaṉiyap potiyilum	        (maṇi. 28: 66)
maḻitōyum uyarmāṭattu
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉappāli (145) yaṭiyum,
	māṭam aḻintakkāl maṟṟum eṭuppatōr
	kūṭam marattiṟkut tuppākum.
eṉṉum paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷum (71) māṭattiṉ perumiyi uṇarttum.
7.ūrtiyum pōkkuvarattum
ūrtal ēṟic cellutal, ūrti ēṟic celluṅ karuvi.
nilavūrtikaḷ aṇikam vaṇṭi, vilaṅku eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
aṇikam, civiki, pallakku, mēṉā, capparam eṉpaṉa.
civiki, iruvar kāvic cellum kūṇṭup pallakku.  pallakku eṇmarukkuk kuṟiyātu tōḷil tāṅkic cellum tiṟanta
taṇṭiyap paṭip pallakku. capparam uruḷiyiṉṟi oṟṟit taṭṭuḷḷa cappi (moṭṭi)t tēr.
civiki eṉpatu cipikkā eṉṟum, pallakku eṉpatu paryaṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. capparam iṉṟu teyvap paṭimiyik koṇṭu cellavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum.
vaṇṭi: cakaṭam, kūṭārappaṇṭi, kollāp paṇṭi, viyam, pāṇṭil, vaṅkam, tēr eṉpaṉa. cakaṭam potuvakiyāṉa māṭṭu vaṇṭi, kūṭārap paṇṭi kūṇṭu vaṇṭi.  kollāp paṇṭi ciṟanta kāḷikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatum perumakkaḷ ēṟic celvatumāṉa nākarikak kūṇṭu vaṇṭi, viyam iru kutiri pūṇṭiḻukkum tēr pōṉṟa vaṇṭi.  pāṇṭil īruruḷiyuḷḷa kutiri vaṇṭi. vaṅkam paḷḷiyōṭa vaṇṭi: atu paḷḷi yōṭam pōṉṟatu. tēr ikkālat tēr pōṟ ciṟiyatu.
vilaṅku: kāḷi, katiri, kōvēṟu kaḻuti, yāṉi, oṭṭakam eṉpaṉa.
nīrūrtikaḷ: puṇi, parical, kaṭṭumaram, ōṭam, ampi, timil, paḵṟi, tōṇi, paṭaku, nīrmāṭam (paḷḷiyōṭam) nāvāy (kappal) eṉpaṉa. nāvāy, vaṅkam, kappal eṉpaṉa ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭaviyāyu mirukkalām.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilum vaṭa nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa pērūr kaṭkum kō nakar kaṭkum, taṭivaḻi eṉṉum peruñcālikaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.  iccālikaḷ kāṭukaḷiṟ kūṭum kavartta iṭaṅkaḷil, avvan nāṭṭu vēntaṉiṉ viṟpaṭikaḷ, vaṇikac cāttukkaṭku vaḻippaṟikkum kaḷvarālum koḷḷikkārarālum poruṭcētamum āṭcētamum nērāvāṟu iravum pakalum kāttu niṉṟaṉa.

eṉṟu kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār toṇṭi nāṭṭip paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka.
nāṭu kippaṟṟaṟkum vāṇikattiṟkum ayalnāṭu pārttaṟkum, aracarum vaṇikarum potu makkaḷum cellak kūṭiya nāvāy eṉṉum peruṅkalaṅkaḷ, kīḻkaṭalilum mēlkaṭalilum aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉa.

eṉṟu maturik kāñci (75-88), vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ cāli (cāvaka)t tīvik kippaṟṟiyati, avaṉvaḻit tōṉṟalāṉa taliyālaṅ

eṉpatu vaṇikam paṟṟiyatu.
	cātuvaṉ eṉpōṉ takavila ṉāki

ulakavāḻkki, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam taṉi vāḻkki eṉa mūvakip
paṭum.
oru kaṟpuṭip peṇṇi maṇantu illattiliruntu aṟañceytu vāḻum vāḻkki illaṟamākum.  ulakap paṟṟi yoḻittu vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvatu tuṟavaṟamākum.  maṇañceyyātu ulakap paṟṟōṭu taṉiyā yiruppatu taṉivāḻkki (celibacy) ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ illaṟattiyē ciṟantatākak koṇṭaṉar tamiḻar.  iṟivaṉ makkaḷi āṇum peṇṇumāy paṭittiruppatē, avarkūṭi vāḻtaṟ poruṭṭē.  illaṟattālum oruvar vīṭupēṟṟi aṭiyamuṭiyum.
	aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkki yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
	pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ			             (kuṟaḷ.46)
	aṟaṉeṉap paṭṭatē yilvāḻkki aḵtum
	piṟaṉpaḻippa tillāyiṉ naṉṟu.		             (kuṟaḷ.49)
eṉṟār tamiḻ aṟanūlāciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar. ivaṟṟiyē,
illaṟa mallatu nallaṟa maṉṟu
eṉac curuṅkac coṉṉār piṟkālattu auviyār. āṭavarum peṇṭirum kūṭātu vāḻvatu aritāyiruppatuṭaṉ, tuṟavaṟattiṟkum illaṟamē iṉṟiyamiyāta tuṇiyākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkūṟiya mūvaki vāḻkkiyuḷ, ovvoṉṟum nallatum tīyatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.  illaṟattil orē maṉiviyuṭaṉ vāḻvatu nallatu; pala maṉiviyaruṭaṉ allatu pala peṇṭirik kātalittu vāḻvatu tīyatu. tuṟavaṟattil uṇmiyāyiruppatu nallatu; uḷḷoṉṟum puṟam oṉṟu
māyiruppatu tīyatu; atu kūṭāvoḻukkam eṉappaṭum.  taṉi vāḻkkiyil oru peṇṇiyum kātaliyātiruppatu nallatu; maṟivāyk kātalit toḻukuvatu tīyatu.  ivaṟṟuḷ nallavaṟṟiyē tamiḻa mēlōr pōṟṟiṉar.
ulaka vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇiyāyiruppatāl maṉivikku vāḻkkit tuṇivi eṉṟu peyar.
illaṟam
maṇap paruvam vanta piṉpē, palavakiyilum otta ōr iḷiñaṉum iḷiñiyum, tāmākak kūṭiyō tam peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭappaṭṭō, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāki vāḻvatu, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻa marapu.  maṇamāṉamikku aṭiyāḷamāka  maṉiviyiṉ kaḻuttil tāli eṉṉum maṅkalavaṇi iṭam peṟum. 
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum eṅṅaṉam kūṭiyiruppiṉum  avarkku iṉṟiyamiyātatākum kātal eṉpatu iṟakkum variyum oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāmi.  kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa kātal, kāmam eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟum. accol iṉṟu peṇṇāci eṉṉum tīyaporuḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
kāmatti oru talikkāmam, irutalikkāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉa mūvakiyāy vakuttaṉar muṉṉōr.  ōr āṭavaṉum peṇṭumākiya iruvaruḷ, oruvar maṭṭum kātalippatu oru talik kāmam; iruvarum kātalippatu iru talik kāmam; yārēṉum oruvar neṟitavaṟik kātalippatu poruntāk kāmam.  ivaṟṟuḷ iru talik kāmamē ciṟantatākavum neṟip paṭṭatākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu.  peṟṟōrum maṟṟōru miṉṟit tāmākak kūṭuvatellām, perumpālum irutalik kāmamākavēyirukkum.
kāmatti akapporuḷ eṉṟum, orutalik kāmattik kikkiḷi eṉṟum, irutalik kāmatti aṉpiṉ aintiṇi eṉṟum; poruntāk kāmattip peruntiṇi eṉṟum ilakkaṇam kūṟum.  aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyē neṟippaṭṭatākak koḷḷap paṭṭatiṉāl atiyē akam eṉṟu ciṟappittum, ēṉi yiraṇṭiyum akappuṟam eṉṟu iḻittum, kūṟuvar ilakkaṇiyar.
kikkiḷi, kuṟippu eṉṟum maṇam eṉṟum iruvakiyāyc collappaṭum.  oruvaṉ kāmavuṇarcciyillāta oru ciṟumiyiṭam allatu kātalillāta oru peṇṇiṭam, cila kātaṟ kuṟippuc coṟkaḷi maṭṭum tāṉē colliyiṉpuṟutal kikkiḷik kuṟippām.  peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭap peṟṟa kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyaruḷ, yārēṉum oruvar kātalillāmalē icintiruppiṉ atu kikkiḷi maṇamām.  kikkiḷi orupakkak kātal. ki pakkam. kiḷi kātal.
oru peṇṇi maṇavuṟavu muṟi tappiyō, valintō, ēmāṟṟiyō, tūkka niliyilō, nōy niliyilō, iṟanta piṉpō pūppu niṉṟa piṉpō kūṭuvatum, taṉpāloṭum vilaṅkoṭum kūṭuvatum, peruntiṇiyām. itu iṅṅaṉam palavakiyāyp perukiyiruppatāṟ peruntiṇi yeṉap
paṭṭatu.  tamiḻukkuc ciṟappāṉa poruḷilakkaṇam āriya varukikku eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē amintu viṭṭataṉāl, āriya voḻukka nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattuḷtāṉ oṉṟē nālvaki peṟṟatiṉāl peruntiṇi yeṉappaṭṭa teṉpatu, kāla
malivum nūṉ malivuṅ kalanta peruvaḻuvām.
peṟṟōr ceytu vikkum maṇam, pēccu maṇamum aruñ ceyal  maṇamum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.  maṇavāḷap piḷḷi vīṭṭār pōyk kēṭka, peṇ vīṭṭār icintu peṇ koṭuppatu pēccu maṇam; peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr kuṟitta ōr aṟaviṉiyō maṟaviṉiyō ceytu peṇṇik koḷvatu aruñceyal maṇam.  paṇṭit tamiḻakattil kollēṟu taḻuvi ataṟkuriya peṇṇi maṇappatu mulli nila vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.  maṇamāṉa aṉṟē maṇamakkaḷ  kūṭuvar.
koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟik kātalar tāmākak kūṭum kūṭṭam, maṟivākat toṭaṅkuvatum veḷippaṭiyāyt toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  maṟivāṉa kūṭṭam kaḷavu eṉṟum, veḷippaṭiyāṉa kūṭṭam kaṟpu eṉṟum, collappeṟum.  kaḷavu perumpālum irumātat
tiṟkuṭpaṭṭēyirukkum.  atu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ kaṟpām. kaṟpellām karaṇam eṉṉum tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅkōṭum pantalaṇi, maṇamuḻā, vāḻttu, varici uṟṟāruṭaṉ uṇṇum uṇṭāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum kūṭiya maṇaviḻāvoṭum toṭaṅkum.  kaḷavuk kālattil kūṭṭam taṭip
paṭiṉum, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr piṟarkkup peṇ koṭukka iciyiṉum, kātalaṉ kātaliyik kūṭṭik koṇṭu vēṟṟūr ceṉṟuviṭum uṭaṉ pōkkum uṇṭu.  avar tirumpi vantapiṉ, kātalaṉ vīṭṭilēṉum kātali vīṭṭilēṉum vatuvi eṉṉum maṇaviḻā nikaḻum.
iṉi, kaḷavukkālattil kūṭṭam taṭippaṭuvatāl, kātali taṉ kātalaṉik kāṇap peṟāmal maṉam varunti mēṉi vēṟupaṭumpōtu, peṟṟōr vēlaṉ eṉṉum mantirakkāraṉi varavaḻittu tam makaḷ nōykkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam ) viṉavuvatum, avaṉ atu murukaṉāl nērntateṉṟu kūṟi, veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṟiyum kaḷḷum vilāpuṭikka vuṇṭu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉṉum kūtti nikaḻtti annōyip pōkkuvatāka naṭippatum uṇṭu. aṉṟu kātali nērākavō taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavō, taṉ peṟṟōriṭam uḷḷatic colli viṭuvāḷ.  atu aṟattōṭu niṟṟal eṉappaṭum. taṉ kātalaṉaṉṟi vēṟu yārkkum taṉṉip pēciṉum, kātali aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ; ataṉpiṉ kātalaṉukku maṇañ ceytu vikkap peṟuvāḷ. maṇamakaṉ allatu avaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaḷukkup paricam koṭuppar. maṇamakaṉ paricam peṟum anākarika māṉaṅkeṭṭa āriya iḻivaḻakku akkālattilli.
tamiḻp peṇṭir kaṟpiṟ ciṟantavarātaliṉ, oruvari maṇantapiṉ allatu kātalitta piṉ veṟōruvariyum kaṉavilum karutuvatilli; vēṟu evariyēṉum maṇakka nēriṉ, uṭaṉē uyiri viṭṭu viṭuvar.
kātalar kūṭum kūṭṭam, uṭampāṟ kūṭuvatum uḷḷattāṟ kūṭuvatum eṉa iruvaki.  ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu meyyuṟu puṇarcci eṉṟum, piṉṉatu uḷḷappuṇarcci eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirkku iraṇṭum oṉṟē.  itaṉālaṉṟō, tilakavatiyammiyār tamakkup pēcap peṟṟirunta kalippakiyār pōrk kaḷattiṟ paṭṭapiṉ iṟakkat tuṇintatum, ataṉ piṉ tam oṟṟikkoru tampiyār tirunāvukkaracariṉ poruṭṭu uyir tāṅkiyatum, iṟuti vari maṇavātiruntatum, eṉka.
(āriyaṉ varumuṉ) karaṇam eṉṉum tāli kaṭṭuc caṭaṅki, ūrt talivaṉ, kuṭi mutiyōṉ, maṅkala mutu peṇṭir, kulap pūcāri mutaliyōr naṭatti vittaṉar.
paṇṭiyaracarum peruñ celvarum perumpālum ciṟṟiṉpa vuṇarcci ciṟantu, pala tēviyariyum kāmakkiḻatti, iṟparatti, kātaṟ

ammāṉ makaḷum akki makaḷumpōl, maṇakkakkūṭiya uṟavumuṟip peṇ urimippeṇ; uṟavu muṟiyiṉṟic celvak kuṭumpattiṉiṉṟu eṭukkum peṇ perumippeṇ; iraṇṭumaṉṟi oruvaṉtāṉē kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kātalittu maṇakkum peṇ uḻuvaṟ peṇ.  pala piṟappākat toṭarntu maṉiviyāy varupavaḷ uḻuvaṟ peṇ eṉpatu, piṟavit toṭar nampikkiyāḷar karuttu. uḻuva laṉpip payiliyatu keḻiiya naṭpu eṉpar iṟiyaṉār (kuṟun. 2). ūḻāl ēṟpaṭṭa āvali uḻuval eṉṟaṉar. itit teyvap puṇarcci yeṉṟum, iyaṟkip puṇarcci yeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum.

eṉpatu (kuṟun.40), kātalaṉ taṉ uḻuvaṟ peṇṇi nōkkik kūṟiyatu.  mūvakip peṇkaḷuḷḷum uḻuvaṟ peṇṇi maṇappatē kulamata nilaccārpu kaṭantatākaliṉ, teyvattāl nērntatāka vitantu kūṟappeṟam.
kātalaṉ kaḷavuk kālattil taṉ kātaliyi nōkki, ulaka muḻuvatiyum peṟṟālum nāṉ uṉṉik kiviṭēṉ eṉṟu (kuṟun.300) uṟuti kūṟiya
taṟku ēṟpa, kaṟpuk kālattil, nī toṭṭatu nañcāyiruntālum eṉakkut tēvar amutamākum. mēlum ūkki iṉpuṟuttuvatu vaḻakkam.
kātal maṉiviyum , taṉ kaṇavaṉit teyvam pōṟ pēṇi, avaṉ iṭṭa cūḷi (āṇiyi) niṟivēṟṟāviṭattu ataṉāl avaṉukkut tīṅku nērātavāṟu teyvatti vēṇṭik koḷvatum, avaṉ cūḷ tappavilli yeṉpatum (kuṟun.87), taṉ talivaṉ kuṟṟattip piṟar eṭutturippiṉ ati maṟuttu avaṉip pukaḻvatum, (kuṟun 3), taṉ kaṇavaṉum tāṉum oruṅkē iṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpuvatum (kuṟun.57), vaḻakkam.
aracarum maṟavarum pōr ceytaṟkum, muṉivarum pulavarum tūtupaṟṟiyum, vaṇikar poruḷīṭṭaṟkum, vēṟṟūrum vēṟṟu nāṭum cella nēriṉ, avar tirumpi varumvari avar maṉiviyar āṟṟiyiruppatum, cuvariṟ kōṭiṭṭu nāḷeṇṇi varuvatum, avar kuṟitta kālattil varāviṭiṉ virintu varumāṟu teyvatti vēṇṭuvatum, iyalpām.

eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum (kuṟaḷ.61), kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. peṇ 
makkaḷāl pala tollikaḷ nērvatāl āṇ piḷḷiyiyē tamiḻar ciṟappāka virumpiṉar. cāṇpiḷḷi āṇpiḷḷi māṇpiḷḷi peṇṇip peṟṟavaṉ pēccuk kēṭpāṉ eṉpaṉa paḻamoḻikaḷ.
kaṇavaṉ tavaṟṟālō maṉiviyiṉ pētimiyālō, cila camiyaṅkaḷil avariṭip piṇakku nērvatuṇṭu.  aṉṟu maṉivi ūṭik kaṇavaṉoṭu pēcātiruppāḷ. ūṭutal caṭivu koḷḷutal. atu kaṇavaṉāl eḷitāyt tīrkkappaṭum. atu  caṟṟuk kaṭumiyāṉāl pulavi eṉappaṭum. atu kuḻantiyik kaṇavaṉ eṭuttuk koṇṭu pōyk koṭuppataṉālum, vīṭṭiṟku viruntiṉar vantiruppatālum, uṟaviṉarum naṇparum taliyiṭuvatālum, tīrkkappaṭum. pulavi muṟṟi viṭṭāl tuṉi eṉappaṭum. ati oruvarālum tīrkka muṭiyātu.  nīṇṭa nāṭceṉṟu atutāṉē taṇiyum. paḻakap paḻakap pālum puḷippatu pōl, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa kāmaviṉpam caṟṟuc cuvi kuṟiyumpōtu ati niṟittaṟkup pulaviyum vēṇṭu meṉṟum, atu uṇaviṟku uppiṭuvatu pōṉṟateṉṟum, ūṭal uppuk kuṟivatum tuṉi uppu mikuvatum pōṉṟavi yeṉṟum, uppu mikiyāṟ cuvi keṭuvatupōl tuṉiyāl iṉpaṅ keṭumātalāl anniliyi aṭiyātavāṟu pulaviyit taṭuttu viṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟuvar.
	uppamin taṟṟāṟ pulavi atu ciṟitu
	mikkaṟṟāl nīḷa viṭal.			         (kuṟaḷ.1302)
peṇṭir ettuṇik kalvi kaṟṟavarāyiruppiṉum, uḻat tiyarum iṭicciyarum maṟattiyarum kuṟattiyarum paṇṭamāṟṟup peṇṭirum  kūliyāṭaṭiyarum vēlikkāriyarum vaṟiyavarumāyirun tāloḻiya, maṇamāṉapiṉ, mūppaṭiyumaṭṭum, kaṇavarōṭum peṟṟōrōṭum aṇṇaṉ akki mārōṭum māmiyārōṭum pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭimārōṭu
maṉṟi, vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyē taṉiyē cellap peṟār.
kaṇavaṉip pēṇutalum camiyal ceytalum piḷḷi vaḷarttalum kaṇavaṉillātapōtu vīṭṭik kāttalumē, peṇṭirkku iyaṟkiyāl allatu iṟivaṉāl vakukkappaṭṭa paṇiyeṉpatu, paṇṭit tamiḻar karuttu. maṇamāṉa peṇ vīṭṭilēyēyiruntu vēli ceyvataṉālēyē, avaḷukku illāḷ, illakkiḻatti, maṉivi, maṉiyāḷ, maṉiyāṭṭi, maṉikkiḻatti, vīṭṭukkāri eṉṉum iṭampaṟṟiya peyarkaḷum, il, maṉi, kuṭi eṉṉum iṭavāku peyarkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭiṟku allatu illattiṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷi yellām kaṇavaṉē īṭṭavēṇṭumeṉpatum, ataṉāl maṉiviyum iḷa makkaḷum iṉpamāy vāḻavēṇṭumeṉpatum, paṇṭiyōr karuttu.
	viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāḷnutal
	maṉiyuṟi makaḷirk kāṭavar uyir. 
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ (135:1-2),  itaṉip pulap paṭuttum.
ikkālattiṟ kālimutal māli vari āṭavar kaṭumiyāy uḻittum, kuṭumpattiṟkup pōtiya aḷavu poruḷ tēṭavō uṇavup poruḷ koḷḷavō muṭiyavilli.  itaṉālēyē, peṇṭir veḷiyēṟi āciriyap paṇiyum araciyalaluvaṟ paṇiyum āṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākiṉṟatu.  ākavē, iṉṟu avar kaṭami iru maṭaṅkāyp perukiyuḷḷatu. innilimi makkaṭ perukkiyum uṇavut taṭṭiyum kāṭṭumēṉum, itaṟku aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam araciyal tavaṟē.
peṇṭirit taṉiyē vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷippōkkāmikku iṉṉoru karaṇiyamumuṇṭu. avar potuvāka āṭavarāl, ciṟappākak kāmukarāl, nukarccip poruḷākak karutappaṭum nilimi iṉṉum māṟavilli. āṭavari nōkka, avar meṉmiyar, valuvaṟṟavar. itaṉāl avarkku melliyal, aciyiyal, taḷariyal, eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ilakkiyattil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tīyōrāl avarkkum avaruṟaviṉarkkum cētamum māṉak kēṭum nērā vaṇṇamē, avar tuṇiyōṭaṉṟi veḷiyē aṉuppap paṭuva tilli. āṭavar nāṉmuḻa vēṭṭiyuṭukkum pōtu, peṇṭir patiṉeṇ muḻac cēli yaṇivatum, ikkaraṇiyam paṟṟiyē.
ākavē, peṇṭiri veḷi viṭātiruppatu, avaruṭiya nalam pēṇalēyaṉṟi avaric ciṟippaṭuttalākātu. kaṭikaṭkum kōyiṟkum tiruviḻāviṟkum uṟaviṉar vīṭṭu maṅkala amaṅkala nikaḻccikaṭkum piṟa iṭaṅkaṭkum, tuṇiyoṭu pōy vara avarkku nirampa vāyppuṇṭu.
pūppaṭinta kaṉṉip peṇkaḷiyum takka tuṇiyiṉṟi veḷiyē viṭuvatilli.

tirukkum pōtu, kaṟpuṭi maṉivu taṉṉi aṇi ceytu koḷvatilli; maṅkalavaṇi tavira maṟṟavaṟṟi yellām kaḻaṟṟi viṭuvāḷ.

eṉṟu (cilap.16:71-73), kaṇṇaki maturiyil taṉ kaṇavaṉi nōkkik kūṟutal kāṇka.
maṟumukam pārāta kaṟpeṉpatu kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyiruvarukkum potuvēṉum, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ pōṉṟa oru cilarē avvaṟattik kaṭippiṭitta āṭavarāvar; peṇṭirō paṟpallāyiravar. kātalilum āṭavar peṇṭirkku īṭākār. uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟutalum uṭaṉuyir viṭutalumē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. maṟumaṇañ ceyyāta kimmi niliyum peṇṭir ciṟappik kāṭṭum.
pulavar, aracar, vaṇikar. veḷḷāḷar ākiya vakuppukkaḷic cērnta peṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ iṟanta piṉ, eṭṭām nāḷ iṟatic caṭaṅkil maṅkala
vaṇiyum piṟavaṇikaḷum nīkkappeṟuvar. atu tāliyaṟuppu eṉappaṭum. ataṉ piṉ veḷḷāṭiyaṇintu vēṟevariyum maṇavāmal tam eñciya kālattik kaḻippar. avar uyarkuṭip piṟantavar eṉappaṭuvar.
vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟi uḻavum kittoḻilum aṅkāṭi viṟpaṉiyum kūli vēliyum teruppaṇṭamāṟṟum ceyyum piṟa vakuppup peṇṭirellām, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ tāliyaṟuppiṉum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvar. atu `aṟuttuk kaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum  iṉi, cila vakuppup peṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum pōtē tīrvi eṉṉum kaṭṭaṇattik koṭuttut tīrttu viṭṭu vēṟoru vaṉi maṇantu koḷvatum uṇṭu. atu ``tīrttuk kaṭṭutal'' eṉappaṭum. atu ettaṉi muṟiyum nikaḻum.
aracar pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟut taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭālum, pakivarāṟ kollappaṭṭālum, avar tēviyarum makaḷirum pakiyara
carkku aṭimiyākātavāṟu tīkkuḷittu iṟappatu muṇṭu.
kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum vari avaṉukku uṇmiyāṉa maṉivi
yāyiruntu, avaṉ iṟantapiṉ vēṟoruvaṉi maṇappatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ maṟumaṇam ceyyātiruppatum, kaṟpiṉ pāṟpaṭu mēṉum, avaṟṟit tamiḻakam kaṟpeṉak koḷḷavilli. tamiḻakak kaṟpu ulakattilēyē taliciṟantatākum. atu paṇpāṭṭup pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum. iṅkuk kūṟiyaviyellām nākarikak kūṟukaḷē.
kuṭumpat talivaṉ iṟantapiṉ, īmakkaṭaṉum iṟutic caṭaṅkum avaṉ putalvarāl, putalvaṉ illāviṭṭāl avaṉ uṭamikku urimi pūṇum uṟaviṉaṉāl, naṭattappeṟum. īmam eṉpatu cuṭali.
piṇattip putippatē tamiḻar vaḻakkam. erippatu āriya vaḻakkamē. āriyarkulap piriviṉiyāl pirāmaṇarkku oppuyarvaṟṟa talimi eṟpaṭṭa piṉ tamiḻarum avar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟalāyiṉar. makkaṭ perukkamum nilattaṭṭum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa ikkālattil, erippatu poruḷāṭci nūṟpaṭi ciṟantatākat tōṉṟum.
peṇ makkaḷ, tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu aṇikalamum veṇkala pittaḷi ceppēṉaṅkaḷum peṟuvataṉālum, piṟkālattiṟ peṟṟōri āṇ makkaḷpōl uṇavaḷittuk kākkum  urimiyiṉmiyālum, peṟṟōr uṭamikku  urimiyuḷḷavarākār.
tuṟavaṟam
paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷiyār pōl ulaka vāḻkkiyiṉ niliyā miyi uṇarntō, tāyumāṉavar pōl kuruviṉāl aṟi
vuṇarttap peṟṟō, civappirakācar pōl iyalpākavē illaṟattil veṟuppukkoṇṭō, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōl iḷamiyilēyē kaṭanta aṟivaṭintō, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pōl uṭaṉ piṟantārkkuk kēṭuvarātu taṭuttaṟ poruṭṭō, kōvala kaṇṇakiyar tantiyarum mātavi maṇimēkaliyarum pōl kaṇṇaṉṉa uṟaviṉar neṭum pirivit tāṅka muṭiyāmalō, tuṟavu pūṇuvatu toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamākum.
kuṭumpac caṇṭiyāṟ caṭivu koṇṭum kuṭumpap poṟuppi nīkkik koḷḷavum, tuṟavu pūṇuvatumuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ciṟappuṭiyataṉṟu; piṉṉatu peruṅkuṟṟamākum.
āṇṭi, paṇṭāram, aṭikaḷ, muṉivaṉ, cittaṉ eṉat tuṟaviyar palavakiyar. āṇṭi irappōṉ; paṇṭāram aṟivu nūlkaḷi nirampakkaṟṟa paṇṭitaṉ; aṭikaḷ uḷḷat tūymiyum āvikkuriya (spiritual) ceytikaḷiṟ paṭṭaṟivum vāyntavar, paṭṭiṉattārum tāyumāṉavarum irāmaliṅkarum pōl. immūvarum nāṭṭiliruppavar. muṉivarum cittarum maliyiliruppavar.
muṉivar allatu muṉivar ulakappaṟṟi muṟṟum veṟuttavar. muṉital veṟuttal, muṉital veṟuttal.
aiyaṉ eṉṉum col viḷakkam
aḷ = ceṟivu. aḷḷal = nerukkam. aḷḷāṭutal = ceṟital. aḷḷiruḷ = ceṟinta iruṭṭu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital.
cērē âu£á,”(bjhš.46) eṉṉum neṟimoḻippaṭi, ceṟivuk
karuttiṉiṉṟu perumik karuttut tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu. moyttal = neruṅkutal. moy = nerukkam,perumi.
ḷakara meyyīṟu yakara meyyīṟākattirital peru vaḻakku.
e-ṭu: eḷ(iḷi) - ey; koḷ - koy; cēḻ (cēḷ) - cēy -cēymi; toḷ - toy,neḷ(naḷ) - ney; paḷ - (pay) - payampu; poḷ - poy; veḷ - vey - veymmi - vemmi = vēṇṭal (tol.
col.334).
	akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
	aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṉum tolkāppiya (56) neṟimoḻippaṭi, ay eṉpatu aiyākum.
o. nō: vaḷ - (vay) - vi = kūrmi.
poḷ - poy - (pay) - pi = uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷatu.
inneṟi muṟikaḷiṉpaṭi, aḷ - (ay)ai eṉṟākum. ai = perumi, periyōṉ, tanti, āciriyaṉ, talivaṉ, aracaṉ, kaṇavaṉ. ipporuḷkaṭkellām aṭippaṭi periyōṉ eṉpatē.
iṉi aiviyap pākum. (tol, 868) eṉpatāl, ai = viyakkat takka perumiyuṭiyōṉ eṉṟumām. aiyaḷ = viyakkattakkavaḷ (aiṅkuṟu. 255).
orē col paṇpup peyarākavum paṇpāku peyarākavu miruppati aṭimi, aṇṇal, cemmal eṉṉum coṟkaḷālum aṟika. aṭimi = aṭimit taṉmi, aṭiyāṉ. aṇṇal = perumi, periyōṉ.
cemmal = 1, talimi.
aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaṟ kāli   (tol. poruḷ, 146).
  2. talivaṉ.
aṟavōṉ makaṉē maṟavōr cemmāl! 	         (puṟam. 366).
ai - aiyaṉ = periyōṉ, tanti, aṇṇaṉ, talivaṉ, aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, muṉivaṉ, aṭikaḷ, iṟiyaṭiyāṉ, teyvam, kaṭavuḷ.
uṟaviṉarallāta mūttōriyum uyarntōriyum aiyā eṉṟu viḷippatē toṉṟu toṭṭa tamiḻ marapu. itu āriyar intiyāviṟku varu muṉṉum āriya iṉamē ulakattil tōṉṟu muṉṉum, paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamumāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattil tōṉṟiya vaḻakkākum. aiyā eṉpatu aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷivēṟṟumi.
kātalum matippum paṟṟi, uṟaviṉarum allārumāṉa, iḷi yōriyum aiyā eṉṟu viḷippatu marapu vaḻuvamitiyām.
e-ṭu: aiyā, ēaiyā - ēyā, ē aiyō - ēyō, ō aiyā - ōyā, vāaiyā - vāyā. ōyā eṉpatu perumpālum ikaḻcciyum ciṉamum paṟṟivarum.
makaṉi, eṉ appaṉ vantāṉ eṉpatu pōl eṉ aiyaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟu mutal vēṟṟumiyilum, aiyaṉ eṉṉum col marapu vaḻuvamitiyāka varum.
tāḻttap paṭṭavar mutal taliyāyār vari, tamiḻarellārum. perumpālum tantiyi aiyā eṉṟē toṉṟu toṭṭu viḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
iraṅkal, nōtal, viyattal mutaliya kuṟippup paṟṟiya iṭic collāka, aiyaṉ eṉṉum tanti muṟip peyarē palvēṟu viḷivaṭivil taṉittum iraṭṭiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu: aiya, aiyā, aiyē, aiyō, aiyavō - aiyakō; aiyiya, aiyiyō.
tantikkup piṉ tamiyaṉ eṉṉum neṟi muṟippaṭi aiyaṉ eṉpatu aṇṇaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum. emiyaṉ numiyaṉ tamiyaṉ eṉṟu, em num tam eṉṉum mūviṭam kuṟitta muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟuvaḻaṅkiya aṇṇaṉ muṟip peyarkaḷuḷ, iṟutiyatu maṭṭum  iṉṟu muṉṉoṭṭup poruḷiḻantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiyaṉ = tam aṇṇaṉ, aṇṇaṉ.
ekkulattārāyiṉum, āciriyari aiyar eṉpatu mēli vaṭārk kāṭṭu māvaṭṭattār vaḻakku.
ākka vaḻippāṟṟal paṟṟi, makkaḷ vakuppāruḷ ciṟantārākak karutap peṟum muṉivari, vitantu aiyar eṉṟal tamiḻ marapu. piṅkalavuric coṟṟokutiyil, muṉivarip paṟṟiyatu aiyar tokuti eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tuṟaviyaruḷ, kāṭṭil vatiyum muṉivarip pōṉṟē, nāṭṭil vatiyum (tiruveṇ kāṭṭaṭikaḷum tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷum irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷum pōṉṟa) aṭikaḷārum ātaṉiyal (ātmīka) vaḷarcciyum ākka vaḻippāṟṟalum aruḷ vaṭivum peṟṟavarātaliṉ, aiyar eṉṟaḻikkap peṟuvar.
	poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
	aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ (1088) kuṟikkap peṟṟa aiyar eṉpār irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōlum aruḷ vaṭivat tamiḻat tuṟaviyarē.
iṉi, iṟivaṉaṭiyārum periyōrātalālum, nāyaṉār eṉṟum tēvar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruttalālum, tuṟaviyar pōl iṟivaṉ paṟṟuṭimiyālum, aiyar eṉṉum peyarukkuriyar.
cārvalit toṭakkaṟukka ēkumiyar eṉṟu, kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār purāṇattil (70) annāyaṉāriyum, aiyarē! ampalavar aruḷālip poḻutaṇintōm eṉṟu, tirunāḷip pōvār nāyaṉār purāṇattil (30) annāyaṉāriyum; aḷavilā makiḻcci yiṉār taminōkki aiyarnīr eṉṟu, tiruñāṉacampanta nāyaṉār purāṇattil (133) tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇa nāyaṉāriyum; uyarvuppaṉmi vaṭivil aiyar eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
makkaḷiṉum uyarnta vakuppār tēvarātaliṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṉum col tēvaṉ eṉṟum kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. tēvaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil atu cāttaṉiyum, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum poruḷil civaṉiyum kuṟikkum. cāttaṉi aiyaṉār eṉpatu marapu.
illaṟattāruḷ oruvaṉukku aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, tanti, tāy, tamiyaṉ eṉṟu aivakip periyōriruppatāl, avar aiṅkuravar eṉṟum; avaruṭ ciṟnta peṟṟōriruvarum iru mutukuravar eṉṟum aḻikkap peṟuvar. kuravaṉ periyōṉ.
tantiyik kuṟikkum āyāṉ, āñāṉ eṉṉum muṟip peyarkaḷum, aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. āyāṉ = tanti, aṇṇaṉ. āyāṉ - āñāṉ = tanti. o.nō: valiyaṉ - valiñaṉ.
aiyaṉ eṉṉum col, mākati eṉṉum pirākirutac citivāṉa pālimoḻiyil ayya eṉṟu tiriyum.
ai eṉṉum tanti muṟip peyariṉiṉṟē āy eṉṉum tāy muṟip peyar tiriyum.
ai - āy = aṉṉi.
em + āy = yāy (em aṉṉi)
um + āy = mōy - moy (um aṉṉi)
nam + āy = (nāy) - ñāy (nam aṉṉi)
tam + āy - tāy (tam aṉṉi, aṉṉi)
aṉṉiyik kuṟitta avvi eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē, āy eṉṉum collum pāṭṭiyik kuṟikkum. itu āyāḷ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
ammi - avvi = aṉṉi, pāṭṭi.
appāy (appaṉip peṟṟa pāṭṭi), ammāy (ammiyip peṟṟa pāṭṭi) eṉṉum muṟippeyarkaḷil, āy eṉpatu aṉṉiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
aṉṉiyik kuṟikkum māyi, mā eṉṉum intic coṟkaḷ, mōy eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripē. mōy - māy - māyi, mā. vaṭanāṭṭup paḻan tiraviṭamākiya tirimoḻiyē pirākirutameṉṟum, cūracēṉip pirākiruta vaḻivanta citimoḻiyē intiyeṉṟum, aṟika.
nāy eṉṉum vaṭivam oru vilaṅkiyum kuṟittalāl, am mayakki nīkkutaṟku ñāy eṉat tirintatu. talivi em āy eṉṉum poruḷil yāy eṉṟum, tōḻi nam āy eṉṉum poruḷil ñāy eṉṟum, naṟṟāyik kuṟittali 7-ām mullikkaliyuṭ kāṇka.
yāyum ñāyum yārākiyarō eṉṉum kuṟuntokiyaṭiyil (40:1) varum ñāyum eṉṉum col, mōyum eṉṟiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. ittakiya marapu vaḻukkaḷ kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē tōṉṟi viṭṭaṉa.
tāy eṉṉum peyar taṉ muṉṉoṭṭup poruḷiḻantu vaḻaṅkuvatu, tamappaṉ, tamiyaṉ, tamakki, tavvi, taṅki mutaliya colvaḻakkup pōṉṟatu. tamappaṉ - takappaṉ. tam avvi - tavvi. appaṉ peyar aṇṇaṉiyum kuṟittatu pōl, ammi peyar akkiyiyum kuṟittatu.
aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl aiyi. aiyi = talivi, āciriyi, āciriyaṉ maṉivi, tuṟaviṉi, kāḷi, civi (malimakaḷ). kāḷiyi ammiyeṉṟum, avvi (koṟṟavi) yeṉṟum, kūṟutali nōkkuka.
tāy, araci mutaliya piṟa poruḷkaḷik kāṭṭum ilakkiyam iṟantupaṭṭatu.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku (tamiḻakattiṟku) vanta piṉ, avaruḷ illaṟattār pārppāreṉṟum, tuṟaviyar pōṉṟavar aiyar allatu antaṇar eṉṟum, aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar. nāḷaṭivil ellāp pirāmaṇarum antaṇar eṉpatik kulap peyarākavum, aiyar eṉpatip paṭṭap peyarākavum, koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. aiyar avarkaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum aiyaravar eṉpatu, teluṅkil aiyavāru eṉṟum aiyakāru eṉṟum tirintatu. aiyakāru - aiyaṅkār.
pirāmaṇarip piṉpaṟṟi, vīra civarum curāṭṭirar eṉṉum paṭṭunūṟkārarum aiyar eṉpatik kulappaṭṭap peyarāka mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
camayak kuravar eṉṉum poruḷil, kiṟittavak kurumārum aiyar eṉa aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam aiyaṉ, aiyi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, kumari nāṭṭuk kālat tiṉiṉṟē aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat toṭarntu vantirukkum tūya teṉcollāyiruppavum, avaṟṟi muṟiyē ārya, āryā eṉṉum (āriya iṉattik kuṟikkum) irupāṟ peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu, pirāmaṇariṉ kuṟumput taṉattiyum, talimip patavi tāṅkum tamiḻp pērāciriyariṉ aṟiyāmiyiyum aṭimit taṉattiyum, ettuṇit teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu!
muṉivarāṉa aiyar antaṇar eṉṟum peyar peṟuvar; perumpālum āṭiyiṉṟiyiruppar.
antaṇar eṉpatu, ellāvuyirkaḷiṭattum aḻakiya kuḷirnta aruḷiyuṭiyavarāy muṟṟat tuṟanta muṉivarik kuṟikkum col; oru kulattiṟkō ōr āriya vakuppiṟkō uriya peyaraṉṟu.
	antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
	centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ. 		             (kuṟaḷ. 30)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar nīttār perumi eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kūṟutal kāṇka. nīttār - tuṟantōr.
imiyavil vāṅkiya īrñcaṭi antaṇaṉ                (kalit. 38) 
eṉpatu civaṉik kuṟittatu. 
	nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē
	āyuṅ kāli antaṇark kuriya 			         (71)
eṉṟu tolkāppiya marapiyaliṟ kūṟiyiruppatu, tamiḻ marapiṟku muṟṟum māṟākum. itu āriyar tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa karuttē.
	maṟṟun toṭarppā ṭevaṉkol piṟappaṟukka
	luṟṟārk kuṭampum miki.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷi (345) nōkki uṇmi teḷika. muṉivariṭam uṭampaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟumirātu.
paṇṭāram eṉṉum collum teṉ collē.
paṇṭāram kaḷañciyam. pala nūlkaḷik kaṟṟa paṇṭitaṉ ōraṟivuk kaḷañciyam pōliruppatāl, uvamiyāku peyarāyp paṇṭāram eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
tirukkāḷatti ñāṉak kaḷañciyamē eṉṟu irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ iṟivaṉi viḷittaliyum (aruṭpā, 1. viṇṇappa. 255), āṅkilattilum oru pēraṟiñaṉi `repository of curious information' eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkiyum, kūrntu  nōkkuka. vaṇṇam pāṭuvatiṟ ciṟanta pāvalaṉi vaṇṇak kaḷañciyam eṉṟu kūṟutalum kāṇka.
paṇṭam poruḷ. paṇṭam+āram = paṇṭāram. o. nō: vaṭṭam+āram = vaṭṭāram. āram eṉpatu ōr iṭap poruḷīṟu.
vaṭamoḻiyār, iyaṟporuḷil varumpōtu paṇṭāram eṉṉum collip pāṇṭāra (bhandara) eṉṟum, ākupeyarpporuḷil varumpōtu accollip piṇṭāra eṉṟum, vēṟupaṭuttiyum vērp poruḷiṉṟiyum kāṭṭuvar.
cittar maliyiliruppavarāyiṉum, eṅkum iyaṅkupavar. avar aṟivar eṉṟum collappaṭuvar. cittu aṟivu.
	maṟuvil ceyti mūvakik kālamum
	neṟiyiṉ āṟṟiya aṟivaṉ tēyamum.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (puṟam. 20) kūṟuvatu cittarip paṟṟiyē.
cettal = karututal, karutiyaṟital, aṟital.
cettu = karuti (i. kā. vi. e.).
aravunī ruṇal cettu (kalit. 45).
ce - cettu (toḻiṟpeyar) = karuttu, aṟivu.
o. nō : o - ottu = 	1. i. kā. vi. e.
                            				2. toḻiṟpeyar.
 cettu - cittu. o. nō : centuram - cinturam. cittu eṉṉum teṉ collum citti (siddhi) eṉṉum vaṭacollum vevvēṟām. cittu aṟivu; citti kikūṭutal. cittaṉ āṟṟal cittu eṉappaṭum. atic citti eṉpatu tavaṟu. cittu viḷiyāṭutal eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakki nōkkuka.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇa maruttuva mutaṉūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyavar muṉivarum cittarumē.
	viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
	muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.           (tol. marapiyal,95).
tamiḻ maruttuvam citta maruttuvam eṉappaṭum. poṉṉākkamum (iracavātamum) cittar kaliyē.
kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa cittaruḷ patiṉeṇmar peyar peṟṟavar. avar aṟivup pāṭalkaḷip patiṉeṇ cittar ñāṉak kōviyiṟ kāṇalām.
cittarum muṉivarum orōvoru camiyam nāṭṭiṟkum vantu celvatākac collappaṭuvar.
āci, cerukku, aṟivu mayakkam eṉṉum mūṉṟum aṭiyōṭu nīṅkappeṟuvatē, tuṟavaṟattiṉ paḻutta niliyām.
kāma vekuḷi mayakkam ivimūṉṟaṉ
	nāmaṅ keṭakkeṭum nōy.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (kuṟaḷ. 360).
tuvarāṭi yaṇinta piṉpum taṉṉip piṟappil uyarntavaṉākak karutum āriyaṉukku, im mūṉṟum, ciṟappāka nāṉ eṉṉum cerukku, nīṅkap peṟuvatu, muṭavaṉ maramēṟik komput tēṉik kuṭippatē yām.
tamiḻar illaṟattil veṟṟi kaṇṭatu pōṉṟē tuṟavaṟattilum, aruḷuṭimi, pulāṉ maṟuttal, kaḷḷāmi, vāymi, vekuḷāmi, iṉṉāceyyāmi, kollāmi, avāvaṟuttal eṉṉum aṟuvaki yaṟaṅkaḷiyum; nōṉpu (tavam), meyyuṇartal (ōkam) eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻikaḷiyum, kaṭippiṭittu veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻat tuṟaviyaruḷ taliciṟantavar cittar. avar vāṉvaḻi iyaṅkutal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭu pāytal, tōṉṟi maṟital, maṇṇuḷ iruttal, uṇṇātu vāḻtal mutaliya pala āṟṟalkaḷi aṭintavar.  tirumūlar oru cittar. paṭṭiṉattārum irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷum, iṟutiyil  cittanili aṭintatāka avar varalāṟu kūṟum.
nōṉpu (tavam) eṉpatu illaṟattārkkum tuṟavaṟattārkkum potuvām.
9. camayavoḻukkam
camayam eṉpatu matam. atu oruvakic camivik kuṟittalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camital nukarccikkup patamātal. arici cōṟākac camivatu uṇpataṟkup patamātal. peṇpiḷḷi maṅkiyāka camivatu  maṇa nukarccikkup patamātal. ātaṉ (āṉmā) iṟiyaṭimiyākac camivatu,  vīṭupēṟṟiṟku allatu iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiyaṭivaṟkup patamātal.
camiyam eṉpatu vēḷiyik kuṟikkum pōtum, oru viṉikkup patamāṉa allatu takka kālanili eṉṉum poruḷatē. nalla camiyam, takka camiyam, ēṟṟa camiyam eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka. palavakic camivukaḷuḷḷum ātaṉ iṟiyaṭimiyākac camivatu taliciṟanta
tātalāl, camivu eṉṉum viṉi, ataṉiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. āyiṉum, poruḷ mayakkamillāvāṟu, cōṟu camivatu camiyal eṉṟum, peṇ camivatu camital eṉṟum, nēram camivatu camiyam eṉṉum, ātaṉ camivatu camayam eṉṟum, vēṟupaṭuttic collap peṟum.
camiyam eṉṉum col amiyam eṉṉum colliṉ mutaṉmiki (prothesis). amital poruntutal allatu takutiyātal. nallatoṉṟu vāyppiṉ, amintu viṭṭatu eṉpar. amiyum nēram amiyam.
uyir mutaṟcoṟkaḷ coṉmutal meykaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum peṟṟuc coṟkaḷip piṟappittal, collākka neṟimuṟimiyām.
e-ṭu : uruḷ - curuḷ, ēṇ - cēṇ.
ami eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum am eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu tirinta
tākum. itaṉāl, camayam eṉṉum col tūya teṉ collātal teḷika. atu vaṭa colleṉṟu vaṭamoḻic coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutaliyilum kuṟittiruppatu koṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
āriyar varumuṉpē, tamiḻar camayat tuṟiyilum tali ciṟantiruntaṉar. āyiṉum, ellārum anniliyaṭiyavilli. enta nākarika nāṭṭilum, taliyāyār, iṭiyāyār, kaṭiyāyār eṉṉum muttiṟattār irukkavē ceyvar.
ākavē, tamiḻar matamum, ciṟu teyva vaṇakkam, perun teyva vaḻipāṭu, kaṭavuḷ neṟi eṉa munnilip paṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ
(1) 	teṉpulattār (iṟanta muṉṉōr).
(2) 	naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷ.
(3) 	pēykaḷ (pēy, pūtam, muṉi, caṭimuṉi, aṇaṅku (mōkiṉi), cūraramakaḷir mutaliyaṉa).
(4) 	tīya vuyirikaḷ (pāmpu, cuṟā, mutali mutaliyaṉa).
(5) 	iṭat teyvaṅkaḷ (āṟṟut teyvam, malit teyvam, kāṭṭut teyvam, nakart teyvam, nāṭṭut teyvam).
(6) 	iyaṟkip pūtaṅkaḷ (kāṟṟum tīyum).
(7) 	vāṉac cuṭarkaḷ (katiravaṉum tiṅkaḷum). 
(8) 	celvat teyvam (tirumakaḷ).
(9) 	kalvit teyvam (nāmakaḷ allatu coṉmakaḷ).
(10) 	pālvari teyvam (ūḻ vakuppatu).
perun teyvaṅkaḷ
ivi aintiṇit teyvaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟiñci 	- 	cēyōṉ.
mulli 	- 	māyōṉ.
pāli 	- 	kāḷi.
marutam 	- 	vēntaṉ.
neytal 	- 	vāraṇaṉ.
cēyōṉ civantavaṉ. cēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarum apporuḷatē. murukaṉ, vēlaṉ, kumaraṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum ivaṉukkuṇṭu. civaṉ eṉpatu cēyōṉ eṉpataṉ ulaka vaḻakku vaṭivam. ippeyar vaṭivu vēṟupāṭṭik koṇṭu, piṟkālattil āriyar orē teyvatti iraṇṭākkit tantiyum makaṉumākak kūṟi viṭṭaṉar. kumaraṉ eṉpataṟkum cēy eṉṉum kuṟukkattiṟkum makaṉ eṉṟu tavaṟākap poruḷ koṇṭatē itaṟku aṭippaṭi. 
civaṉ eṉṟu āriyat teyvam oṉṟumilli. civa eṉṉum col, nalla allatu maṅkala eṉṉum poruḷil, uruttiraṉukkum intiraṉukkum akkiṉikkum potuvāṉa aṭimoḻiyākavē āriya vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
puṟakkaṇ kāṇa muṭiyātavaṟṟiyum neṭuntoliviluḷḷa vaṟṟiyum kaṇṭaṟiyum ōr aṟivuk kaṇ pōṉṟa uṟuppu, kumari nāṭṭu makkaḷ neṟṟiyiliruntateṉṟum, ataṉālēyē avartam iṟivaṉukkum (civaṉukkum) oru neṟṟik kaṇṇip paṭittuk kūṟiṉareṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. veḷḷimali (kili)  yirukkiyum kāḷiyūrtiyum civaṉik kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākak kāṭṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ talimiyamiccarum pirāmaṇarum ciṟanta civaṉaṭiyārum vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi vallunarumākiya māṇikkavācakar,
pāṇṭi nāṭē paḻampati yākavum	           (tiruvākacam, 2 : 118)
teṉṉā ṭuṭiya civaṉē pōṟṟi                (tiruvācakam 4 : 164)
eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭiyē civaneṟip piṟappiṭamākak kuṟittal kāṇka. iṉi, civaperumāṉ aṟupattu nāṉku tiruviḷiyāṭal kaḷiyum maturiyil aruḷic ceytatiyum, muttoḻil allatu aintoḻil tirunaṭattit tilliyil āṟṟuvatiyum, eṇmaṟac ceyalkaḷ ceyta iṭaṅkaḷ (aṭṭavīraṭṭam) tamiḻ nāṭṭilirup patiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
māyōṉ kariyavaṉ, mā karuppu. māya kiruṣṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkkum itaṟkum yātoru toṭarpumilli. kaṇṇapirāṉ piṟakku  muṉpē, kiruṣṇa eṉṉum col karuppu eṉṉum poruḷiṉ irukkum   vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kiruṣṇa pakṣa  (karumpakkam)  kiruṣṇa sarppa (karumpāmpu) eṉṉum peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. kiruṣṇa eṉṉum coṟku vēta moḻiyil vērilli; tamiḻilēyē uḷḷatu.
kaḷ - karu - karuḷ = 	1. karumi.
karuḷ tarukaṇṭattu......ifiyah®” 		     (tēvā. 337, 4)
				2. iruḷ (piṅ.).
				3. kuṟṟam.
karuḷ tīrvaliyāl 		          (cētupu. muttīrt. 5).
karuḷ = kruṣ. ḷakara meyyīṟu vētamoḻiyil ṣakara meyyīṟākat  tiriyum.
e-ṭu : Rḷ- Rṣ(to dry) cŸ- uZ, (to burn). 
māyōṉ, māl, viṇṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ oru poruṭcoṟ kaḷ. 
māl = karumi, mukil, tirumāl.
viṇṭu = mukil, vāṉam, tirumāl.
viṇ = vāṉam, mukil, mēlulakam.
viṇ - viṇṭu.
āriya vētattil viṣṇu vṉṉumbpayarftiravaṉikFṟittatu.fhiy eḻucciyiyum naṇpakaṟ celaviyum eṟpāṭṭu (cāyuṅkāla) vīḻcciyiyumē mūvaṭiyāl (mūṉṟu eṭṭāl) ulaka muḻuvatiyum viṣṇu (katiravaṉ) aḷappatāka mutaliṟ koṇṭaṉar vēta āriyar.
kuṟiñci nilattār tam teyvatti maliyil tōṉṟum nerup piṉ kūṟākavum, mulli nilattār tam teyvatti vāṉattiṉiṉṟu poḻiyum mukiliṉ kūṟākavum, koṇṭaṉar. itaṉālēyē, civaṉukkut tīvaṇṇaṉ, antivaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tirumālukkuk kārvaṇṇaṉ, maṇivaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tōṉṟiṉa.
kāḷi karuppi. kaḷ - kaḷvaṉ = kariyavaṉ. kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ = karumi. kāḷ - kāḷam = karumi. kāḷ - kāḷi. kāḷi kūḷikaṭkut talivi. kūḷi pēy. pēy karuppeṉṟum iruḷ eṉṟum peyar peṟum, karuppi, karuppāy eṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarkaḷi, iṉṟum peṇṭirkkiṭuvatu peruvaḻakku. tāy eṉṉum poruḷil ammi (ammaṉ) aiyi eṉṟum, iḷiñi eṉṉum poruḷil kaṉṉi, kumari eṉṟum, veṟṟi tarupavaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil koṟṟavi (koṟṟavvi) eṉṟum, kāḷikkup peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum. aṅkāḷammi eṉpatu aḻakiya kāḷiyammi eṉṉum poruḷatu.
āriyar varumuṉ vaṭa intiyā muḻutum tiraviṭar paraviyirun tatiṉāl, vaṅka nāṭṭil kāḷik kōṭṭam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ = aracaṉ. mutaṟkālattil aracaṉum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ. ataṉāl, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉukkum kaṭavuṭkum, kōyil eṉṉum peyar araṇmaṉikkum teyvak kōṭṭattiṟkum, potuvāyiṉa. vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ vāṉavarkkum aracaṉāki vāṉulakattiṉiṉṟu maḻiyip poḻivikkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu paṇṭi maruta nilamakkaḷ karutiyatāl, maḻit teyvatti vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
āriyar varumuṉpē, moḻi vēṟupāṭṭiṉāl vēntaṉukku vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta peyar intiraṉ eṉpatu. intiraṉ aracaṉ. ākavē iraṇṭum oru poruṭ coṟkaḷē. vēta āriyar intira vaṇakkatti, vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭarip piṉpaṟṟiyē mēṟ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mēliyāriya nāṭukaḷil intira vaṇakkamē yiruntatilliyeṉṟu, mākacu mullar (Max Muller) kūṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
kaṭik kaḻakak kālam vari kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil    naṭipeṟṟu  vanta intira viḻāvum, tamiḻar vēntaṉ viḻāvē.
vāraṇaṉ = kaṭal teyvam. vāraṇam kaṭal. kaṭalukku oru teyvamiruppatākak karuti, ati neytal nilamakkaḷ vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
mutaṟkālat tiraviṭar vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu kirēkka nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ, vāraṇam eṉṉum col (ouranos) eṉat tirintatu. appeyart teyvam mutaliṟ kirēkkarkkuk kaṭal teyva mākavēyiruntu, piṉpu, vāṉatteyvamāyiṟṟu. kirēkkattiṟku mika neruṅkiya moḻiyip pēcivanta kīḻiyāriyaruḷ oru pirivārāṉa vēta āriyar, kaṭali
yaṟiyāmal neṭukavum nilavaḻiyākavē vantatiṉāl, maḻitteyvattiyē varuṇā eṉa aḻittaṉar. avar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭarōṭum teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻarōṭum toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē, varuṇaṉik kaṭal teyvamākak karutat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, iṉṉum maḻikkāka varuṇaṉi vēṇṭuvatē pirāmaṇar vaḻakkamā yiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇalulakamum (akat. 5) eṉṟu, vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum peyari vaṭamoḻi vaṭiviṟ kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum. atu vāraṇaṉ mēya ērmaṇa lulakamum eṉṟiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aintiṇit teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ, cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭum māyōṉ vaḻipāṭum piṟkālattil iruperuñcamayaṅkaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭin
tuḷḷaṉa.
aintiṇit teyvaṅkaḷum tamiḻt teyvaṅkaḷē yeṉṟum, piṟkālattil āriyar avaṟṟippaṟṟip palvēṟu katikaḷ (purāṇaṅ kaḷ) kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar eṉṟum, eṉ tamiḻar matam eṉṉum nūlil virivāy viḷakkap peṟum.
kaṭavuḷ neṟi
ūrpēr kuṇaṅkuṟiyaṟṟu, maṉamoḻi meykaḷik kaṭantu eṅkum   niṟintiruttal, ellām aṟintiruttal, ellām vallatātal, eṉṟu  muṇmi, aruḷvaṭivuṭimi, iṉpa niliniṟṟal, oppuyar viṉmi,  mācumaṟuviṉmi ākiya eṇkuṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyatāy ellāvulakaṅ  kaḷiyum paṭittuk kāttaḻittuvarum oru param poruḷuṇṭeṉṟu nampi, ataṉi vaḻipaṭuvatē kaṭavuḷ neṟiyām. itu cittamatam eṉavum paṭum.
ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantiruppataṉālēyē iṟivaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. āriyar vantapiṉ, iccol mutaliṟ perun teyvaṅkaṭkum, piṉpu ciṟu teyvaṅkaṭkum, iṟutiyil makkaḷāṉa  muṉivarkkum vaḻaṅki iḻivaṭintuḷḷatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar tam nūṉ mukattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu uruvamaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttē. tamiḻariṉ uruvavaṇakkamallāk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiuḷi yiṭṭa kalliyum eṭṭut ticiyum eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭaliyum aṅkiṅkeṉātapaṭi, paṇṇēṉ uṉakkāṉa pūci eṉṉum tāyumāṉavar pāṭaliyum nōkkiyuṇarka.
itaṉ virivi eṉ tamiḻar matam eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
kaṭavuḷiyum maṟumiyiyum nampāta oru ciṟu kūṭṭattārum akkālattiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, aṟiñar avarik kaṇṭittaṉar.
	ulakattār uṇṭeṇpa tilleṉpāṉ viyat
	talakiyā vikkap paṭum.			          (kuṟaḷ. 850) 
	nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum
	illi yeṉpōrk kiṉaṉā kiliyar. 	  (puṟam. 29: 11-12)
	maṟumiyoṉ ṟuṇṭō maṉappaṭṭa ellām
	peṟumāṟu ceymmiṉeṉ pārē - naṟuneyyuḷ
	kaṭṭi yaṭiyik kaḷivittuk kaṇcorīi
	iṭṭiki tīṟṟu pavar. 			    (paḻamoḻi,108).
10. toḻilkaḷ
1. uḻavu
	uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam
	uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē		 (puṟam.18)
	uḻuvār ulakattiṟ kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟā
	teḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu		         (kuṟaḷ.1032)
	irappōr cuṟṟamum purappōr koṟṟamum
	uḻaviṭi viḷippōr			 (cilap. 10: 151-2)
uḻavar, ātalāl, orunāṭṭu vāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭi uḻavu eṉpatip paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ uṇarntirun taṉar. 
uḻavutoḻiṟku iṉṟiyamiyātavi nilam, nīr, viti, erutu eṉṉum nāṉkām.
paṇṭit tamiḻar, aintiṇikaḷuḷ marutattiyum neytaliyum meṉpulameṉṟum, kuṟiñciyiyum, mulliyiyum vaṉpulameṉṟum, vakuttiruntaṉar. marutanilatti nāṭu eṉṟum, maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷik kāṭu eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. kuṟiñci nilattil uḻattakka iṭatti ērkāṭu eṉṟum, uḻat takātatik kottuk kāṭu eṉṟum, pakuttaṉar. kuṟiñciyilum mulliyilumuḷḷa viḷi nilaṅkaḷ kolli allatu puṉam eṉṟum, marutanilattiluḷḷa viḷinilaṅkaḷ cey eṉṟum, putuk kolli iti eṉṟum, paḻaṅ kolli muti eṉṟum, ciṟitu ceyyap peṟṟa cey puṉcey eṉṟum, naṉṟāyc ceyyap peṟṟa cey naṉcey eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa. ceytal eṉpatu tiruttutal allatu paṇpaṭuttutal. puṉmi ciṟumi.
kolli eṉpatu vāṉa vārik kāṭeṉṟum, puṉcey kiṇaṟṟup  pāyccaleṉṟum, naṉcey āṟṟu allatu ērip pāyccaleṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. eḷ, koḷ mutaliyaṉa kollip payir; kēḻvaraku, cōḷam mutaliyaṉa puṉceyp payir;  nel, karumpu mutaliyaṉa naṉceyp payir.
naṉceykaḷuḷ, paḻamiyāṉatu paḻaṉam eṉṟum, pōraṭikkum kaḷa muḷḷatu kaḻaṉi eṉṟum, collap peṟum. paṇṇi eṉpatu paṇ ṇappaṭṭatu (paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭatu) eṉṉum poruḷa tēṉum,            vaḻakkil kaḷamar allatu ceṟumar eṉṉum paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ kuṭiyiruntu vēli ceyyum periya vayaṟparappiyē (farm) kuṟikkum. cēṟuḷḷamiyāl ceṟu eṉṟum, vippup pōṉṟamiyāl vayal  eṉṟum, naṉceykkup peyarkaḷuṇṭu.
puṉamāyiṉum puṉceyāyiṉum, paṇṭittamiḻar mēṭṭu nilattiṟ payir ceyya virumpavilli. mēṭu cuval eṉṟum paḷḷam aval eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
mēṭṭup puṉ ceyi uḻutavaṉum keṭṭāṉ, mēṉi miṉukkiyi maṇantavaṉum keṭṭāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
naṉceyp pācaṉattiṟku, āṟṟunīr illāviṭaṅkaṭkuk kaṇṇāṟu
kaḷum kālvāykaḷum veṭṭippāycciṉar. atu iyalā viṭattu  ērikaḷi veṭṭiṉar. ērt toḻiṟku utavuvatu ēri. kuḷippatu kuḷam. iṉṟu ēriyit tavaṟākak kuḷameṉpar oru cārār. iyaṟkiyāka uṇṭāṉa ēri allatu kuḷam poyki eṉappaṭum. mulli nilattiṟ puṉceyp pācaṉattiṟkuk kiṇaṟukaḷi veṭṭiṉar.
erutukaḷik koṇṭu kiṇaṟṟu nīri iṟikkum ēṟṟam kammāli eṉappaṭṭatu. am = nīr, am - kam = nīr. kam + āli = kammāli.  āli cuṟṟi varuvatu. karumpāli eṉpati nōkkuka. ālutal āṭutal. mutaṟkālattil erutukaḷ oru marat tūṇic cuṟṟi vantaṉa. kammāliyeṉpatu iṉṟu kamali eṉṟum kavali eṉṟum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉṟum kamaliyāṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. iṉṟu erutukaḷ nērākac celvatāl kavaliyōṭṭutal  eṉṟum kūṟuvar. kamali yēṟṟattik kapili yēṟṟam eṉṟu ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali kuṟit
tiruppatu tavaṟākum.  kapili (va.) yeṉpatu kurāl eṉṉum āvaki. āvik kaṭṭi nīriṟippatu vaḻakkattiṟku māṟākum.
vitik keṉṟu mutaṟ kāyppiyum ciṟanta maṇikaḷiyum otukki vittaṉar. akkālattu uḻavar poruḷīṭṭalik kuṟik koḷḷātu, uṇavu viḷittaliyē kuṟikkoṇṭu patiṉeṇ kūlaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ vakikaḷiyum, āṇṭutōṟum viḷittu   vantaṉar. nūṟṟuk
kaṇakkāṉa nelvakikaḷ viḷikkappeṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉa
vaṟṟi iṉṟu kaṇṇālum kāṇa muṭiyavilli; kātālum kēṭka muṭiyavilli. poṉtiṉi, centiṉi, karuntiṉi eṉṉum mūvakiyuḷ, iṉṟu poṉṟiṉiyē kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. avarivakikaḷuṭ pala āṇṭutōṟum ovvoṉṟāy maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu āṭciyi naṭattupavarukkup pataviyik kākka vēṇṭu meṉpatēyaṉṟi, viti vakikaḷip pēṇavēṇṭu meṉṉum kali nōkkilli.
tamiḻakattil uḻavut toḻiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇiyāka, toṉṟu toṭṭup payaṉpaṭṭuvarum vilaṅku erutākum, ērt toḻiṟku utavuvataṉāl kāḷi eruteṉap paṭṭatu. ēr - ērtu-erutu.  kāṭṭu māṭṭip piṭittup paḻakki vīṭṭu vilaṅkākki ērt toḻiṟkup payaṉ
paṭuttiṉar.
erutiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi nōkkiyē ērt toḻilip pakaṭu eṉṟaṉar.

paṇṭiyuḻavar ellāp payir paccikaḷiyum paruvamaṟintē viḷittu vantaṉar. (cittirimāta vuḻavu pattari māṟṟut taṅkam. paṭṭantappiṉāl naṭṭam. āṭippaṭṭam tēṭi viti, eṉpaṉa piṟkālattup paḻamoḻikaḷākum.) mārikkāla vēḷāṇmiyik kālam eṉṟum, vēṉiṟkāla vēḷāṇmiyik kōṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
iṉṟu naṭipeṟṟu varum vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷ pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu toṭṭē paṇṭāṭu paḻanaṭiyāy varuvaṉa vākum.
kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal, kal poṟukkutal, eru viṭutal, āḻavutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (tāḷiyaṭittal, palliyāṭutal, ūṭṭittal, paṭaliḻuttal). puḻutiyuṇakkal, vitittal, kaḷiyeṭuttal, kāval kāttal. aṟuvaṭi ceytal, kaḷañcērttal, cāṇiyaṭital (cūṭu pōṭutal, pōramittal), cāṇi pirittal, kāyap pōṭutal, piṇiya
laṭittal (kaṭāviṭutal, atari tirittal), vikkōl allatu cakki allatu kappi nīkkal, poli tūṟṟal, poliyaḷattal, vitikkeṭuttal, kaḷañciyañ cērttal eṉpaṉa vāṉavārip putuk kolli vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷām. kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal putukkollikkē nikaḻum.

naṉcey vēḷāṇmiyāyiṉ, nāṟṟuppāval, pātti piṭittal, nīr pāyccal toḷi(cēṟu)kkalakkal, kuḻi mitittal, naṭutal eṉṉum viṉikaḷum, avaṟṟiṟkuppiṉ kaḷiyeṭuttal mutaliya vāṉavāri vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷum, nikaḻum.

tiruppataṉālum.

3.kammiyam
karuttal ceytal. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa oru tamiḻ viṉiccol.
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ karuniṟamum pukar (brown) niṟamum poṉṉiṟamum uḷḷavar toṉṟu toṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. poṉṉiṟattic civappeṉṟum veḷḷiyeṉṟum colvatu vaḻakkam. poṉmi karumi ākiya iruniṟam paṟṟiyē, veṇkaḷamar karuṅkaḷamar. veḷḷāḷar, kārāḷar, veḷḷokkalar, kārokkalar eṉṉum colliṇikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. kāyppēṟumāṟu viṉi ceyyiṉ karuppar akaṅki karuppatum civappar akaṅki civappatum iyalpu. itupaṟṟi viṉiceytalik kuṟikka, karuttal ceytal eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. kikarukkumā ceyvatu karuttal; civakkumāṟu ceyvatu ceytal.
karu - karumam = ceyki. karu - karuvi = ceykikku vēṇṭum tuṇipporuḷ. karu - karaṇam = ceyki, karuvi. aṇam ōr īṟu.
iṉi, karumic coṟkup perumip poruḷumiruttalāl, karuttal = mikuttal eṉṟumām. putitāyc ceyyap peṟum oru poruḷ ēṟkeṉavēyuḷḷa ataṉ vakiyi mikuttal kāṇka. make eṉṉum āṅkilac colliyum magnus (great) eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirippar āṅkilac collārāycciyāḷar. taṉ viṉiyum piṟaviṉip poruḷ payappatu collākka marapiṟ kottatē.
o.nō: veḷuttal = veḷḷiyātal (taṉviṉi), veḷḷiyākkutal (piṟaviṉi). vaṇṇāṉ tuṇikaḷi veḷukkiṟāṉ eṉpatil veḷuttal viṉi piṟaviṉiyāyiruttal kāṇka.
karumam - kammam = ceyki. toḻil, kittoḻil kol toḻil,  kammiyat toḻil.
kammam = kammiyattoḻil.
kammañcey mākkaḷ 			         (nālaṭi.393)
kammam - kamam
ivviru vaṭivum mutalil toḻil eṉṟē poruḷpaṭṭaṉa. ulakil mutal tōṉṟiya toḻil uḻavē. toḻil, ki eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarum, cey, paṇṇi eṉṉum nilap peyarum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
toḷ - (toḷil) - toḻil. toḷḷutal tōṇṭutal.
ki = kiyāṟ ceyyum ceyki allatu toḻil, mutal toḻilāṉa uḻavu.
	iravār irappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
	kiceytūṇ māli yavar.

uḻavu mutal toḻilāṉatiṉāl, kammam, kamam eṉṉum iruvaṭivum mutalil uḻavik kuṟittaṉa.

	nilaṅkari kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭiṟan tōrē. 		            (kuṟun. 67).
eṉpavi poṟkollariṉ paṇiyik kuṟippaṉa. kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷil, poṟkollaṉ putukkampiyiṟ kōkkumāṟu taṉ ukirāl (nakattāl) paṟṟiyirukkum uruṇṭiyāṉa poṟkāciṟku, vēppam paḻattik kavvik koṇṭirukkum kiḷimūkki uvamaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu, pārāṭṭattakkatu.
taccu vēliyiṟ ciṟanta vēlippāṭuḷḷa cey poruḷ tērākum.

	tiṅkaḷ valitta kālaṉ ṉōṉē. 			      (puṟam)
eṉpatu tērttaccaṉik kuṟittatu.

	maḻuvuṭik kāṭṭakat taṟṟē 		(puṟam. 206: 11-12)

āṅkilattilum, taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷ ceyyum taccaṉi joiner eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
taccaṉ eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil takṣa eṉṟum, kirēkkattil tekton eṉṟum, tiriyum. uṇmi iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutaliyit tokutta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar takṣa eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu taccaṉ eṉpatu vantatākak kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyiṉ vaṭṭakkaccik kaṭṭil, piṉvarumāṟu yāṉi maruppiṉāl (tantattiṉāl) ciṟanta vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟṟatāka, neṭunel vāṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
nāṟpatāṇṭu akaviyuḷḷatum, muracupōṉṟa periya kālkaḷiyum aḻakiya pukar niṟinta mattakattiyum uṭiyatum, pōrukkuc ciṟanta teṉṟu peyar peṟṟatum, pōr ceytu iṟantatumāṉa, yāṉiyiṉ tāṉē vīḻnta kompi irupuṟamum kaṉamum cemmiyum oppac cetukki, kūriya ciṟṟuḷiyālē periya ilit toḻili iṭiyilē amittu; niṟi
cūliyiṉ pālkaṭṭiya mārpu pōlat tiraṇṭa kuṭattiyum, uḷḷip pūṭu pōṉṟa kaṭiccaliyum, koṇṭa kālkaḷip porutti; mūṭṭuvāy māṭcimippaṭat takaṭukaḷi āṇikaḷāl tittu, aḻakākat toṭutta muttuk kuñcaṅkaḷic cuṟṟi varat toṅka viṭṭu; puliyiṉ variyik koṇṭa polivu peṟṟa kaccālē takaṭumaṟiya naṭuviṭam muḻutum piṉṉi; kuṟṟamaṟṟup pēraḷavu koṇṭu perum peyarpeṟṟa vaṭṭak

(neṭunal. 115-128)
nilattiṉmēlōṭu vaṇṭiyum tērum pōṉṟē nīrmēlōṭum marakkalaṅkaḷum, mutaṉ mutal tamiḻ nāṭṭiltāṉ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa. ōṭam ceypavaṉukku ōṭāvi (ōṭāḷvi) eṉṟum, nāvāy pōṉṟa peruṅkalam ceypavarukkuk kalañcey kammiyar (maṇi. 25:124); kalampuṇar kammiyar (maṇi. 7: 70) eṉṟum, peyar. ivar kappal taccar.
 kalattiyuṭiya neytal nila makkaḷ kalavar, kalatti ōṭṭum talivaṉ nīkāṉ (nīkāmaṉ, mīkāmaṉ mīkāṉ (captin). kalattil viṉi ceypavar ōcunar (silors) kalattiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceypavaṉ kaṭalōṭi.
tirikaṭa lōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu.
eṉṟu auviyār kūṟiyatu kāṇka.
koṭittēr vītiyum tēvar kōṭṭamum
eṉṟu maṇimēkali (21: 120) kūṟuvatāl, teyva uruvatti vittirukkum perun tērum akkālat tiruntirukkalām.
ikkālattuḷḷa tērkaḷuḷ tiruvārūrt tēr mikap peritu. atuvum ariyē yarikkāl tēreṉṟum, muṉpu erintu pōṉatu mukkāl tēreṉṟum, kūṟuvar. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ataṟkum muṉpē muḻuttēr iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. muḻuttēr ēḻutaṭṭum mukkāltēr aintu taṭṭum arit tēr mūṉṟutaṭṭum uḷḷavaṉākac collappaṭum. ivi muṟiyē nūṟaṭiyum, eḻupattintaṭiyum aṟupataṭiyum uyaramuḷḷaṉa eṉpar.

maṉṉārkuṭimatilaḻaku...kidaHF, tiruvārūrt tēraḻaku, tiruviṭimarutūrt teruvaḻaku eṉṟoru paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu.
vāṉaḷāvum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭirunta paṇṭik kālattil, māperun tērkaḷ ceyyap paṭṭiruntatil viyappilli.
teyvat tēriṟ pala aṇiyuṟuppukkaḷum tēvar muṉivar paṭimikaḷum yāḷi kutiri yuruvaṅkaḷum ceytu vikkap peṟutalāl, tērttaccar taccut kaliyil taliciṟantavarāvar.
kaṭṭumaram mutal kappalvari palvakik kalaṅkaḷiyum ceyyum taccar kalampuṇar kammiyar (kappal taccar) eṉap paṭṭār.
kāṭci kālkōḷ nīrppaṭi naṭukal
eṉṉum veṭcittiṇit tuṟikaḷum (tol. puṟat5), ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṉimaliyiṟ kalleṭuttuk kaṇṇakikkup paṭimañ camippittatum, kaltaccu viṉikkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
maṇṭapaṅkaḷi yellām kallāṟ kaṭṭuvatē toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkam. nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum viḻāppaṭi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum attakiya kaṟkōyil kaṟṟaḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu. taḷikōyil. teyvap paṭimikaḷ (cilikaḷ) aivakik kollarālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.

11. vāṇikam
nākarika makkaḷ vāḻkkikku, uṇavu pōṉṟē uṭi, kalam, uṟiyuḷ, ūrti mutaliya poruḷkaḷum, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya celvamum, iṉṟiyamiyātiruppatāl, poruḷāṭcit tuṟiyil, oru nāṭṭiṉ nal vāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāṉatu uḻavut toḻileṉṟum, ataṟkaṭuttavi iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumāṉa viḷi
poruṭkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum palvēṟu kittoḻilkaḷeṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟ kaṭuttatu nilavaḻiyum nīrvaḻiyum naṭattappeṟum vaṇikameṉṟum, paṇṭit tamiḻar naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, mūvēntarum iruvaki vaṇikattiyum ūkkutaṟkuc cālikaḷum tuṟimukaṅkaḷum amittum, avaṟṟip pātukāttum vantaṉar.
vaṇikam eṉpatu vaṇij eṉṟum, vāṇikam eṉpatu vāṇijya eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
nāṭṭiṟku uḷḷiruntu āṭciyic cevviyāyc ceytaṟku oru marutanilat talinakarum, nāṭṭōrattiṟ kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu nīr vaṇikatti ūkkutaṟku oru neytal talinakarumāka, iru talinakari mūvēntarum toṉṟutoṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.

pāṇṭiyaṉ 	maturi 		koṟki
cōḻaṉ 		uṟiyūr 	pukār (kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam)
cēraṉ 		karuvūr (karūr) 	vañci
mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum muṟṟum aḻintu pōṉamiyāl, pāṇṭiyariṉ iṭikkaḻakakkāla marutanilat talinakarum talikkaḻakakkāla neytal nilattalinakarum, aṟiyappaṭavilli. cērar koṅkunāṭṭi iḻanta piṉṉar, vañcikkē karuvūrp peyari iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar.
kōnakarkaḷilum tuṟinakarkaḷilum iruntu nila vaṇikamum nīr vaṇikamum ceytu vanta nakarattār allatu nakara māntar eṉṉum vakuppiṉari, eṇpērāyaṅkaḷuḷ ōrāyamākakkoṇṭu, avari maṉṉar piṉṉōr eṉṟu ciṟappittum, avaruḷ talimiyāṉavarkku eṭṭip
paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyum, mūvēntarum  ūkkivantaṉar.

eṭṭippaṭṭam, atippeṟṟa vaṇikaṉiṉ maṉivikkum, allatu makaṭkum matippuravup paṭṭamāka (Title of Courtesy) vaḻaṅki vantatākap peruṅkati kūṟum.

nilavāṇikam
nilavāṇikar vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷik kutirikaḷ mēlum kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷ mēlum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyk kāṭṭu vaḻiyē tamiḻakattiyaṭutta vaṭuka nāṭṭiṟkum, neṭun tolivāṉa vaṭanāṭṭiṟkum, kāvaṟ paṭiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu ērāḷamāyp poruḷīṭṭi vantaṉar. avvaṇikak kūṭṭaṅkaṭkuc cāttu eṉṟu peyar. cāttu kūṭṭam. cārttu cāttu. cārtal cērtal.
cāttu =    	1. kūṭṭam.
curivaḷic cāttu niṟimati tavaḻum 		            (kal.63,32)
		2.vaṇikak kūṭṭam.
cāttoṭu pōntu taṉittuya ruḻantēṉ 	      (cilap.11,190)
cāttu eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil sārtta eṉṟu tiriyum.
vaṇikac cāttukkaḷiṉ kāval teyvamākiya aiyaṉārkkuc cāttaṉ eṉṟu peyar. ataṉāl. vaṇikarkkuc cāttaṉ cāttuvaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟpeyarāy vaḻaṅkiṉa. aiyaṉār kōyilil, vaṇikac cāttik kuṟittaṟku maṇkutiri yuruvaṅkaḷ ceytu vittiruttalik kāṇka. cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvap peyar vaṭamoḻiyil cāstā eṉat tiriyum. 
iṉi, vaṇikac cāttiṉ talivaṉum cāttaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ippeyarum sārtta eṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyil cāttik kuṟikkum coṟkum cāttiṉ talivaṉik kuṟikkum coṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉmiyum, cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvattik kuṟikkum col vēṟupaṭṭiruppatum, kaṇṭu uṇmi teḷika.

nīrvāṇikam
	ulakukiḷarn taṉṉa urukeḻu vaṅkam
	pulavuttirip peruṅkaṭal nīriṭip pōḻa
eṉpatāl (akam. 255:1-2), (kaṭalip piḷantu cellum māperuṅ kappalkaḷ tamiḻakattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭami aṟiyappaṭum.)
kappalkaḷ taṅkum tuṟimukattic cērppu, koṇku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku.
kīḻ kaṭaṟkariyil koṟki, toṇṭi, pukār (kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam) eṉṉum tuṟinakarkaḷum, mēl kaṭaṟkariyil vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi eṉṉum tuṟinakarkaḷum iruntaṉa. iṭikaḻakak kālattil pāṇṭiyar tuṟinakar kumariyāṟṟiṉ kayavāyil (Estuary) amintiruntatu. ataṉ tamiḻp peyar (alivāyil?) maṟintu, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa kapāṭapuram eṉṉum vaṭacollē iṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkilirukkiṉṟatu.
iravil vaḻitappic cellum kalaṅkaṭku vaḻi kāṭṭuvataṟku, ov voru tuṟinakarilum kalaṅkari viḷakkam (Light house) iruntatu.
	ilaṅkunīr varippiṟ kalaṅkari viḷakkamum       (cilap. 6: 141)

	ēṇi cāttiya ēṟṟaruñ ceṉṉi
	viṇpora nivanta vēyā māṭattu
	iravil māṭṭiya ilaṅkucuṭar ñekiḻi
	uravunīr aḻuvat tōṭukalaṅ kariyum
	tuṟi.  				        (perumpāṇ.349-51)
(kaṭaṟkari, uḷnāṭṭi nōkka mikat tāḻnta maṭṭattilirup patāl, tuṟinakarkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.

iṉi, pataṉam eṉpatu, kalaṅkaḷ kāṟṟiṉālum koḷḷik kārarālum cētamiṉṟip pātukāppāyirukkumiṭam eṉṟumām.
pataṉam =    pātukāppu.
paṭṭiṉam eṉpatip paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. ik kālattiṟ paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ceṉṉiyic ciṟappāyk kuṟittal pōl, akkālattil paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu pukāric ciṟappāy kuṟittatu. paṭṭiṉappāli, paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷiyār eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.
oru tiṇikkum ciṟappāyuriyataṉṟi nakarap potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkum pati eṉṉum col, makkaḷ pativāy(niliyāy) irukkum iṭattik kuṟikkum. patital niliyāyk kuṭiyiruttal.
kappalkaḷ tuṟimukattiṟku vantu cērumpōtum ativiṭṭup puṟappaṭumpōtum, muracaṅkaḷ muḻakkappaṭṭaṉa.

paḻam pāṇṭināṭu, teṉpāli nāṭu mutal kumariyāṟu vari eḻunūṟu kātam (2001 kal) nīṇṭiruntatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ atiyiḻantapiṉ, piṟkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭākiya nelli maturi mukavi māvaṭṭap pakutiyi, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kippaṟṟi yāṇṭāṉ. itaṉ uṇmiyi,

eṉṉum mullikkalit taravālum (4),
aṅṅaṉamākiya nilak kuṟikkuc cōḻa nāṭṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēramāṉāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamumeṉṉu mivaṟṟi, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkākavāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār uriyālum (cilap. pak. 303), aṟika.
kumarikkaṇṭam muḻuki, vaṭanāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻ tiraviṭamākat tirintu vaṭanāṭu moḻipeyar tēyamāṉa piṉ tamiḻakam mikac curuṅki viṭṭatu. mutaṟkālattil pāṇṭi nāṭu aimmaṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, cōḻa nāṭu puṉal maṇṭalam (puṉṉāṭu) toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅ
kaḷākavum, cēranāṭa mali maṇṭalam koṅku maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭiruntatākak terikiṉṟatu.

toṇṭi maṇṭalam cōḻar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉatu pōṉṟē, koṅku maṇṭalamum cērar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu piṟkālattil nīṅki viṭṭatu. koṅku maṇṭalam mutaliṟ cēranāṭṭiṉ pakutiyāyiruntami, kollic cilampaṉ eṉṉum cēraṉ peyarālum, cērar kuṭiyiṉaṉāṉa atikamāṉ takaṭūri āṇṭatiṉālum, cērar koṅkuvi kāvūrnaṉṉāṭatil  eṉṟu aruṇakirinātar pāṭiyiruppatālum, vañcikkuk karuvūr eṉṉum peyaruṇmi
yālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
ovvoru nāṭum pala kōṭṭam allatu vaḷanāṭu eṉṉum perum pirivukaḷākavum, ovvoru perum pirivum pala kūṟṟam allatu nāṭu eṉṉum ciṟu pirivukaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. muttamiḻ nāṭum orē vēntaṉatu āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa piṟkālattil, ovvoru tamiḻ nāṭum oru peru maṇṭalamākak karutappaṭṭatu. in nilimi muṟkālattil illi. āṭcipaṟṟi munnāṭum vēṟupaṭṭavēṉum, moḻi paṟṟit tamiḻakam eṉa oṉṟupaṭṭē yiruntu vantaṉa. ovvoru kūṟṟamum atiyotta nāṭum pala taṉiyūrkaḷākavum pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ cērnta paṟṟukkaḷākavum pirikkap
paṭṭiruntaṉa. taṉiyūr ūrāṭciyum paṟṟu ūrāṭci yoṉṟiyamum ottaṉa.
ovvoru taṉiyūrilum allatu paṟṟilum, ūravi eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyāṭcik kuḻu iruntatu. atu āṇṭutōṟum tiruvuḷac cīṭṭup pōṉṟa kuṭavōliyāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭatu. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷum poṟuppaṟṟavarkaḷum atil iṭam peṟavilli. ūrāṭciyiṉ ovvoru tuṟiyiyum kavaṉikka, vāriyam eṉṉum uṭkuḻu amikkappaṭṭatu. avvāriyaṅkaḷ ēri vāriyam, kaliṅku vāriyam, kaḻaṉi vāriyam, tōṭṭa vāriyam, kuṭumpu vāriyam, poṉ vāriyam, kaṇakka vāriyam, kōyil vāriyam, taṭivaḻi (peruñcāli) vāriyam, pañcavāra vāriyam, āṭṭi vāriyam mutaliyaṉavākum. ūravi naṭapaṭikkikaḷiyum kaṇakkiyum eḻuti vikka ūrkkaṇakkaṉ eṉṉum aluvalaṉ iruntāṉ.
pañcavāra vāriyam varittaṇṭalik kavaṉippateṉṟum, piṟa vāriyaṅkaḷi mēṟpārppateṉṟum, palavāṟākak kūṟuvar. atu iyaṟkiyākavō ceyaṟkiyākavō uṇṭākum uṇavup pañcattit taṭukka ēṟpaṭṭatākavum irukkalām. vaḻakkuttīrttu muṟi ceytal āṭṭi vāriyattiṉ kaṭamiyākum. cÇik(civil) vaḻakku, kuṟṟa (Criminal) vaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭiyum kēṭṭu, kolit taṇṭam ceyyavum avvāriyattiṟku atikāramiruntatu. maṉṟupāṭu, taṇṭā, kuṟṟam eṉat taṇṭaṉi mūvakippaṭṭiruntatu.

kōṉ - (Turk.khan) (kāṉ) eṉpar kālṭuvel. kōṉ - ko.
mūvēntarum, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, uṟutic cuṟṟam eṉa mūvakippaṭṭa patiṉeṇkaṇat tuṇivaruṭaṉ, āṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
amiccar, pōṟṟiyar, paṭittalivar, tūtar oṟṟareṉpavar aimperuṅ kuḻuvār. amiccar araciyal viṉikaḷi amippavar allatu aracaṉukku neruṅkiyiruppavar. amital-neruṅkutal. pōṟṟiyar tamiḻap pūcāriyar. pirāmaṇar vantapiṉ, pōṟṟiyar iṭattil purōkitar amarttappaṭṭār. tūtaṉ eṉpatu muṉ celpavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉ col. tūtu - tūtaṉ. ivviru collum vaṭamoḻiyil tūta (duta) eṉṟu tiriyum.
 kaṇakkar, karumat talivar, poṉ cuṟṟattār allatu pokkaca cāliyar, vāyiṟ kāvalar, nakara māntar, paṭittalivar, kutiri maṟavar, yāṉi maṟavar eṉpavar eṇpērāyattār. itiṟ paṭit talivar eṉpār tērppaṭit tavirāyirukkalām.
āruyir naṇpar, antaṇar, camiyaṟkārar, maruttuvar, kaṇiyar eṉpavar uṟutic cuṟṟattār.
iṉi, aṟivālum akaviyālum mūtta periyōri aracar aṟivuriyāḷarāka amarttik koḷvatumuṇṭu. avar mutukaṇ eṉappaṭuvar.
ovvōr araciyal tuṟiyum tiṇikkaḷam (Department) eṉappaṭṭatu. araciṟit tiṇikkaḷat talivaṉ puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷa nāyakam eṉap
paṭṭāṉ. nilavari, toḻilvari, vaṇikap pōkkuvarattu vari, nallā nallerutu mutaliya iyaṅku tiṇivari, tirumaṇa vari (kaliyāṇak kāṇam) kāval vari mutaliya palavaki varikaḷ kuṭikaḷiṭam vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭukūṟu eṉpavaṉ nilat tīrviyit tiṭṭañ ceypavaṉ. variyilār eṉpavar varukiṉṟa varittekikaḷi vāṅkip pativu ceypavar. varik kūṟiṭuvār eṉpavar pativu ceyta varit tokikaḷip paṅkiṭupavar allatu pākupāṭu ceypavar. varip pottakam eṉpavaṉ nilavarik kaṇakkaṉ. puravu vari eṉpavaṉ piṟa varik kaṇakkaṉ. nilavari viḷivil āṟiloru paṅku kūlamākavum, piṟa varikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa paṇat tokiyākavum, vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ūraviyār avaṟṟi vāṅkit talinakariluḷḷa paṇṭārattiṟkum pokkaca cālikkum aṉuppi vantaṉar. nilavariyit tiṭṭañceyya nilaṅkaḷ ellām tulliyamāy aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa.
vēntaṉ atik kālattil ovvoru nāḷum pakalil mutal pattu nāḻiki koṭikkum, iṭippattu nāḻiki vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟi ceyvataṟkum, kaṭip pattu nāḻiki taṉ illaṟa iṉpattiṟkum celaviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṟiyappaṭum. eṭṭar eṉpavar, avaṉukku nāḻikiyaṟivippavar. akavar (cūtar) eṉpavar, niṉṟu koṇṭu avaṉ muṉṉōr perumiyik kūṟi avaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭupavar. ōvar (mākatar) eṉpavar, iruntu koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam pāṭupavar. ēttāḷar piṟa camiyaṅkaḷiṟ pukaḻntu pāṭupavar.
vēntaṉ taṉ kolu maṇṭapattiṟkuc cellum pōtum, aṅku amarntirukkum pōtum, atiṉiṉṟu ña1. moḻi  mīḷum pōtum, mey kāvalar pakkat tuṇiyāvar piṟa nāṭṭaracarum avar tūtarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum patiṉeṇ kaṇattārum tiṇikkaḷat tavirum nāṭṭup perumakkaḷum kūṭiya kolu maṇṭapattil, aracu kaṭṭil eṉṉum ariyaṇiyil vēntaṉ vīṟṟirukkum kolu olakkam eṉappaṭum. kālik kolu nāḷō lakkam eṉṟum, aritāyk kūṭum mālik  kolu allōlakkam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. koṭi vēḷi koṭi muracum muṟi vēḷi muṟi muracum aṟintu terivikkap peṟum.
vēntaṉ araciyaṟ paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭirukkum pōtellām, cila atikārikaḷ avaṉuṭaṉiruppar. avar uṭaṉ kūṭṭattār eṉappaṭuvar.
vēntaṉ avvappōtu iṭum kaṭṭaḷikaḷit tiruvāyk kēḷvi eṉpavar kēṭṭu vantu, eḻuttāḷariṭam aṟivippār. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷi eḻutupavar tirumantiravōli eṉpār. avarukkut talivarāyiruppavar tirumantiravōli nāyakam eṉappaṭuvar. nāḷtōṟum naṭappavaṟṟi nikaḻccik kuṟippil eḻuti vippavar paṭṭōlip perumāṉ. ūravi
kaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟa atikārikaḷiṭattiruntum varum olikaḷip paṭittup pārttu, avaṟṟiṟkut takka viṭiyaṉuppuvōr viṭiyilār eṉpōr. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷ ceyyappaṭum pottakam kēḷvi vari eṉappaṭum.
nāṭṭut talinakarilum kōṭṭat talinakarilum, nilap pativu ceyyum āvaṇak kaḷariyum aṟaṅkūṟa viyam eṉṉum vaḻakkut tīrppu maṉṟamum, iruntatākak terikiṉṟatu. aracar vaḻakkukkuḷiyum aṟaṅkūṟavi
yattāriṉ tavaṟāṉa tīrppukkaḷiyum, vēntaṉē kavaṉittu vantāṉ.
varip paṇamum putiyalum ciṟṟaracariṭum tiṟiyum tōṟṟuppōṉa pakiyaracar koṭukkum taṇṭamum, vēntaṉ varuvāykaḷākum.
paṇam, acciṭṭa kācākavum niṟip poṉṉākavum iruvakiyil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. akkam, kācu, kāṇam, poṉ, māṭi mutaliyaṉa kācu vakikaḷ. poṟkācukaḷiyum poṟ kaṭṭikaḷiyum nōṭṭañ ceyyum atikārikaḷ vaṇṇakkar eṉṉappaṭṭār. kācu - E.Cash.
kōnakarkkāvalum pāṭikāval eṉṉum ūrkkāvalum pōkkuvarattuc cālikkāvalum, iravum pakalum oḻuṅkāy naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
	nilaṉ akaḻ uḷiyar kalaṉ aciik koṭkum
	kaṇmā ṟāṭavar oṭukkam oṟṟi
	vayakkaḷiṟu pārkkum vayappuli pōlat
	tuñcāk kaṇṇar añcāk koḷkiyar
	aṟintōr pukaḻnta āṇmiyar ceṟinta
	nūlvaḻip piḻiyā nuṇuṅkunuṇ tērcci
	ūrkāp pāḷar ūkkaruṅ kaṇiyiṉar
	tērvaḻaṅku teruvil nīrtiraṇ ṭoḻuka
	maḻiyamin tuṟṟa arināḷ amayamum
	acivilar eḻuntu nayam vantu vaḻaṅkaliṉ
	kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkum kiyaṟu kaṅkulum
	accam aṟiyā tēmamākiya
	maṟṟi yāmam pakaluṟak kaḻippi
eṉṉum maturik kāñcip pakuti (641-53), maturiyil ūrkāvalar perumaḻi poḻinta naḷḷiravilum ūkkamāyc cuṟṟi vantu ūr kāttamiyit terivikkum.
marutanilat talinakarellām, akaḻiyālum palavakip poṟikaḷik koṇṭa kōṭṭi matilālum cūḻap peṟṟiruntaṉa. kirēkkarum urōmarum matil vāyil kāvalarāyiruntaṉar.
	kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
	aṭēlvāḷ yavaṉar 			      (cilap. 14:667)
kari pari tēr kāl eṉṉum nālvakip paṭikaḷ vēntaṉuk
kiruntaṉa. terinteṭukkappeṟṟa ciṟanta porunarik koṇṭa taṉip
paṭip pirivukaḷumiruntaṉa. avi terinta eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyāl vitantu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: uttama cōḻat terinta antaḷa kattāḷar   (S.I.I.ii,97)
antaḷakam meymmaṟi (kavacam)
iṉi, vēntaṉ mēl aḷaviṟanta paṟṟuṭiyavarāy, avaṉukkut tuṉpam nērnta vēḷiyil uyirikkoṭuttuk kākkavum uṭaṉ māyavum cūḷiṭṭuk koṇṭa pōr maṟavarumiruntaṉar. avar vēḷikkārar eṉappaṭṭār. avar uyir koṭuttal ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭaṉ kaḻittal eṉappaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ, allatu tuṟantapiṉ, allatu kaḻiperu mūppaṭintapiṉ, avaṉ mūtta makaṉ, mūtta makaṉ illāviṭṭāl iḷiya makaṉ. makaṉē illāviṭṭāl makaḷ, makaḷum illā viṭṭāl takutiyuḷḷa neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ, muṭicūṭṭappaṭṭār. taṭātaki yeṉṉum kayaṟkaṇṇiyār pāṇṭināṭṭi yāṇṭatiyum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār cēranāṭṭi yāṇṭatiyum kāṇka. muṭicūṭṭu viḻāvilum vēntaṉ piṟanta nāḷākiya veḷḷaṇi viḻāvilum, piṟa ciṟanta nikaḻccikaḷilum, kōppēruntēviyum uṭaṉ koluviruppāḷ.
kāviti eṉpatu ciṟanta amiccaṉukkum, ēṉāti eṉpatu ciṟanta paṭit talivaṉukkum, vēḷ allatu piḷḷi eṉpatu ciṟanta kuṟunila maṉṉarukkum, māvariyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟanta araciyaṟ karumat talivaṉukkum, eṭṭi eṉpatu ciṟanta vaṇikaṉukkum, ciṟutaṉam, peruntaṉam, talikkōl eṉpaṉa nāṭakak kaṇikiyarkkum, vēntaṉ aḷikkum paṭṭaṅkaḷākum.
ēṉi + ari = ēṉāri (yāṉikaḷi aḻippavaṉ) - ēṉāti. ta - ra oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu pōliyāka varum. e-ṭu: viti - viri, kural vaḷi - kutavaḷi(kocciyulaka vaḻakku). māvariyaṉ -māvariyam - mārāyam.
mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yāṉum                     (tol.puṟat.8)
araciyal viṉiñar ūtiyattiṟkuk kimmāṟākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu, nellum poṉṉumāyiṉ campaḷam eṉṟum, nilamāṉiya māyiṉ umpaḷam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
13. kalvi
kalvi oru kulattarkku maṭṭum eṉṟu variyaṟukkappaṭātu, ellāt toḻil vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. iti,
	tanti makaṟ kāṟṟum naṉṟi yaviyattu
	munti yiruppac ceyal.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷālum (67)
	īṉṟu puṟan tarutal eṉṟalik kaṭaṉē
	cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē
	vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
	oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
	kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷikkuk kaṭaṉē.	             (puṟam.312)
	vēṟṟumi terinta nāṟpā luḷḷum
	kīḻppāl oruvaṉ kaṟpiṉ
	mēṟpā loruvaṉum avaṉ kaṭ paṭumē.                    (puṟam.183)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭukkaḷālum, palvēṟu toḻilār kaṭikkaḻakak kālattup pulavarāyiruntamiyālum, aṟiyalām.
kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalic cāttaṉār, poṉvāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār, aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār, iḷam poṉ vaṇikaṉār, maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār, taṇkāṟpūṭ kollaṉār, olikkaṭik kaṇṇam pukuntārāyattaṉār, vaṭama vaṇṇakkaṉ peruñcāttaṉār mutaliya pulavar palvēṟu toḻilārā yiruntami kāṇka.
kalvi peṇṭirkku vilakkappaṭavilli. okkūr mācāttiyar, auviyār, kākki pāṭiṉiyār, nacceḷḷiyār, kāvaṟpeṇṭu, kuṟamakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi. nakkaṇṇiyār, pāri makaḷir, pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, pēymakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, māṟōkkattu nappacaliyār, veṇṇik 
kuyattiyār, veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār mutaliyōr peyar veḷivanta kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulattiyar. akkālattuk kuṭittaṉap peṇṭir tam kalviyaṟivi yellām illaṟattiṟkē payaṉpaṭuttiyamiyāl. avaruṭ perum pālār peyar veḷi varavilli. kārikkālammiyār ciṟanta pulattiyarāyiruntum, kaṇavarāl vilakkappaṭumvari, avar pāvaṉmi veḷippaṭātiruntami kāṇka. civappirakāca aṭikaḷ kālamāṉa patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tirukkāṭṭuppaḷḷiyil teruvil uppu viṟkum peṇ orutti, aṭikaḷ niṟiya vuḷatō eṉṟu pāṭi viṉaviya veṇpāviṟku viṭiyāka,
	teṉṉōṅku tillic civappirakācapperumāṉ
	poṉṉōṅku cēvaṭiyip pōṟṟiṉōm - aṉṉōṉ
	tirukkūṭṭam attaṉikkum teṇṭaṉiṭṭōm tīrāk
	karukkūṭṭam pōkkiṉōm kāṇ.
eṉṟu kaṭuttup pāṭiṉami kāṇka. kaṭuttal - virital.
pukaḻēntip pulavar kālamāṉa 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, uḻavaṉ, kollaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, taccaṉ, mañcikaṉ (muṭi tiruttāḷaṉ) mutaliya palvakit toḻilāḷarum ciṟanta ceyyuḷ ceyyum āṟṟal peṟṟirun
tamiyāl, paṇṭikkālak kalvip parappu aṟiyappaṭum, āṟalikkum kaḷvar kūṭa akkālattiṟ kaṟṟuvalla pāvalarāyirun tirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ikkālattiṟpōl kalvittuṟi eṉṉum araciyal tuṟi akkālattilli. makkaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil, āciriyaṉukkup poruḷ koṭuttum toṇṭu ceytum kaṟṟu vantaṉar.
	uṟṟuḻi yutaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum
	piṟṟinili muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟē.
eṉṟu āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka. (puṟam.183).
kaṟpippōr kaṇakkāyaṉ eṉṟum āciriyaṉ eṉṟum kuru allatu kuravaṉ eṉṟum mūvakiyar. kaṇakkāyaṉ eḻuttum ciṟṟilakkiyamum uriccollum(nikaṇṭum) kaṇakkum kaṟpippōṉ; āciriyaṉ, piṟkālattil aintāka virikkappaṭṭa mūvilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṟkeṭuttuk kāṭṭāṉa pērilakkiyamum kaṟpippōṉ; kuravaṉ camaya nūlum paṭṭāṅku (tattuva) nūlum kaṟpippōṉ.
iḷa māṇava māṇaviyarkkuk kaṇakki mikutiyākak kaṟpip pavaṉ kaṇakkāyaṉ.
aiyaṉ - āyaṉ = āciriyaṉ.
āyaṉ - āyāṉ - tanti, tamiyaṉ.
āyāṉ - āñāṉ = tanti.
āyāṉ - ācāṉ = mūttōṉ,āciriyaṉ,
ācāḷ = āciriyaṉ maṉivi,talivi.
ācu + iriyaṉ - āciriyaṉ = kuṟṟam nīṅkiyavaṉ allatu kuṟṟaṅ
keṭac colpavaṉ. ācu - kuṟṟam. irital -iriñaṉ = pakivaṉ. o.nō: kaliyaṉ - kaliñaṉ. āciriyarum nūlāciriyar. nuvalāciriyar, uriyāciriyar eṉa mūvakiyar. nuvalutal kaṟpittal. mutaliru kaḻaka nūlāciriyarum tam nūl
kaḷil akavaṟ pāviyē āṇṭamiyāl, ap pāviṟku nūṟpā, akaval, āciriyappā eṉṉum peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āciriyaṉ eṉṉum colli, ā - cārya (a-carya) eṉṟu pirittum tirittum vaṭacollākkuvar.
kuru = 	1. parumaṉ.
		2. kaṉam.
	pacumaṭ kurūut tiraḷ pōla 		              (puṟam.32).
		3. perumi (uri.ni.).
		4. aracaṉ (piṅ.)
		5. āciriyaṉ (cūṭā.).
		6. tanti.
kurumoḻi cirattil tāṅkiṉāṉ 	     (kāñcippu. irēṇu.11).
kuru - kurucil - kuricil = perumiyiṟ ciṟantōṉ, talivaṉ.
pōrmiku kurucil 			   (patiṟṟup.31:36).
pōrmiku poruna kuricil 			    (tirumuru.276).
kuru - kuruvaṉ = camaya vāciriyaṉ. o.nō.
ciṟu - ciṟuvaṉ.
vāṉōr kuruvaṉē pōṟṟi	(tiruvāca.5,68).
kuruvaṉ - kuravaṉ = periyōṉ. aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, tanti, tāy, tamiyaṉ eṉṉum aivarum aiṅkuravar eṉappaṭutaliyum;iru peṟṟōrum iru mutu kuravar eṉappaṭutaliyum nōkkuka.
kuru eṉṉum colliṟ kakaratti eṭuttolittum, kuravaṉ eṉṉum collil ataṉōṭu īṟu tirittum, vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. kurucil (kuricil) kuruvaṉ eṉṟa vaṭivukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
akkālattiṟ perumpālum ovvōr ūrilum kaṇakkāyar paḷḷi iruntatu.
	kaṇakkāyar illāta vūrum piṇakkaṟakkum
	mūttōri yillā avikkaḷaṉum - pāttuṇṇum
	taṉmi yilāḷar ayaliruppum immūṉṟum
	naṉmi payattal ila 			      (tirikaṭukam,11).
kaṇakkāyar paḷḷi piṟkālattil tiṇṇip paḷḷikkūṭamākac ciṟuttatu.
kaṇakkāyaṉiṭam kaṟpōṉukku māṇi, caṭṭaṉ, olik kaṇakkaṉ  eṉṟum; āciriyaṉiṭam kaṟpōṉukku māṇavaṉ, kaṟṟuccolli, maḻapulavaṉ eṉṟum,kuravaṉiṭam kaṟpōṉukkuk kēṭpōṉ eṉṟum; peyar.
maṇi eṉpatu ciṟumi allatu kuṟumip poruḷtarum muṉṉoṭṭu. maṇikkuṭal, maṇittakkāḷi, maṇippuṟā eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
maṇi - māṇi = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟpōṉ.
caṭṭam = eḻutum ōli. caṭṭam - caṭṭaṉ = oliccuvaṭi paṭippōṉ. caṭṭanampi = tiṇṇippaḷḷi māṇavar talivaṉ. caṭṭanampip piḷḷi - caṭṭām piḷḷi.
olik kaṇakkar oliyaṭaṅku puṉcekkar 
eṉpatu nālaṭiyār(397).
oru caṭṭaṉi oru caṭṭaṉ piḻikkap pēcuvāṉākil eṉpatu kalveṭṭu (T.A.S.I.i.9)
caṭṭaṉ eṉṉum collic cātra (chatra) eṉṟu tirittu, ataṉiyē teṉcoṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar!
māṇi - māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ - māṇākkaṉ. māṇavaṉ māṇava(va.). māṇavakaṉ - māṇavaka(va.). māṇi - māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illi. māṇavam eṉpatu kalvi.
kaṟkappaṭum ēṭṭuk kaṟṟikaḷ, ēṭu. cuvaṭi, pottakam, paṉuval, nūl eṉap palapeyar peṟum. muṉ mūṉṟum olik kaṟṟiyic ciṟappākavum, piṉṉiraṇṭum uṭporuḷic ciṟappākavum, kuṟikkum.
pottakam eṉpatē paḻiya vaṭivam.
niṟi nūṟ pottakam neṭumaṇi yēṟṟi
(peruṅ. uñcik.34,26)
varineṭum pottakattu	                (kōyil nāṉmaṇi māli)
varip pottakam (T.A. S.I.i,166)
pottutal cērttal. cuvaṭi cērttal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
pottakam - pustaka (va) - pustak(i). paṉuval pāṭal (pirapantam). nūl eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇamum aṟivayalum.
kaṟkum iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷi (tiv. periyati. 2,3,8) eṉṟum, ōtum paḷḷi (tivā) eṉṟum, kallūri (cīvaka.995) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
paḷḷi = paṭukki, paṭukkiyaṟi, paṭukkum vīṭu. vīṭu pōṉṟa kōyil allatu maṭam, kalvi kaṟkum maṭam, kalvikkūṭam paḷḷi-palli (va.).
paḷ = paḷḷam, tāḻvu, tāḻvākak kiṭattal,tūṅkutal.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭi, paṭukki.
kaṟṟōrkkuc ceṉṟaviṭamellām ciṟappiruntatu. vēntarip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pulavarkkuc ciṟanta paricum muṟṟūṭṭum (carva māṉiyamum) aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pāṇṭiyar taliciṟanta pulavari yellāṅkūṭṭi, tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuvip pōṟṟiṉar.
pulavariṉ pāṭalkaḷum nūlkaḷum, pāṇṭiyar tamiḻk kaḻakattil kuṟṟamaṟṟaviyeṉa oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭāloḻiya, ulakil vaḻaṅkā. aṅṅaṉam oppam peṟutal araṅkēṟṟam eṉappaṭṭatu.
nāṭakak kaṇikiyariṉ āṭal pāṭalkaḷum vēntar muṉṉiliyilēyē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. araṅkēṟiya kaṇikiyar talikkōṟpaṭṭamum āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu paricamum peṟṟaṉar.
