14.kalikaḷ
payiṟciyāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa kalikaḷ.
(1) ici
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar nuṇmāṇnuḻi pulattarāyum taliciṟanta nākarikarāyum eḵkuc ceviyarāyu miruntatiṉāl,ēḻu pēriciyum aintu ciṟṟiciyumākiya paṉṉīriciyi (curatti)yum kaṇṭu āyappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil eḻupālip paṇkaḷit tirittatumaṉṟi, appaṉṉīriciyiyum vaṭṭappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 24 ākavum, tirikōṇappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 48 ākavum caturappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 96 ākavum, nuṭpamāyp pakuttu elliyaṟṟa icip pēriṉpatti nukarntirukkiṉaṟaṉar. paṉṉīricikku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇṇiciyiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟum nūlē ici nuṇukkam nghY«! ēḻiciyum kural, tuttam, kikkaḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟṟici ākaṇam eṉṟum, kuralum iḷiyumallāta pērici antaram eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
tiri, caturam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkappeṟum. catur eṉpatu, nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup poruḷilaṉṟi, nāṟkōṇam eṉṉum vaṭivupporuḷil vaṭamoḻi yililli.
akanili marutam, puṟanili marutam, arukiyal marutam, perukiyal marutam eṉṉum paṇ vakuppum, kiḻami niṟikuṟi eṉpaṉavum, tamiḻariṉ ici nuṇukkattik kāṭṭum.
ikkālattu āriya iciyaṟiñar, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ aṟivu nuṇukkatti ōrātu, tampōl avariyuṅ karuti 96 icikaḷ pāṭaṟkiyalātavi eṉpar.
icik karuvikaḷ tōl, tuḷi, narampu, veṇkalam (kañcalam) eṉa nālvakip paṭṭiruntaṉa.
tōṟ karuvikaḷ āṭal muḻā, pāṭal muḻā, potu muḻā eṉṟum; akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇami muḻavu, nāḷ muḻavu, kāli muḻavu, eṉṟum; maṇappaṟi, piṇappaṟi eṉṟum; vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. paṟi eṉpatu tōṟkaruvip potup peyar. teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi(tol.964).
iṉṉicit tuḷik karuvikaḷ pullāṅkuḻal, icik kuḻal (nātacuram), mukavīṇi eṉṉum ēḻicik kuḻalkaḷākavum; ottu (ūmik kuḻal), eṉṉum ōricik kuḻalākavum; iruvakip paṭṭiruntaṉa. tāri, ciṉṉam, vāṅkā, kompu, caṅku mutaliya oricit tuḷik karuvikaḷ, ūrvalam pōr mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷil āravāra icik karuvikaḷāyp payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. makuṭi eṉpatu nalla pāmpi mayakkutaṟkeṉṟē amikkappaṭṭa ēḻicic ciṟappiṉṉicik kuḻalākum.
narappuk karuvikaḷ oru narampuḷḷa curiyāḻiliruntu āyiram narampukaḷ peruṅkalam (ātiyāḻ) vari, palvēṟu vakip paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅ kōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum nāṉkum ciṟantavi. avaṟṟuḷḷum taliciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ. atuvē piṉṉar vīṇiyākat tirintatu. āyiram narampeṉpatu, āyiraṅkāṟ pūcci eṉpatiṟpōl, narampiṉ peruntokiyiyē kuṟikkum.
``iṉṉici vīṇiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl eṉṉum tiruvācakak kūṟṟu (369) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇiyeṉṟu ciṟappup peyar peṟṟamiyiyē kāṭṭum. ellā narappukruvikaḷum yāḻē. vīṇi eṉṉum peyarum viṇ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉ collē. atu vaṭamoḻiyil vīṇā eṉṟu tiriyum. viṇṇeṉal narampu teṟittolittal. viṇ - vīṇi mutukuṉṟam (paḻamali) viruttācala meṉṟa peyar māṟiyatāl vēṟu nakaramākiviṭātu. aṅṅaṉamē vīṇiyeṉap paṭum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum eṉka.
iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cilappatikārattil nārataṉ vīṇi nayanteri pāṭalum eṉṟum, 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa appar tēvārattil mācil vīṇiyum eṉṟum, varutalāl, vīṇi11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpatu poruntātu. vētakāla nāratar tamiḻnāṭu vantē icikaṟṟup pañcapāra tīyam eṉṉum icit tamiḻ nūli iyaṟṟiṉār. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cīvakacintāmaṇik kāntaruvatattiyilampakattil, yāḻeṉṟum vīṇiyeṉṟum orē karuvi kuṟikkappaṭuvatāl, yāḻ vēṟu vīṇi vēṟu alla.  ikkālattil curiyāḻum vīṇiyeṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tiruñāṉa campantar pāṭiya yāḻmurip paṇṇiṉ iyalpu iṉṟu evarukkum teriyāmiyāl, yāḻmuri vīṇikkamiyum eṉpatum poruntātu.
tāḷak karuvi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyapṭṭatiṉāl, atu veṇkalak karuviyeṉappaṭṭatu. cal allatu callari eṉṉum ōciyuṭimiyāl tāḷak karuviyiṟ periyatu callariyeṉṉum ciṟiyatu cālar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kural eṉpatu ma eṉṉum 5-ām iciyeṉṟum, oru narampil orēyici yeḻūum narappuk karuviyē tamiḻaratu eṉṟum, kūṟuvār tamiḻarāyiṉum icit tamiḻ aṭippaṭiyē aṟiyātār āvar. ``kural mutalēḻum'' eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap.5: 35), ``mutaṟṟāṉa mākiya kuralilē eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟu uriyāciriyarum (puṟam.11), mūvēḻ tuṟiyum muṟiyuḷik kaḻippi eṉṟu vaṉparaṇarum (puṟam.152), kūṟiyiruttaliyum, avar kavaṉittilar.
kural eṉṉum collē, iyalpāṉa kuralākiya mutaliciyiyē kuṟikkum. curiyāḻum peruṅkalamumē vilyāḻ (Harp)nghš meṭṭiṉṟi ōricik koru narampu koṇṭavi. pēyāḻ mutaliya piṟayāḻellām meṭṭukkaḷōṭukūṭi, orē narampil palaviciyicik kakkūṭiya paṇmoḻi narampukaḷ koṇṭaṉavākavē terikiṉṟaṉa.
paṇkaḷi, ēḻiciyumuḷḷavi paṇ eṉṟum, āṟici yuḷḷavi paṇṇiyal eṉṟum, aiyiciyuḷḷavi tiṟam eṉṟum, nāliciyuḷḷavi tiṟattiṟam eṉṟum, nālvakiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar.  innālvakip paṇkaḷum mottam 11,991 eṉak kaṇikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.

(2) nāṭakam
nāṭakam eṉpatu teṉcollē. naḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal. naḷ - naḷi eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu. naḷital ottal.naḷi - naṭi o.nō: kaḷiṟu - kaṭiṟu. naṭittal eṉpatu, iṉṉoruvaṉip pōl allatu illātati uḷḷatu pōlac ceytu kāṭṭutal.
naṭi - naṭam - naṭṭam - nrutta (va.)
o.nō: vaṭṭam - vrutta (va.)
naṭṭam - naṭṭaṇam, naṭṭaṇi
naṭṭam - naṭṭa(pirā)
o.nō: vaṭṭam - vaṭṭaṇam, vaṭṭaṇi
naṭṭaṇam - narttaṉa (va.)
naṭṭam - naṭṭuvaṉ. o.nō: kuṭṭam - kuṭṭuvaṉ
naṭi - naṭaṉam. o.nō: paṭi - paṭaṉam = paṭippu
naṭi - nāṭakam. o.nō: paṭi - pāṭakam = pātattiṟ paṭinta kiṭakkum aṇi.
muttamiḻ toṉṟutoṭṭa iyalici nāṭakam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkum.
nāṭakak kali kūttu, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kutittāṭuvatu kūttu. atu vēttiyal, potuviyal; ulakiyal, tēviyal; vacik kūttu, pukaḻk kūttu; varikkūttu, variyamitik kūttu (variccāntik kūttu); amitik kūttu (cāntik kūttu), vēṭikkik kūttu(vinōtak kūttu); akak kūttu, puṟak kūttu, viḷiyāṭṭuk kūttu, viṉik kūttu; veṟṟikkūttu, tōlvik kūttu; eṉappalvēṟu vakiyil ivviruvakippaṭum.
naṭaṉam allatu naṭam eṉpatu, aḻakuṟa āṭuvatu.  atu nūṟṟeṭṭu uṭaṟkaraṇaṅkaḷōṭum kikāl kaṇvāy mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷōṭum kūṭiyatu. kiviṉikaḷ eḻiṟki toḻiṟki, poruṭki eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭu,piṇṭi allatu iṇiyā viṉikki yeṉappaṭum oṟṟikki vaṇṇam muppattu mūṉṟum, piṇiyal allatu iṇikki yeṉappaṭum iraṭṭikki vaṇṇam patiṉintum, koṇṭaṉavākum.
naṭam naṭaṉam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil ,naṭṭa naṭṭaṉa eṉṟu valikkum. naṭṭa eṉpatiṉiṉṟum nāṭya eṉṉum coṟ piṟakkum.
naṭi eṉṉum mutaṉili vaṭamoḻiyil illi. nirutta eṉṉum colliṉ nrut eṉṉum aṭiyiyē mutaṉiliyāka āḷvar.
tamiḻ naṭaṉam iṉṟu parata nāṭṭiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. parata cāttiram vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu  ki.mu 4ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭākum. ataṟkum muntiyatu tamiḻp paratamē yeṉpati, nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiya mutalāvuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷu miṟantaṉa, eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāravurip pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟintu koḷka.
nāṭakam eṉpatu kati taḻuvivarum kūttu. atu poruḷ, kati (yōṉi), talimi (virutti), nili (canti), cuvi, vakuppu (cāti), kuṟippu, viṟal (cattuvam), naḷinayam (apinayam), col, colvaki, vaṇṇam, vari,cētam eṉṉum patiṉāṉ kuṟuppukkaḷiyuṭiyatu.
nāṭaka araṅku nalla keṭṭi nilattil, īraṭi nīḷamuḷḷakōlāl, eṇkōl nīḷamum eḻukōl akalamum orukōl uyaramum uḷḷatāka amikkappaṭṭu,mēlē mukaṭum, orumuka veḻiṉi porumuka veḻiṉi karantuvara leḻiṉi eṉṉum mūvakit ticikaḷum, pukuvāyil puṟappaṭa
vāyil (Exit) eṉṉum iruvāyilkaḷum, uṭiyatāyiruntatu.
tamiḻ nāṭakamellām icippaṭṭuḷḷaviyē (Operas).
nāṭakam eṉpatu nāṭṭakka eṉṟum, araṅku araṅkam eṉpaṉa raṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
ar - araṅku = aṟi, araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭaka mēṭi, nāṭakacāli, viḷiyāṭiṭam, paṭikkalam payilumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, āṟṟiṭik kuṟi, tiruvaraṅkam.
ici nāṭakam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ virivi eṉ muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
(3) maṭinūl:
maṭi = cōṟu, uṇavu. maṭinūl camiyal nūl.
kantukak karuttum maṭinūṟ ceytiyum 	            (maṇi.2:22)
eṉpatu tamiḻil maṭa nūliruntamiyit terivikkum.
	kāveri yūṭṭiya kavarkaṇit tūṇip
	pūviri kaccip pukaḻōṉ taṉmuṉ
	paṉivari mārpaṉ payanta nuṇporuṭ
	paṉuvaliṉ vaḻāap palvē ṟaṭicil
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi(238-41) kūṟum vīmaṉ maṭinūl, tamiḻ maṭi nūlit taḻuviyatēyeṉpatu ataṉ piṉmiyālum, avaṉ pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyāṉa tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉāyiruntamiyālum. aṟiyappaṭum. naḷaṉum tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉē.
(4) maruttuvam.
tamiḻ maruttuvak kali cittarāl vaḷarkkappeṟṟatu. ataṉāl atu citta maruttuvam eṉappeṟum. kaṭṭikaḷiyum piḷavikaḷiyum karippatum,oṭinta elumpi oṭṭavippatum, mutiyavari iḷiñarākkuvatum, nūṟṟuk
kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻvippatum, cittamaruttuvam.
	mikiṉum kuṟiyiṉum nōyceyyum nūlōr
	vaḷimutalā eṇṉiya mūṉṟu. 		           (kuṟaḷ.941)
ātalāl, ūti (vātam) pittam kōḻi eṉṉum munnāṭiyiyum nāṭi ataṉāl, iṟṟik karuvikaḷik koṇṭu taliciṟanta tērcci peṟṟa mēli maruttuvarum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāta,
	nōynāṭi nōymutal nāṭi yatutaṇikkum
	vāynāṭi vāyppac ceyal 		           (kuṟaḷ.648)
citta maruttuvaṉiṉ teyvattiṟamām.
nuḷ - naḷ - nāḷam - nāḷi - nāḻi - nāṭi.
nāṭi pārkkum tiṟamillātāṉ maruntaṉē yaṉṟi maruttuva ṉākāṉ. intiya maruttuvam cittamaruttuvamē. āyurvētam eṉṉum āriya maruttuvam, citta maruttuvattiṉ vaṭanāṭṭuvakiyē. cēra vēntar iruntavari citta maruttuvamāyirunta maliyāḷa maruttuvam, iṉṟu āriya maruttuvamāy māṟiyiruttal kāṇka. orē nōykkup pala maruntukaḷ uḷa. maruntukaḷ iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭum, āyiṉ,maruttuvamuṟi oṉṟē. āriya maruttuvam vērkaḷi mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, citta maruttuvam centūratti mikutiyākakkoṇṭa teṉṟum, cilar kūṟuvar. avar aṟiyār. "vērpār, taḻipār, mella mellac centūrac cuṇṇam pār.''  eṉpatu cittamaruttuvap paḻamoḻiyākum. āyiram pērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ. eṉpatu āyiram vērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ciṟupañcamūlam, perumpañcamūlam eṉṉum īrintu vērkaḷum, tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē viḷiyum tamiḻ maruntuc carakkām. maruntu eṉṉum peyarē maṇamuḷḷa vēriyum taḻiyiyum tāṉ kuṟikkum. maru = maṇam. maru - maruntu.
citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟanta maruntu, mūvaki uppuc cērnta muppu eṉpatākum. atu irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟip poṉṉākkavum ōrilakkam āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻac ceyyavum vallateṉa, ataṉ āṟṟali uyarvu
naviṟciyākak kūṟuvar.
neṭu nāḷirunta pērum, niliyākavēyiṉum kāyakaṟpantēṭi neñcu puṇṇāvar. eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar kūṟiyatu, mūppi nōkkittāṉ pōlum!
aṟuvi (Surgery) muṟiyum cittamaruttuvattiliruntami
	mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
	ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
	neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ
	ampucēr uṭampiṉar 			       (patiṟ.42:2-5)
(kayam mūḻkic ciral peyarntaṉṉa neṭaveḷḷūci = kuḷattilē muḻu ciccilik kuruvi eḻukiṉṟakālattu ataṉ vāyalakippōla, puṇkaḷi nūlāltikkum pōtu appuṇṇiṉ arattattil maṟinteḻukiṉṟa nīṇṭa veḷḷiyāṉa ūci. neṭuvaci = nīṇṭa ūcittaḻumpu. vaṭu = kāyppu.)
eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattaṭikaḷālum,
	uṭaliṭit tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉṟi yaṟuttataṉ utira mūṟṟic
	cuṭaluṟac cuṭṭuvēṟōr maruntaṉāl tuyaram tīrvar 									(kamparā.kumpa.146)
	ārār talivaṇaṅkār ārārtam kiyeṭār
	ārārtām cattirattil āṟātār - cīrārum
	teṉpuliyūr mēvum civaṉaruḷcēr ampaṭṭat
	tampipukāṉ vācalilē tāṉ.
eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭukkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
maruttuvattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi yaṟintē, paṇṭik kālattil ūrmaruttuvaṉukku iṟiyili nilam māṉiyamāka viṭappaṭṭatu. atu
maruttuvappēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. (S.I.1,ii.43).
ulakiṟ ciṟanta cittamaruttuvam, ūkkuvāriṉṟi nāḷukku nāḷ maṟintum kuṟintum varukiṉṟatu.
kuḻanti maruttuvam, pēṟukāla maruttuvam, aracamaruttuvam (vilakkamaṟṟatu), nañcu maruttuvam. māṭṭu maruttuvam eṉpaṉa, citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷākum. vilakkam pattiyam.
nīr,karukku (kacāyam) kuḻampu,ney allatu eṇṇey (kirutam) kaḷimpu, meḻuku (lēkiyam), kuḷiki, nīṟu (paspam) mutaliya pala vaṭivilum citta maruntukaḷ uṇṭu. ovvoru nōykkum ovvoru puṟatti uracum māttirik kaṭṭikaḷum uḷa.
kuḷiki - kuḷikā(va.)centūḷ - centūḷam - centūram -cintūram - sintūra(va.)
maruttuvaṉukkup paṇṭitaṉ eṉṟum, maruttuvattiṟkup paṇṭitam eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. maruntu koṭuppavaṉ maruttuvaṉ; palporuḷ
kaḷi aṟintavaṉ gṇṭitaṉ.g©oj‹ eṉpatu paṇṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, paṇṭitam eṉpatu paṇṭuvam eṉṟum,maruvum.
paṇṭuvam (Medical Treatment) maruttuvap paṇṭuvam eṉṟum nampikkip paṇṭuvam (Faith cure) eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭum. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉukkuk kaṭivāyil pāmpuṇik karuṅkal vippatum, erukkam pūvit tiṉṉak koṭuppatum, mūkkiṟ pacciliccāṟu piḻivatum, maruttuvap paṇṭuvam; mantirattiṉāl nañciyiṟakkuvatu nampikkip paṇṭuvam.
nampikkip paṇṭuvam, iṟumpūtu (Miracle), nērttikkaṭaṉ; kuḷicam (Amulet), maṇi, pārvi,mantiram, ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), pāṇippu (pāvakam), muṭṭi, aracak kāṭci mutaliyaṉavākap palavakippaṭum.
iyēcu perumāṉ toḻu (kuṭṭa) nōyāḷiyit toṭṭu nalappaṭuttiyatum, tiruñāṉa campantar kūṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ curanōyit tīrttatum. iṟampūtu  vaḻipaṭuteyvattinōkki, oṉṟu paṭippatāka allatu ceyvatāka nērntu koḷvatu nērttikkaṭaṉ, mantira eḻuttuḷḷa takaṭṭik kiyiṟ kaṭṭik koḷvatu kuḷicam. uruttirākkam, tuḻāy (tuḷaci) maṇi, muttumāli mutaliya aṇikaḷ maṇiyākum. mantirikaṉ tēṭkoṭṭup paṭṭavaṉi allatu pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭavaṉip pārttuk kuḻiyaṭittu mantirippatu pārvi. nañcēṟiyavaṉē mantiratti ōtuvatu mantiram pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭavaṉ kaluḻaṉi (karuṭaṉi) uḷḷuvatu ūḻkam. avaṉ taṉṉik kaluḻaṉākavē karutuvatu pāṇippu.
tiṭaṅkoḷ mantiram tiyāṉapā vakanili muṭṭi      
(periya pu.34:1060)
maṇimantiramātiyāl vēṇṭu cittikaḷ  (tāyumā.paripū.9)
cēramāṉ karuvūrēṟiya oḷvāṭ kōpperuñ cēra lirum poṟiyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, nariverūuttaliyār nalluṭampu peṟṟatāka, 5-ām puṟappāṭṭiṉ koḷukkūṟum, itu, kaṇṭa māliyi (Scrofula) aracaṉ toṭiṉ kuṇamākumeṉṟu karuti ataṟku `aracaṉ tīṅku' (King’s evil) eṉṟu āṅkilēyar peyariṭṭa taṉōṭu oppu nōkkattakkatu.
(5)maṇinōṭṭam (irattiṉapparīṭci)
	kāka pātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum
	ēkiyum nīṅki iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
	nūlavar noṭitta nuḻinuṇ kōṭi 
	nālvaki varuṇattu nalaṅkēḻ oḷiyavum 
	ēkiyuma māliyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
	pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyavum
	vitimuṟi piḻiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum
	pūca uruviṉ polanteḷit taṉiyavum
	tītaṟu katiroḷit teṇmakaṭ ṭuruvavum
	iruḷteḷit taṉiyavum iruvē ruruvavum
	orumit tōṟṟat tivēṟu vaṉappiṉ
	ilaṅkunīr vīṭūum nalaṅkeḻu maṇikaḷum
	kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum
	tōṟṟiya kuṟṟam tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum
	cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa
	vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum
	karuppat tuḷiyavum kalliṭi muṭaṅkalum
	tirukku nīṅkiya ceṅkoṭi valliyum
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakuti (14:180-198), maṇikaḷiṉ kuṇaṅ kuṟṟaṅ
kaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
6. ōviyam
ōviyac cennūl urinūṟ kiṭakkiyum
eṉpatu (maṇi 2:32), ōviyanūlit terivikkum.
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyirat toṭarum, cuvari vittukkoṇṭaṉṟō cittirameḻuta vēṇṭum? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, māṭaccuvarkaḷilellām akkālattil ōviyam variyap peṟṟiruntamiyi aṟivikkum.
	vampa mākkaḷ kampali mūtūrc
	cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunili maṉitoṟum
	miyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
	evvaki uyirkaḷum uvamam cāṭṭi
	veṇcuti viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
	kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
eṉṟu maṇimēkali (3:126-131) kūṟutal kāṇka.
oruvaṉ ōr aracaṉatu avikkaḷam ceṉṟu aṅku aracaṉ taṉ amiccaruṭaṉamarntiruntatik kaṇṭu,tāṉ koṇṭuvanta kāṇikkiyi nīṭṭa aracaṉ ati vāṅkāmiyāl neruṅkic ceṉṟu atu ōr ōviyamāyirukkak kaṇṭāṉeṉṟu, oru kati vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. attakiya ōviyattiṟavōr akkālattiruntaṉar.
ōviyakkāririk kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟum (5:30). āṭi aṇikalam kaṭṭiṭam mutaliya ellāp poruḷum ōviya vēlippaṭuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntaṉa.
(7) uruvam (Sculpture)
maṇṇāl uruvañ ceypavar maṇṇīṭṭāḷar         (maṇi.28:37)
marattālum kallālum poṉvakiyālum uruvañceypavar
kammiyar; cāntiṉāṟ ceypavar kottar
kammiyanūl tolvaram pelli kaṇṭu       
(tiruviḷi, tirunakaṅ.38)
kōyil tērum kōpuramum uruvaṅkaḷ niṟintavi. pāvi
yuruvamum pūtappaṭimiyum pukārilum piṟanakarkaḷilum iruntaṉa.
(8) kaṭṭiṭam
māṭamāḷikikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum maṇi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, mūvēntar talinakarkaḷilum kōnakarkaḷilum mikuntiruntaṉa.
māḷiki, kōpuram, maṇi, maṇṭapam eṉṉum nāṟcollum teṉcollē.
mālutal māṇpuṟutal.
māṉṟa pūṇmuliyiṉāḷ(kāñcippu. tirukkaṇ 174). māl perumi. māl - (māḷ) māṇ - māṇpu, māṭci - māḷ - māḷiki -mālikkā (va.) = māṭcimippaṭṭa maṉi.
kō. = aracu, talimi. puram = uyarvu, uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam.
puri = uyarcci. puriuyar pākum (tol.uri.4). vēntaṉ irunta uyarnta eḻunilik kaṭṭiṭam mutaliṟ kōpuram eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpu atip pōṟ kōyilil amanta eḻunili vāṉaḷāvi appeyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ amippu tēri ottatākum.
kōpuram uḷḷa nakarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē, mutalil puram eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: kāñcipuram, kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻapuram.
vēntaṉ taṉ talinakari nāṟpuṟamumnōkkavum, toli viṟpakivar varavik kāṇavum, pakivar muṟṟukiyiṭṭu uḻiñip
pōri naṭattuṅkatāl noccip pōrik kaṇ kāṇikkavum, avaṉ araṇmaṉiyiṉ mēl eḻunilikoṇṭa ōr uyarnta tēr pōṉṟa kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. atu puram, eṉappaṭṭatu. puram = uyarnta kaṭṭiṭamāṉa mēṉmāṭam. puravi = uyarnta cuvarit tāṇṭum kutiri.
puram eṉpatu, piṉpu purattik koṇṭa araṇmaṉiyiyum ataṉ cūḻaliyum (Acropolis) kuṟittu, ataṉ piṉ, nakar eṉṉum coṟpōl talinakar muḻuvatiyum kuṟittu, nāḷaṭivil nakarap potup
peyarāyiṟṟu.
araṇmaṉiyiluḷḷa puram aracaṉ irukkiyātalāl, kōpuram eṉappaṭṭatu. kō aracaṉ. kō irunta il kōyil eṉappaṭṭati nōkkuka.
pakivar varavu kāṇṭaṟkuk kōpuram ciṟanta amippeṉṟu kaṇṭapiṉ, nakaric cūḻnta kōṭṭi matililum, vāyiliṟ peritākavum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟiyaṉavākavum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟiyaṉa kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
maturi nakaric cūḻnta matiliṉ nāṟpuṟattilum, vāyilum māṭamum vāṉaḷāviya kōpuramum iruntaṉa. nāṉku vāyil māṭaṅkaḷ irunta
tiṉāl, maturi nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. kūṭal eṉpatu tamiḻk kaḻakam. atu piṉpu iṭavaṉāku peyarāy maturiyik kuṟittatu. itiyaṟiyātu, tolkatiñar (purāṇikar) oru katiyik kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal taṇkōl vēntē 	         (puṟam.58:13)

eṉṟu maturikkāñci (355-6) kūṟutal kāṇka.

aracaṉukkuriya ciṟappukkaḷellām iṟivaṉukkum ceyyap peṟṟa
tiṉāl, kōyiltēr mikap peritāyc ceyyappeṟṟatupōl, kōyil matiṟ kōpuramum mikapperiya eḻunili vāṉaḷāviyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟatu. ataṉ amippum tēri ottatākum. ataṉ eḻunilikaḷum tēriṉ eḻutaṭṭukkaḷip pōṉṟavi. eḻunili allatu eḻutaṭṭuk karuttu ēḻulakam eṉṉum karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. ēḻulakak karuttum eḻutīvuk karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum.
ēḻuṭiyāṉ poḻil (tirukkōvi. 7)
taccuk kaliyil kōyiltērpōl, kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil kōyiṟ
kōpuram paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ aṟiviyum āṟṟaliyum ciṟappakkāṭṭum. tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyiṟ kōpurattiṟku, 10 kal tolivuluḷḷa cārappaḷḷam eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu cāram kaṭṭiyatākavum, kaṭikālil oḻukiya nīrttuḷikaḷi aṭittaṟkuk kuṟavi mīṉkaḷip piṭittu viṭṭatākavum, kūṟuvar.

yiṉiṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu maḷkutal eṉṉum viṉiyi ṉiṉiṉṟum, tōṉṟiyavi yākum. aḻaku eṉpatu ivviraṇṭi ṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya vaḻipporuḷ.

kaḷum iruntaṉa.

māṭa maturi (puṟam;32), māṭamali maṟukiṟ kūṭal (muruku.71), māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭal (maturi.429) nāṉ māṭak kūṭal makaḷirum mintarum (kalit.92, matimali purici māṭak kūṭal (tirumukap paracuram).
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīrik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ celuttutaṟku, karantupaṭi eṉṉum puticālakam iruntatu. atu terunaṭuviṟ kaṭṭap
paṭṭu yāṉik kūṭṭam mēṟcellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭap
paṭṭiruntatu. atiṟceṉṟa nīr, yāṉit tutikki pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.

eṉṉum cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷkaṭku (1:101-4)
poṟkoṭikaḷ patākiyōṭu koḻikkum tiruntumatil, māṟṟavar maṟappaṭi akaḻik kaṭantu taṉṉip paṟṟiṉ, attevvartali paṉikkumpaṭi nūṟṟuvarikkolli mutal maraniliyīṟāka vuḷḷaviyum, cempurukukaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟi umiḻvaṉavāka yavaṉar tāṭpaṭutta poṟikaḷum, paṉṟi
mutal nuṇṇūlīṟāka vuḷḷaviyum, tiruntiṟṟeṉka. eṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyar uri varintuḷḷapaṭi, 103-ām ceyyuḷiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa poṟikaḷiyē yavaṉar ceytatākat tiruttakka tēvar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avi piṟavaṟṟip pōl attuṇic ciṟantaṉa valla vātalālum, tiruttakka tēvarkku ēḻu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muntiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ aṅṅaṉam kūṟāmiyāṉum, cilappatikāram pōl cintāmaṇi uṇmik katiyit taḻuvāmiyāṉum, tamiḻar kirēkkarukku muṟpaṭṭa iṉat
tārātalālum, mēli maruttuvatti mēliyariṭam kaṟṟa kīḻiyar mēli nāṭukaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟuvatu pōl yavaṉar cila poṟikaḷ ceytirukkalāmātalālum, iṟṟi vāṉūrtik koppāṉa mayiṟ poṟiyi yavaṉar ceytatākat tēvar kūṟāmiyāṉum, cintāmaṇik kūṟṟu koḷḷat takkataṉ ṟeṉṟum, ataṉāl paṇṭit tamiḻap poṟiviṉik kammiyarkku iḻukkilliyeṉṟum, kūṟiviṭukka.
(10) poṉnūl
	cāta rūpam kiḷicciṟi āṭakam
	cāmpū natameṉa ōṅkiya koḷki
eṉpatu (cilap. 14: 201-2), poṉṉiṉ vakikaḷik kāṭṭum.
jāta rūppa eṉṉum vaṭacol polinta vaṭivam eṉṟum, 
hāṭṭaka eṉṉum vaṭacol oḷirvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟip poṉ eṉṟum taṅkam eṉṟum collalām. jampūnata eṉṉum vaṭacol, poṉ (mēru) malikku vaṭakkil nāvaṟ paḻaccāṟu perukiyōṭuvatāyk karutappaṭṭa āṟṟiṉ peyar. itu āriyat tolkatik koḷki. itaṉpaṭi, cāmpū natatti nāvalāṟi eṉṟu collal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ ēmāṟuntaṉmiyip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu, āriyar iṅṅaṉamē ellāt tuṟikaḷilum tamiḻnāṭṭup poruḷkaṭku vaṭacoṟ peyarkaḷiyiṭku vaḻakkāṟṟup paṭuttiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉar.
poṉ eṉpatu, poṉpōṉṟa piṟa kaṉiyap poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu , īyam mutaliya piṟa kaṉiyaṅkaḷum paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terintiruntaṉa. īyam eṉpatu iḷakuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
īyam - sīsa (va.).  iḷ - (iy) - ī - īyam,
o.nō: eḷ - ey. eyttal iḷittal.
veṇkalam tamiḻar, mutaṉ mutal amitta kalavik kaṉiya
mākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ oru coṟpeyar uṟi, muṟi eṉpaṉa.
(11) itaḷ māṟṟiyam (iracavātam)
cittar itaḷiṉāl (pātaracattāl) tāḻnta kaṉiyaṅkaḷi (ulōkaṅ
kaḷi) veḷḷiyākavum ciṟappākap poṉṉākavum māṟṟiṉatāka, maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. appoṉṉākkam vaṭamoḻiyil (i)raca vātam eṉappaṭum. akkaliyik kuṟikkum Alchemy eṉṉum colliliruntu Chemistry eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, kemiya nūli rasāyaṉam eṉṟaṉar. immuṟiyil atit tamiḻil itaḷiyam eṉalām.
poṉṉākkam ekipatu (Egypt) nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatāl, akkali arapiyil alkimiya (al - kimia) eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, al eṉpatu anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cuṭṭuc colleṉṟum, kimiya eṉpatu ekipatu nāṭṭiṉ peyareṉṟum, erutantuṟi āṅkilac curukka akara mutali kūṟum. (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English).
aḷakēcaṉ nikarāka ampoṉ mika vitta pērum nēcittu racavāta vittik kalintiṭuvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar pāṭuvatāl (paripūra.10), itaḷ māṟṟiyak kali tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
potuvāka, itu cittar kaliyeṉappaṭum. irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ cittaniliyaṭintiruntatiṉāl ikkaliyi aṟintiruntaṉar.
(12) maṟa nūl
taṉimakkaḷ pōr, paṭimakkaḷ pōr eṉappōr iru tiṟappaṭum. cilampam, maṟpōr, kuttuc caṇṭi, vāṭpōr mutaliyaṉa taṉi makkaḷ pōrām.
mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūrmallaṉip porutu koṉṟa kōpperu
naṟkiḷḷi, oru pōravi naṭatti vantāṉ; ataṉāl, pōravik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ naṭatti vantatu maṟpayiṟcik
kaḷari. maṟpōril, cila uyir nāṭiyāṉa narampukaḷit toṭṭu, etiriyi vīḻttik kollavum, vīḻntavaṉi mūṉṟē mukkāl nāḻikikkuḷ eḻacceyyavum, cila marumap piṭikaḷ uḷa.
maṟkaḷari, viṟkaḷari, vāṭkaḷari eṉap pōravi allatu muraṇkaḷari palavakip paṭum.
	iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi kaṉmār, 
	ikaliṉar eṟinta akalili murukkiṉ
	perumarak kampam 				(puṟam. 169 : 9-11)
eṉpatiliruntu, eypaṭi eṟipaṭi mutaliya paṭikkalap payiṟci naṭipeṟṟa vakiyi aṟiyalām.
kutirip paṭip payiṟci naṭipeṟṟa kaḷam ceṇṭu veḷi yeṉap
paṭṭatu.
kari pari tēr kāl ākiya nālvakip paṭip pōriyum paṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iṟantu paṭṭaṉa.
(13) ōka nūl
ōvutal - ottal, oṉṟutal. ōvu - ōku. ōvu - ōvam -ōkam = aṟivaṉ uḷattil iṟivaṉōṭu oṉṟum ūḻkam (tiyāṉam).
ukam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil yuka eṉṟum utti eṉpatu pukti eṉṟum āyatupōl, ōkam eṉpatum ammoḻiyil yōka eṉṟākum. immuṟi paṟṟi ōku eṉpatu yōku eṉappaṭum.
ōkap payiṟci eṇṇuṟuppukaḷi yuṭiyatu. avi oḻukkam (iyamam), oḻuṅku (niyamam), irukki (ācaṉam), vaḷinili (pirāṇāyāmam), pulaṉaṭakkam (pirattiyākāram), niṟi (tāraṇi), ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), oṭukkam(camāti) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ irukkiyum vaḷiniliyum uṭaṟ
payiṟci; ēṉiya uḷap payiṟci.
piṟappāl tammi uyarvākak karutum pētimiyum cerukku
muḷḷōr, ōkappayiṟci ceyvatu muyaṟkompām. iṭikali, piṉkali, cuḻimuṉi eṉpaṉa vaḷinilit toṭarpāṉa nāṭikaḷik kuṟikkum teṉ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟi iṭākalā, piṅkalā, sūṣūmuṉā eṉat tirin
tuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
ōka nūliṟ kūṟappaṭum aṟunilikkaḷaṅkaḷ allatu narappup piṉṉalkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivari, muṟiyē, aṇṭi kuṟiyiṭi nālitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum,  aṇṭi koppūḻiṭi āṟitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, koppūḻ maṇṭalattil pattitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, neñcāṅkuli maṇṭalattil paṉṉīritaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, aṭinā maṇṭalattil patiṉāṟitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, iru puruvattiṭi īritaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, iruppatākac collap peṟum.
ōkanūl iṟantu paṭṭatāl aṟunilikkaḷa (ṣaṭātāra)p peyarkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. irukkikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ palavāṟu tirikkappaṭṭum moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭum uḷa. āsaṉam eṉṉum collē teṉ colliṉ tiripeṉpatu, eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkap peṟum.
(14) māyam (Conjury)
itu mālam allatu kaṇ kaṭṭu.
(15) vaciyam (Enchantment)
itu makaḷiriyum piṟariyum maruntālum mantirattālum maṉap payiṟciyālum vayappaṭuttal.
vayiṉ - vayam - vacam - vaci - vaciyam.
(16) mantirak kaṭṭu
itu tī kāṟṟu mutaliya iyaṟkip pūtaṅkaḷiyum, puli nāy pāmpu  mutali uyirikaḷiyum māntariyum, iyaṅkātavāṟum tīṅku ceyyatāta vāṟum mantirattāl taṭuttal.
karaṭi vempuli vāyiyum kaṭṭalām		     (tāyu.tēcō.8)
(17) makiṭi (mōṭi)
itu mantirattāṟ poruḷkaḷi maṟittalum avaṟṟi eṭuttalu
mākum.
(18) pēyōṭṭal (Exorcism)
palvakip pēykaḷiyum kōṭaṅki allatu uṭukkaṭittu, pēy kōṭpaṭṭāriṉiṉṟu ōṭṭutal pēyōṭṭal ākum.
(19) kuṟaḷi
itu kuṭṭip pēyāl ciṟu kuṟumpukaḷ ceyvittal.
(20) ceyviṉi (Sorcery or Witchcraft)
itu pēyi āḷum mantirakkāraṉik koṇṭu, vēṇṭātavarkku nōyum cākkāṭum varuvittal.
itu cūṉiyam eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkum.
cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam - cūṉya (va.)
(21) karavaṭa nūl
itu kaḷavu nūl.
	mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam
	tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuviyeṉ
	ṟeṭṭuṭa ṉaṉṟē iḻukkuṭi marapil
	kaṭṭūṇ mākkaḷ tuṇiyeṉat tirivatu.
eṉpatu (cilap.16:166-9), karavaṭa nūliṉ kūṟukaḷit terivikkum. nimittam tantiram eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku, puḷ viraku eṉpaṉa muṟiyē nikar teṉcoṟkaḷām.
(22) uṭal nūl
meyp poruḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟēṉum paṭṭāṅku nūl muṟiyilum, maruttuva muṟiyilum, pulāluṇavu muṟiyilum, maṟpōr muṟiyilum, uṭaṟkūṟukaḷip paṇṭit tamiḻar naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar.
(23) kāval nūl
	nūlvaḻip piḻiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ tērcci
	ūrkāp pāḷar.
eṉṉum maturik kāñciyaṭikaḷiyum (646-7), avaṟṟiṉ kaḷavu kāṇṭaṟkum kāttaṟkum kūṟiya nūlkaḷ pōvār eṉṉum uriyiyum, nōkkuka.
15 aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences)
perumpālum paṭippiṉāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa aṟiviyalkaḷ.
(1) ilakkaṇam
ilakku - ilakkiyam. ilakku - ilakkaṇam. ilakku = kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ. ciṟanta vāḻkkik kuṟikkōḷāṉa aṟatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu ilakkiyam. ciṟanta moḻik kuṟikkōḷāṉa amippi eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu ilakkaṇam.
	uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻintati nilaṉeṉak
	koḷḷum eṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (akat.47)ilakkaṇattik kuṟiyeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟum, ilakkiyattiṟku aṇaṅkiyam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
ïy¡F - yº(t.)., ilakkiyam - lakṣya (va.) ilakkaṇam -  lakṣṇa - (va.). ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōl, lakṣaṇa lakṣya eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ moḻiyamitiyiyum (Grammar) nūṟṟokutiyiyum (Literature) kuṟippatilli; kuṟi allatu iyalpu, kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷ eṉṉum iraṇṭiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ilakkaṇattiyum ilakkiyattiyum kuṟikka, viyākaraṇam (vyākaraṇa) cākittiyam (sāhitya) eṉṉum coṟkaḷiyē vaṭamoḻiyāḷār āḷkiṉṟaṉar.
ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. 
	oruvarik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
	oṉṟaṉik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
	vaḻakki ṉākiya uyarcoṟ kiḷavi
	ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collā ṟalla.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil (510), ilakkaṇam eṉṉum col vantiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ, eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. poruḷil yāppum yāppil aṇiyum aṭaṅkum. paṇṭi yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷil iruntatiṉāl, urinaṭikkeṉac ciṟappāy ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭavilli. colliṟkē poruḷiruppatāl, colliṟkup  piṉpu poruḷiyē ilakkaṇap pakutiyāka eṭuttuk
koṇṭaṉar. poruṭpakutiyiṉṟit tamiḻilakkaṇam niṟivuḷḷatākātu.
tamiḻic cariyāy uṇarāta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar, poruḷilakkaṇattip pāṭṭiyal eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar. ilakkiyap poruḷkaṭku ilakkaṇam kūṟuvatē poruḷilakkaṇam eṉa aṟika.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku kālṭuvelār kūṟiyavāṟu vaṭamoḻi yip piṉpaṟṟiyataṉṟu. vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkē tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiya tākum.
tamiḻ eḻuttu kīṟeḻattum veṭṭeḻuttum eṉa iruvakip paṭṭatu. muṉṉatu oliyeḻuttu; piṉṉatu paṭṭaya veḻuttu. paṭṭaya veḻuttiyē vaṭṭeḻut teṉpar.
iṉṟu acciluḷḷa ōliyeḻuttu toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
	tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ
	ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.
eṉṟu 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūl (98) kūṟutal kāṇka. āṇṭu eṉṟatu muṟkālatti. ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟṟatē muṟkāla eḻuttiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉpatu naṉṉūlār karuttu. avar kālattil ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟavilli; peṟṟirukkumāyiṉ ati ēṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum? ātalāl, atu uriyaṉmi aṟika. akkālam eṉpatu iṉṟum muṟkālattik kuṟittal kāṇka.
eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil kīṟiyeḻutuvataṟku vaḷi kōṭṭeḻuttum,uḷikoṇṭu paṭṭayattil kuḻitteḻutuvataṟku nērkōṭṭeḻuttumē, ēṟṟatātal kāṇka.
intiya āriyarkku mutalil eḻuttilli. avar maṟi eḻutākkiḷaviyeṉappaṭṭatu. vētakkālap pirāmaṇar tamiḻ nāṭu vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil nūleḻutat tamiḻeḻuttiyoṭṭik kiranta veḻutti amittukkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, ki.pi. 10-ām allatu 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu.
aṟiyappaṭṭa camaṟkiruta mutaṟpaḻaṅkalveṭṭu, kattiyavāril cuṉākar eṉṉumiṭattil oru pāṟi mēl uḷatu. atu uruttira tāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉa vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālam ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. atu nākariyil illi; paḻiya kalveṭṭeḻuttil uḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuriya pavar kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ, nākariyi nōkkiya mikunta muṉṉēṟṟattik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; atē camiyattil tantiturukkaṉiṉ ki.pi.750-ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu, iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyip peritum otta kuṟikaḷiṉ muḻut tokutiyiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmiyāṉa iṟṟi nākariyuluḷḷa mutaṟkalveṭṭu, ki.pi.11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muntiyataṉ ṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (māṉiyar ulliyamcu camaṟkiruta- āṅkila akara mutali - muṉṉuri, g¡.XXVIII,  aṭik kuṟippu).
tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttip piṉpaṟṟiyatē eṉpatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyilumilli.
tamiḻar iṉṉicik kaliyilum nāṭakakkaliyilum ciṟantirunta
tiṉāl, moḻiyoṭu avviru kalikaḷiyum cērttu, tamiḻi iyalici nāṭakameṉa muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṉar. ittakiya moḻiyamippiṭam vēṟeṅkaṇumilli.
tamiḻar iyalumiṭamellām tam viṉikaḷi iciyoṭu ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu, tālāṭṭup pāṭṭu, ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭu, naṭa vippāṭṭu, mukavippāṭṭu, ēṟṟap pāṭṭu, ēlap pāṭṭu. vaḷḷippāṭṭu, kaḻiyaṟ pāṭṭu, kummip pāṭṭu, kōlāṭṭappāṭṭu. ūñcaṟ pāṭṭu, vaḻinaṭic cintu , oppu (oppāri)p pāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷilum irunta ilakkaṇamellām mut tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷē. iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇam piṇṭam eṉṟum, muttamiḻilakkaṇam māpiṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum.
piṇṭittal = piṭittal, tiraṭṭutal piṇṭi - piṇṭam = tiraḷi. piṇṭi - piṭi. o.nō: taṇṭi - taṭi. piṭittal = tiraṭṭutal, kikkuḷ tiraḷak koḷḷutal. pori viḷaṅkāy piṭittal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum, piḷḷiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu, eṉṉum paḻamoḻi  yiyum, nōkkuka.
poruḷilakkaṇam poruḷkaḷi akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kātaliṉ patti akam eṉac ciṟappittu, maṟṟellāvaṟṟiyum puṟattuḷ aṭakkum. puṟattuḷ pōrttuṟi mikac ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, maṟṟaviyellām eḻupuṟap poruḷ tiṇi
kaḷuḷ āṟāvatāṉa vākit tiṇiyuḷ aṭakkappeṟum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivi eṉ tolkāppiya viḷakkam eṉṉum nūluṭ kaṇṭu koḷka. ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻ mikunta ilakkaṇa varampuḷḷatu.
	kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
	paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
	maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
	eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar (tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam) kūṟutal kāṇka.
(2) moḻi nūl
moḻi nūṟku vittūṉṟiyavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻc coṟkaḷi, ilakkaṇa vakic col mūṉṟum poruḷvakic col oṉṟum coṟpiṟappiyal vakic col mūṉṟumāka vakuttiruntaṉar.
	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.  	    (tol.peyar.1)
	colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉieṉṟu
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē. 	    (tol.peyar 4)
	iṭic col kiḷaviyum uriccol kiḷaviyum
	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkil tōṉṟum eṉpa. 	    (tol.peyar 5)
iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol eṉa mūṉṟiyalum ceyyuḷ īṭṭaccollē.
	centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
	taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi. 	      (tol.ecca.4)
	annāṟ collum toṭukkuṅ kāli
	valikkumvaḻi valittalum melikkumvaḻi melittalum
	virikkumvaḻi virittalum tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum
	nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum kuṟukkumvaḻik kuṟukkalum
	nāṭṭal vaḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar. 		       (tol.ecca 7)
innūṟpākkaḷum, mutaṉili, iṭinili, īṟu, uṟupu, puṇarcci, cāriyi mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷum, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ moḻi nūlaṟivikkāṭṭum.
iyaṟcol eṉpatu vērc collum aṭic collumāṉa iyalpāṉa col (Primitive mood). tirinta col eṉpatu atiṉiṉṟu tirinta col (Derivative mood). ivi yiraṇṭum centamiḻc col. ticiccol eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc col. akkālattil vaṭa collum piṟa ayaṟcollum tamiḻiṟ kalakkavilli.
(2)aṟanūl
itu poruḷkaḷi aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttu, nalloḻukkattip paṟṟik kūṟum.
(3) poruḷ nūl
itu ellārum tattam toḻilāṟ poruḷīṭṭuvataṟku, iṉṟiyamiyāta pātukāppuc ceyyum araciyalip paṟṟikkūṟum.
(4) iṉpa nūl
itu āṭavar peṇṭir kāmaviṉpattic ciṟappittuk kūṟum. iṉpa vāḻkkiyik kūṟuvatu akapporuḷ nūl eṉṟum iṉpat tuyppi maṭṭum kūṟuvatu kāmanūl allatu vēṇūl eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
(5)maṟinūl (Theology)
itu perumpālum camayak kuṭumikaḷi allatu koṇmuṭipu kaḷik kūṟum. itu mantiram eṉavum vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. maṉṉum tiram (tiṟam) mantiram. maṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal. itu uṇmi
yākum eṉṟu tiṇmiyāy eṇṇic colvatu mantiram.
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymori piciyē 		   (tol.cey.78)
	niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
	maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa. 		(tol.cey.176)
muṉṉutal karututal. muṉṉam = maṉam. muṉ - maṉ - maṉam. iṉi, muṉṉam- muṉam - maṉam eṉṟumām.
mantiram camayak koḷki paṟṟiyatum cāvippu vāḻttup paṟṟi yatum eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
camayak koḷki paṟṟiyatum, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, koṇmuṭipu nūl eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. nammāḻvār aruḷic ceyta tiruvāymoḻi kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu; tirumūlar aruḷic ceyta tirumantiram koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) nūl.
	niṟimoḻi māntar perumi nilattu
	maṟimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum.
eṉṟatu (kuṟaḷ.28) ittakiya nūlkaḷi nōkkiyē.
cāvippu vāḻttu moḻikaḷi mantiram eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku; nūl vaḻakkaṉṟu.
6.paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy)
paṭṭāṅku uṇmi. paṭṭāṅku nūl meypporuḷ nūl. māntaṉ uṭalamippup paṟṟit tamiḻar kaṇṭa meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ), 96. avi ātaṉ (āṉma) meyporuḷ 24 , nāṭi10, nili (avatti) 5, malam 3,kuṇam 3, maṇṭalam 3, piṇi 3. tiripu (vikāram) 8, nilikkaḷam (ātāram)  6, tātu 7 ūti (vāyu) 10. uṟi (kōcam) 5, vāyil 9, eṉpaṉa.
ātaṉ meyp poruḷ 24 āvaṉa: - pūtam 5, pulaṉ 5, aṟivup pulaṉ 5, karumappulaṉ 5, karaṇam 4.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum aimpūtamāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; āṇṭavaṉ (pati), ātaṉ (pacu), ācu(pācam) eṉa mūṉṟāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; uyir, mey (uṭampu) eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; tamiḻariṉ vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkikaḷām.
ātaṉukku iṟivaṉōṭuḷḷa toṭarpumuṟi irumi (tuvitam), oṉṟiya irumi (viciṣṭāttu vitam) eṉa iruvakiyākavē, paṇṭit tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. orumi (attuvitam) tamiḻar koḷki
yaṉṟu.
tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl muṉivaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟatiṉāl, uyir, mey, uyirmey, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam, puṇarcci, mutaliya eḻuttilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; peyar, viṉi mutal, eṇ vēṟṟumi, viṉi, viṉimuṟṟu. viṉiyeccam, iṟantakāla viṉi, nikaḻkāla viṉi, etirkāla viṉi mutaliya collilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; meypporuḷ nūṟkaruttum taḻuvumāṟu amikkap
peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkatti eṉ centamiḻc ciṟappu. muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇka.
(7) aḷavi nūl
itu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟitaṟku ētāṉa (ētuvāṉa) aḷavikaḷi (piramāṇaṅkaḷi) eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu. aḷavikaḷ mottam pattu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉavi kāṭci karuttu oppu uri eṉṉum nāṉku. karutteṉpatu uyttuṇarvu.
(8) ēraṇam (Logic)
itu aḷavikaḷik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟiyum vakikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu.
vaṭivēl ceṭṭiyār patippitta tarkka paripāṣieṉṉum nūliṟutiyil, akattiyar pērāl 20 tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu,
	poruḷkuṇam karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
	iṉmi yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu. itu poruḷkaḷi ēḻākap pakuppatu. itiyē piṟkālattil vaṭavar vicēṭikam eṉṟaṉar.
ēraṇam uravam yōkam icikaṇak kiratam cālam   (taṉippāṭal)
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar       (tirukkōvic ciṟappuppāyiram).
(9) vāṉa nūl (Astronomy)
vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ (naṭcattiram), kōḷ (kirakam) , ōri (irāci) mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkik kūṟuvatu vāṉa nūl. irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum eḻu kōḷkaḷum paṉṉīrōrikaḷum kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar.
eḻu kōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl ēḻu nāṭkiḻamiyi mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭuttiyavar tamiḻarē. atu piṉṉar ulaka muḻutum paraviyuḷḷatu.
koḷ - kōḷ. koḷḷutal vaḷital. koḷ - koṭku. koṭkutal = cuḻalutal, cuṟṟi varutal. koṭpatu kōḷ. kōḷkaḷ vāṉattiṟ cuḻaṉṟu cuṟṟivarum tōṟṟattāl appeyar peṟṟaṉa. irāku kētu eṉpaṉa cāyikaḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. niṟam paṟṟi avi aṇivakiyil karumpāmpu, cempāmpu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iruttal karuttal. ir - irā - irāku. cē - cētu = civappu. cētāmpal cevvām pal. cētu - kētu. o.nō: cempu - kempu.
tamiḻ 		vaṭamoḻi 		āṅkilam
ñāyiṟu 		ātittaṉ 		Sun-Sunday
tiṅkaḷ 		cōmaṉ 			Moon-Monday
cevvāy 		(maṅkaḷam) 		Mars-Tuesday
aṟivaṉ 		putaṉ 			Mercury-Wednesday
viyāḻaṉ 		pirukaspati(kuru)		Jupiter-Thusday
veḷḷi  		cukkiraṉ 			Venus-Friday
kāri    		(caṉi) 			Saturn-Saterday
vaṭamoḻikkiḻamip peyarkaḷuḷ, maṅkaḷa vāram, caṉi vāram ākiya iraṇṭu tavira maṟṟaviyellām nēr moḻipeyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
āṅkilap peyarkaḷuḷ, piṉ aintum piṟkālattil māṟi viṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, eḻunāḷ eṉṉum kāla aḷavu māṟātiruttal kāṇka.
cevvāy cenniṟamuḷḷateṉṟum, viyāḻaṉ poṉṉiṟamuḷḷa teṉṟum, veḷḷi veṇmiyāṉateṉṟum, kāri kariyateṉṟum, kaṇṭaṟintatu viyakkat takka ceytiyām.
viyal = 1. perumi.
mūḻttiṟutta viyaṉṟāṉi 			          (patiṟ.33,5).
		2. akalam
viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē.           (tol.col 354).
		3.mikuti. (cilap.3,7, uri).
viyal - viyalaṉ - viyāḻaṉ.
viḷḷutal = virital, malartal. viḷ - viy - viyal.
pirukaspati eṉṉum vaṭacol viyāḻaṉ eṉpataṉ moḻi peyarppāyiruppatōṭu, peruku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripi nilimoḻi mutaṉiliyākak koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
paṉiṉiru orikaḷ
tamiḻ 	vaṭamoḻi 	ilattīṉ
mēḻam 	mēṣam 	Aries
viṭi 	riṣapam 	Taurus
iraṭṭi 	mituṉam	Gemini
alavaṉ 	karkkaṭakam	Cancer
āḷi 	cimmam 	Leo
kaṉṉi 	kaṉṉi 	Virgo
tuli 	tulā 	Libra
naḷi 	viruccikam 	Scorpion
cili 	cāpam 	Sagitarius
cuṟavam 	makaram 	(Capricornus)
kumpam 	kumpam 	Aquarius
mīṉam 	mīṉam 	Pisces
vaṭamoḻiyil ellāp peyarkaḷum, ilattīṉil patiṉoru peyarkaḷum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ nēr moḻipeyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
muḻuttal tiruḷutal. muḻutta āṇ piḷḷi eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. muḻutu mottam. muḻu - muḻā = tiraṇṭa muracu. muḻā - muḻavu - muḻavam. muḻā - miḻā = tiraṇṭa māṉ (Stag). miḻā - mēḻam = tiraṇṭa cemmaṟiyāṭṭukkaṭā. mēḻam - mēṣa(va.) mēḻam - mēḻakam - ēḻakam - ēṭakam - ēṭu - yāṭu - āṭu.
viṭiyum kaṉṉiyum muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
tullutal = poruntutal, ottal. tul - tuṉ. tuṉṉutal poruntutal. tul - tuli = oppu. iru puṟamum otta niṟikōl. tul - tulā = tuli pōṉṟa ēṟṟam. tulā - tulām - tulāṉ = oru niṟi. o.nō: oppu = tuli. opparāvi = tuli ceyvōṉ. kummutal kuvital. kam - kampu - kumpam - kumpa (va.). miṉ - mīṉam -mīṉa (va.).
paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil paṉṉī rōrip peyarkaḷē paṉṉiru mātap peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
mati - mātam - māsa(va.). ovvoru piṟi niliyum pakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. vaḷarpiṟi veṇpakkam eṉṟum, tēypiṟi karumpakkam eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
paku - pakkam. paku - Bhaj (va.). pakkam-pakṣa(va.).
irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇi , uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi , muracu, tōṇi eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu. iviyallāta potu uṭukkaḷ veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa. pukikkōḷ (Comet) vāl
veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
oru pakalum ōr iravum cērntatu oru nāḷ eṉṟum ēḻu nāḷ koṇṭatu oru kiḻamiyeṉṟum, oru vaḷarpiṟiyum, oru tēypiṟiyum cērntatu oru mātam eṉṟum, katiravaṉ paṉṉīrōrik kuḷḷum pukuvatu paṉṉiru mātam eṉṟum, ataṉ oru vaṭa celavum oru teṉ celavum cērntu ōr āṇṭeṉṟum, kaṇakkiṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. oru nāḷi āṟu ciṟu poḻutākap pirittatu pōṉṟu, ōr āṇṭi āṟu perum poḻutākap pirittiruntaṉar.
oru kōnakart tōṟṟam allatu oru pēraracaṉ piṟappup pōṉṟa nikaḻcciyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, toṭarāṇṭu kaṇittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
	ceññā yiṟṟuc celavum añ ñāyiṟṟup
	parippum parippuc cūḻntamaṇ ṭilamum
	vaḷitiri taru ticiyum
	vaṟitu niliiya kāyamum eṉṟivi
	ceṉṟaṉan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
	iṉitteṉ pōrum uḷarē
eṉṉum (puṟam.30) uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār kūṟṟum, 
mākavicumpu (puṟam.35, akam. 253, maturikkā.454, pari.1) eṉṉum  vaḻakkum, mākamāvatu pūmikkum cuvarkkattukkum naṭuvu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar uriyum,
	iṉṉici yeḻiliyi irappavum iyivatō
	vaḷitarum celvaṉi vāḻttavum iyivatō?
eṉṉum pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkōviṉ pāṭṭaṭikaḷum (kalit.15), kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
maḻikkum kāṟṟiṟkum katiravaṉ karaṇiyam eṉpati muṉṉit tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar. eḻili mukil. eḻiliyit tōṟṟavikkum katiravaṉi eḻili eṉṟatu ilakkaṇiyeṉṉum ākupeyarp pōli.
(10) kaṇiyam (Astrology)
oruvar piṟanta nāḷiyum vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷkōḷ nili yiyum aṭippaṭiyāka vittu, avarukku, varum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi muṟpaṭak kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu kaṇiyam. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvataṟkum variyappaṭum kaṇiyamē piṟappiyam (jātakam).
kōccēramāṉ yāṉikkaṭ cēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟi iṉṉanāḷ iṟappāṉ eṉṟu, kūṭalūrkiḻār oru viṇvīḻ koḷḷiyik kaṇṭu kaṇittaṟinta ceytiyi 229-ām puṟappāṭṭuk kūṟum. nalla nāḷum vēḷiyum pārttu viṉikaḷit toṭaṅkuvatu, paṇṭināḷiṟ peruvaḻakkamā yiruntatu. nāḷē raṭittal, kuṭināṭkōḷ, vāḷnāṭ
kōḷ eṉpaṉa itit terivikkum.
toṉṟu toṭṭuk kaṇiyat toḻil ceytu varum tamiḻ vakuppāṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ. vaḷ kūrmi. vaḷḷuvaṉ kūrmatiyaṉ. āriyak kulap pirivāl, avaṉ tāḻttappaṭṭup pēriṭaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum piḻip piḻantāṉ.
kaṇṇutal = akakkaṇṇāṟ pārttal, karututal, matittal. kaṇṇiyam matippu. kaṇ - kaṇi. kaṇittal = matittal, kaṇakkiṭutal, aḷaviṭutal. kaṇi - kaṇitam - kaṇicam = matippu (Aprroximation). kaṇi - kaṇiki = tāḷam kaṇittāṭupavaḷ. kaṇi - kaṇikaṉ. kaṇiyaṉ = nāḷ kōḷ nilikaṇṭu varuṅkāla naṉmi tīmi kaṇippavaṉ. kaṇ, kaṇi, kaṇitam, kaṇiki eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil  mutaleḻut teṭup
pōciyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇitam eṉpatu kaṇita eṉṟum, kaṇiki eṉpatu kaṇikā eṉṟum, īṟu kuṉṟiyum tirintum vaḻaṅkum. 
(11) kaṇakku
kaḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal, kūṭutal, kaḷḷa =
okka (uvama urupu), kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭumiṭam. kaḷ - kaḷa - kaṇa. kaṇattal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal. kaṇakka = pōla. kuraṅku kaṇakka ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. kaṇa - kaṇam = kūṭṭam. kaṇavaṉ kūṭukiṉṟavaṉ. kaṇa - kaṇakku = kūṭiya toki, aḷavu
kaṇakku eṉpatu mutalil kūṭṭal kaṇakki maṭṭum kuṟittu, piṉpu nālvakik kaṇakkiṟkum potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērntavar eṉpatu, avar kiyāṇṭa nuṇṇiya aḷavikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.

mēlvāyilakkap pēreṇkaḷ
tolkāppiyattil, ilakkam nūṟāyiram eṉṉum toṭarc collāl kuṟikkap paṭṭālum, ilakkam eṉṉum colliṉmiyāl, āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pērilakkap peyarkaḷ tamiḻil illiyeṉap palar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyarē,
	aiam paleṉa varūum iṟuti
	alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum.
eṉap (393) pala kōṭikaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷik kuṟittalāl, ilakkam, kōṭi eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ avar kālattil tamiḻil illiyeṉpatu poruntātu.
	aṭukkiya kōṭi peṟiṉum kuṭippiṟantār
	kuṉṟuva ceytal ilar.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷil (954), kōṭi vantiruttal kāṇka. aṭukkiya kōṭikaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷiṟ cila varumāṟu:
kumpam = āyiraṅ kōṭi
kaṇikam = pattāyiraṅ kōṭi
tāmari = kōṭā kōṭi
caṅkam = pattuk kōṭā kōṭi
vāraṇam = nūṟu kōṭā kōṭi
paratam = ilakkam kōṭik kōṭā kōṭi.
(1iṉ piṉ 24 cuṉṉaṅ koṇṭatu.)
ai am paleṉa varuum iṟuti eṉṟu tolkāppiyar (tol.393) kuṟippāy īṟupaṟṟiccoṉṉavaṟṟi:
	neytalum kuvaḷiyum āmpalum caṅkamum
	miyil kamalamum veḷḷamum
eṉṟu (paripāṭal,2;13-14) kīrantiyār veḷippaṭiyāyk kūṟiṉār. kamalam (va.) tāmari.
tāmari, kuvaḷi eṉpaṉa aiyīṟu; kaṇikam, caṅkam, veḷḷam eṉpaṉa ammīṟu; āmpal pallīṟu; neytal allīṟu. itit tolkāppiyar kuṟittilar. āmpal kumutam eṉṟum kuṟikkappeṟum.
kumpam, tāmari, caṅkam, vāraṇam eṉpaṉa tūya teṉ coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkatti eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka. caṅkam caṅku. tāmari eṉṉum eṇṇip patumam eṉṟu moḻipeyarttuk kūṟuvar vaṭavar; muḷari eṉṟu oru poruḷ maṟucollāṟ kuṟippar kampar.
tāmari - tāmaraca(va.). caṅku - caṅkam - Sankha (va.)
kaṇakku allatu kaṇita nūl eṇṇūl eṉavum peyar peṟum. ērampam eṉṉum paṇṭit tamiḻk kaṇita nūl iṟantupaṭṭatu.
ciṟṟilakkattiṟkum pērilakkattiṟkum perukkal vāypaṭiruntatu pōṉṟē, catura (Square) vāyppāṭum paṇṭik kālattiliruntatu. atu kuḻikkaṇakku eṉappeṟṟatu. kuḻittal caturittal. atu ōr eṇṇi avveṇṇikkoṇṭē perukkutal. ciṟṟilakkak kuḻippu ciṟu kuḻiyeṉṟum, pērilakkak kuḻippu peruṅkuḻiyeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kuḻi - kuṇi - gun (va.).
ḻa - ṇa. o. nō: āḻi - āṇi - āḻamāyiṟaṅkuvatu. āṇivēr āḻamāy iṟaṅkum vēr.
taḻal - taṇal.
kuṇittal perukkutal, caturittal.
kuṇi + aṉam = kuṇaṉam.
(12) uṭaṟkuṟi nūl (Physiognomy)
tali, kaḻuttu, mārpu, kikāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiluḷḷa vari (irēki), maṟu, maccam, cuḻi mutaliyavaṟṟikkoṇṭum; av vuṟuppuk
kaḷiṉ vaṭivu, niṟam mutaliyavaṟṟikkoṇṭum; makkaḷiṉ iyalpu
kaḷiyum avarkku nērum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu uṭaṟ kuṟi nūlākum.
kivari nūl (Palmistry) oru taṉi nūlāy vaḻaṅki variṉum, atu uṭaṟkuṟi nūliṉ oru pirivē.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟṟik kaṇṭu avarukku aracāḷum tiruppoṟi yuṇṭeṉṟu kuṟikāraṉ kūṟiyatu, uṭaṟkuṟi nūl taḻuviyē.
	nunti tāḷniḻal iruntōy niṉṉi
	aricuvīṟ ṟirukkum tiruppoṟi yuṇṭeṉṟu
	uricey tavaṉ 			            (cilap.30: 174-6)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pattiṉit teyvak kūṟṟāyk kūṟutal kāṇka.

(13) puḷ nūl
vallūṟu, ānti, kākam, karikkuruvi, kāṭi mutaliya paṟavi
kaḷiṉ kuraliyum iyakkattiyum koṇṭu, varappōkum naṉmi tīmikaḷik kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu puḷ nūl.
puḷ paṟavi. puḷḷāl aṟiyappaṭum kuṟiyip puḷ eṉpatu ākupeyar.
vaḻiccelvār vāyiṉiṉṟu taṟceyalāy varum collik kuṟiyākak koḷvatu, vāyppuḷ eṉappaṭum.
uṭampiṉ palavuṟuppukkaḷ tuṭippatik kūṟum tuṭinūlum, kaṭṭu eṉṉum neṟkuṟiyum, kaḻaṅku eṉṉum kāykkuṟiyum , ētēṉum ōr ēṭṭit toṭum toṭukuṟiyum, eṇkuṟiyum, peyarkkuṟiyum, niṉṟa nilik kuṟiyum kavaṭikkuṟiyum, kaṇṭa kāṭcik kuṟiyum nērnta nikaḻccik kuṟiyum, coṉṉa
coṟ kuṟiyum, kōṭiḻittaṟkuṟiyum, mūccu viṭaṟkuṟiyum. tēvarāḷar
kuṟiyum, viriccikkuṟiyum, (Oracle) iraṇṭiloṉṟaṉ kuṟiyum, palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷāṉa naṟkuṟi tīkkuṟikaḷum piṟavum puḷnūliṉpāṟpaṭuvaṉavē.
(14) kaṉā nūl
iṉṉa yāmattil iṉṉa poruḷ allatu nikaḻcci kāṇiṉ, ivvaḷavu kālattil iṉṉatu nērum eṉṟu kūṟum nūl kaṉā nūlām.
maturit tamiḻc caṅkam veḷiyiṭṭa oru kaṉā nūl iṉṟum uḷatu.
(15) uḷa nūl (Psychology)
taṉippaṭṭavarum tokutiyāḷarumāṉa makkaḷiṉ uḷappāṅku kaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu uḷa nūl.
itu tolkāppiya meyppāṭṭiyalāl aṟiyappaṭum. eṇcuvi
kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum, mutaṉ mutal eṭuttuk kūṟiyatu tamiḻilakkaṇamē.
(16) pūta nūl
nilam nīr tī vaḷi veḷi eṉṉum aimpūtaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷik kūṟum nūl pūta nūl.
``	karuvaḷar vāṉat ticiyiṉ tōṉṟi
	uruvaṟi vārā oṉṟaṉ ūḻiyum
	untuvaḷi kiḷarnta ūḻuḻ ūḻiyum
	centīc cuṭariya ūḻiyum paṉiyoṭu
	taṇpeyal taliiya ūḻiyum aviyiṟṟu
	uṇmuṟi veḷḷam mūḻki yārtarupu
	mīṇṭum pīṭuyar pīṇṭi avaṟṟiṟkum
	uḷḷī ṭākiya irunilat tūḻiyum''		        (pari. 2 : 5-12)
``	kaltōṉṟi maṇtōṉṟāk kālattu''	           (pu.ve. 35)
``	malimāṟiya viyaṉñālattu	                           (maturi. 4)
``	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
	kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ''		     (tol. mara. 90)
``	vañca maṉattāṉ paṭiṟṟoḻukkam pūtaṅkaḷ
	aintum akattē nakum.''			          (kuṟaḷ. 271)
ivi aimpūtaṅkaḷip paṟṟip paṇṭiyat tamiḻarkkirunta aṟivip pulappaṭuttum.
(17) nila nūl
nilattiṉ vakikaḷiyum nilappaṭi vakikaḷiyum maṇṇiṉ vakikaḷiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu nila nūl.
(18) nīr nūl
kiṇaṟu tōṉṟuvataṟku nīrirukkum iṭaṅkaḷit terivippatu nīr nūl. itu kūva nūl eṉavumpaṭum.
(19) putiyal nūl
putiyal irukkumiṭaṅkaḷi aṟiyac ceyvatu putiyal nūl.
(20) kōḻi nūl
itu pōrc cēvaṟ kōḻikaḷiṉ niṟaṅkaḷiyum tiṟaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu.
(21) pari nūl
itu palvakik kutirikaḷiyum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiyellām viḷakkik kūṟuvatu.
(22) yāṉi nūl
itu yāṉiyip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiyellām virivākak kūṟuvatu.
(23) varalāṟṟu nūl
mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟil pāṇṭiyar toki kuṟikkappaṭṭiruntālum, aṉṟāṭa naṭavaṭikkikaḷip paṭṭōlip perumāṉ eḻutivantatālum, paraṇi eṉṉum vākip paṉuvalkaḷil araca vaḻimarapu kūṟappaṭuvatālum, varalāṟṟu nūl oruvakiyil eḻutappaṭṭu vantami uyttuṇarappaṭum. mūvēntar kuṭi varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷum avar varalāṟṟuk karuvi nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
``	ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratam cālam
	tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampamnīr nilamu lōkam
	māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
	vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻi vaḻip peyarum māḷa.''
eṉpatu, iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻkkali nūl vakikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṭum taṉippāviṉam. piṟkālac ceyyuḷātalāl, cila kali nūlkaḷ vaṭacoṟ peyarāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(25) tiṇi nūl
mutal karuuri eṉṉum mūvakip poruḷiyum paṟṟik kūṟum tiṇi nūl, oru vakiyil ñālanūli (Geography) okkum.
(26) poḻutu pōkku
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ oḻivu nērattiyum ōyvu nāṭkaḷiyum piṉvarumāṟu kaḻittu vantaṉar.
ciṟuvar:
kōliyaṭittal, tel teṟittal, kiḷittaṭṭu, caṭukuṭu, killit
tāṇṭu, pāṇṭi (cillākku), mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭāṭal, kāṟṟāṭi viṭutal, maramēṟutal, nīruḷ mūḻki viḷiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
iḷiñar:
caṭukuṭu, kiḷittaṭṭu mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭāṭal, maṟpayiṟci iḷavaṭṭakkal tūkkal, kariyalaṭittal, vēṭṭiyāṭal, ēṟukōḷ (callikkaṭṭu) mutaliyaṉa.
mutiyōr.
cēvaṟpōr, kaṭāppōr, katuvālippōr, kāṭippōr, puṟāppōṭṭi mutaliyaṉa kāṇal, vēṭṭiyāṭal,tūṇṭil pōṭutal, paṇiyam vittum viyātum tāyamāṭutal mutaliyaṉa.
ciṟumiyar:
vīṭukaṭṭi viḷiyāṭal, kummiyaṭittal, pūppaṟittal, kaḻaṅ kāṭal, teḷḷēṇam koṭṭal, ammāṉiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
peṇṭir:
kiḷi vaḷarttal, pūvi (nākaṇam) vaḷarttal, puṟā vaḷarttal, māṉ vaḷarttal, māli toṭuttal, tāyamāṭutal, kuravi yāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
aracar:
kutirip pantayam, tērppantayam, yāṉippōr, cākkik kūttu mutaliyaṉa kāṇal;  yāḻppōr, icippōr, āṭaṟpōr, ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi, paṭṭimaṉṟam, palkavaṉa araṅku, nūlaraṅkēṟṟam mutaliya naṭappittal; vēṭṭiyāṭal, paṉiyam vittut tāyamāṭal, urimic cuṟṟattuṭaṉ ilavantikic cōliyil viḷiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
ūrār:
tiruviḻāk koṇṭāṭal, nāṭakam naṭippittal, ēṟukōḷ
naṭattutal, mutaliyaṉa. ēṟukōḷ iṉṟu callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum mañcu veruṭṭu eṉṟum māṭupiṭi caṇṭi eṉṟum collappeṟum.
nakarattār:
vēntaṉ (intira) viḻāk koṇṭāṭal, putuppuṉalāṭal mutaliyaṉa. vēntaṉ viḻāvik koṇṭāṭumpōtu piṟa teyvaṅkaṭkum pūcikaḷ naṭattiṉar.
2
paṇṭit tamiḻap paṇpāṭu
1. potu
 mēli nāṭukaḷellām nākarikamaṭintiruntālum, avaṟṟuḷ āṅkila nāṭē paṇpāṭṭiṟ ciṟantatākac collappaṭuvatupōl, nāvalan
tēyattilum tamiḻ nāṭē nallatākac collappaṭṭatu.
	vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
	āyiṭit tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu
eṉṟu, tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyiram iyaṟṟiya paṉampāraṉār kūṟutal kāṇka.
	vaṭukar aruvāḷar vāṉkaru nāṭar
	cuṭukāṭu pēyerumi eṉṟivi yāṟum
	kuṟukār aṟivuṭiyār.
eṉpatu oru paḻañ ceyyuḷ (tol. col 55. cēṉā. uri mēṟkōḷ).
	ciṟappuṭi marapiṟ poruḷum iṉpamum
	aṟattuvaḻip paṭūum tōṟṟam pōla
eṉpatāl (puṟam 31), tamiḻar aṟattiyē ellāp pēṟukaṭkum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākum.
moḻittuṟiyil, avar nākarikam aṭintiruntatu pōṉṟu paṇpāṭum aṭintiruntaṉar. oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkup pōtal eṉṟum kāl kaḻuvutal eṉṟum iṭakkaraṭakkiyum, iṟantavaṉit tuñciṉāṉ eṉṟum cāvip perum piṟitu eṉṟum nalla pāmpu kaṭittalik koṭit taṭṭal eṉṟum maṅkala vaḻakkākavum, kūṟivantaṉar.
periyōrik ki nīṭṭic cuṭṭāmalum, avari nōkkik kāl nīṭṭāmalum, avar niṟka iruntu koṇṭu pēcāmalum, avari nīṅkaḷ eṉṟu muṉṉilip peyarāṟkuṟiyātu tāṅkaḷ eṉṟum aṅkuṟṟi eṉṟum paṭarkkic collāṟ kuṟittum, paṇivuṭimi kāṭṭi vantaṉar. potuvāka, iḻintōṉi nī eṉṟum, ottōṉi nīr eṉṟum. mūttōṉi nīṅkaḷ eṉṟum, uyarntōṉit tāṅkaḷ eṉṟum tuṟavumaṭat talivaṉi aṅkuṟṟi eṉṟum, aracaṉiyum muṉivaṉiyum aṭikaḷ eṉṟum collāṟ cuṭṭuvatu tamiḻ marapākum.
teṉpulattār (iṟanta muṉṉōr) nāḷaṉṟu irappōrkkum ēḻi yeḷiyavarkkum āṇṭiyarkkum viruntaḷittum, iṟantupōṉa cūliyiṉ poruṭṭuc cumitāṅkik kal naṭṭum, vēṉiṟ kālattil vaḻip
pōkkarkkut taṇṇīrp pantalum mōrp pantalum vittum, āviṟku uriñcutaṟi naṭṭiyum, vilaṅkukaṭkut taṇṇīrt toṭṭi kaṭṭiyum, palavāṟu aṟañceytu vantaṉar.
	aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇa rōmpalum
	tuṟavōrk ketirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
	viruntetir kōṭalum 		                    (cilap.16:71-3)
illaṟattāral iyaṉṟavari kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. mutaṟkāla antaṇar tamiḻa muṉivar.
vīṭu kaṭṭumpōtu, ayalārum āṇṭiyarum paṭuttuṟaṅkat terut tiṇṇi amikkappeṟṟatu. māṭaṅkaḷiṉ ucciyil kāṭṭup puṟākkaḷ taṅkaṟkup purikaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. cāraṅkaḷ iṭṭa tuḷikaḷum kuruvikaḷ kūṭukaṭṭa viṭappaṭṭaṉa. kōyil maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭukaḷ vuvālkaḷ taṅkumāṟu ariyiruṭ ṭaṟikaḷāy amikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi vuvāl natti eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ayal nāṭṭārum vaḻippōkkarum taṅku
vataṟku ūrār ūr maṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vittaṉar.
uṭikō vaṇamuṇṭuṟaṅkap puṟantiṇṇiyuṇṭu
cakamuḻutum, paṭukkap puṟantiṇṇi yeṅkeṅkumuṇṭu
eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
	karuṅkik kollaṉ irumpuvicit teṟinta
	kūṭat tiṇṇici verīi māṭat
	tiṟiyuṟi puṟaviṉ ceṅkāṟ cēval
	iṉṟuyil iriyum poṉtuñcu viyaṉakar
eṉpatu (perumpāṇ. 437-440), māṭap puṟāvik kuṟittal kāṇka.
kūri vīṭukaḷiṉ iṟappilum ciṭṭuk kuruvikaḷ taṅkum. itaṉāl, piṟappiṟappilē, eṉṟu ciṭṭukkuruvikkum civaperu māṉukkum iraṭṭuṟalākac coṉṉār kāḷamēkaṉār. makkaḷi nampi aṭikkalam pukuvataṉālēyē, vīṭṭuk kuruvikku aṭikkalāṉ eṉṟu peyar vantatu.
tiruvīḻimiḻaliyil oru vuvāl natti maṇṭapam iṉṟum irukkiṉṟatu.
peṇṭir tam vīṭṭu muṟṟattiṟ kōla miṭuvataṟku aricu māvip payaṉpaṭuttiyatu, eṟumpiṟku  uṇavātaṟ poruṭṭākum.
tamiḻar ellārum potuvāka aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭi vantaṉar. vīṭṭil vaḷarppaṉavum mikappayaṉpaṭuvaṉavum kaṇṇiṟ kiṉiyaṉavumāṉa nilittiṇi (tāvaram) ōṭuyiri paṟavi mutaliya
vaṟṟip piḷḷikaḷip pōṉṟē pēṇiṉar. ataṉāl avaṟṟiṉ iḷamikkup piḷḷip peyar tōṉṟiyatōṭu, avaṟṟip piḷḷip pēṟillātavarkaḷ piḷḷikaḷākavē karuti vaḷarkkum vaḻakkamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. irukkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, paṟakkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. teṉṉam piḷḷi pōṉṟatu irukkum piḷḷi; kīrippiḷḷi pōṉṟatu ōṭum piḷḷi; kiḷip piḷḷi pōṉṟatu paṟakkum piḷḷi.
māṭu vaḷarppavarkaḷ, ciṟappāka uḻavarum iṭiyarum, kāḷikkum āviṟkum, muṟiyē, cāttaṉ, cātti , muṭak koṟṟaṉ muṭakkoṟṟi, koṭum
puṟa marutaṉ koṭumpuṟa maruti mutaliya makkaṭ peyarkaḷiyē iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. ataṉāl, avi irutiṇikkum potuvāṉa viravupyer vakiyāka ilakkaṇattilum iṭam peṟalāyiṉa. (tol col. 20-29)
poṅkaṟ paṇṭikiyil māṭukaṭkum oru nāḷi otukki, avaṟṟiṟku aḻakiya aṇikaḷip pūṭṭic ciṟanta uṇavūṭṭi māṭṭup poṅkal eṉak koṇṭāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
ellārum vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻar potu nōkkam.
	paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum nīṅki
	vaciyum vaḷaṉum curakkeṉa vāḻtti 		    (maṇi. 2: 70-71)
viḻāt toṭaṅkuvatum,
	pāraka maṭaṅkalum pacippiṇi yaṟukeṉa
	ātiri yiṭṭaṉaḷ āruyir marunteṉ
eṉṉum maṇimēkalik kūṟṟum (16: 134-5).
	ellārum iṉpuṟṟirukka niṉippatuvē
	allāl vēṟoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parāparamē
eṉṉum tāyumāṉavar parāparak kaṇṇiyum (221) tamiḻariṉ potunala nōkkit teḷivāyk kāṭṭum.
piṟappāl ciṟappilli yeṉpatum, māntar ellārum ōriṉam eṉpatum paṇṭit tamiḻar koḷkikaḷām. toḻil paṟṟiya vakuppē tamiḻar kulappirivākum.
	piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā
	ceytoḻil vēṟṟumi yāṉ. 		          (kuṟaḷ. 972)
	teṇkaṭal vaḷākam potumi yiṉṟi
	veṇkuṭi niḻaṟṟiya vorumi yōrkkum
	naṭunāḷ yāmattum pakalum tuñcāṉ
	kaṭumāp pārkkum kallā voruvaṟkum
	uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppavi iraṇṭē
	piṟavum ellām ōrok kummē
	celvattup payaṉē ītal
	tuyppēm eṉiṉē tappuna palavē.	 	             (puṟam.189)
	nalla kulameṉṟum tīya kulameṉṟum
	colvaḷa vallāṟ poruḷilli - tolciṟappiṉ
	oṇporuḷ oṉṟō tavam kalvi āḷviṉi
	eṉṟivaṟṟāṉ ākum kulam.  		          (nālaṭi.195)
	oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum          (tirumantiram , 2104)
	kulamum oṉṟē kuṭiyum oṉṟē	
	piṟappum oṉṟē iṟappum oṉṟē.                       (kapilar akaval)
tamiḻar kaṭip piṭitta taliyāya aṟaṅkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu, vāymiyum, nērmiyumākum, poy coṉṉa vāykkup pukāk kiṭiyātu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. pukā = uṇavu.
māvali eṉṉum māperuñ cēra vēntaṉ, taṉakku iṟuti nērvatu terinta piṉṉum taṉvāyc col tappavilli.
ūravit tērtalil , iḷiñar mutal mutiyōr vari ellārum kūṭiyuḷḷa maṇṭapattiṉ naṭuvil vikkappaṭṭa kuṭatti, aṅkuḷḷa nampimāruḷ mutirntār oruvar eṭuttuk kaviḻttu ataṉuḷ oṉṟu
milliyeṉṟu kāṭṭik kīḻē vippar. ovvoru kuṭumpilum (Ward) takutiyuḷḷavar peyariyellām taṉittaṉi varinta olikaḷi, kuṭumpiṉ peyar poṟitta vāyōliyuṭaṉ cērttukkaṭṭi, akkuṭattuḷ iṭuvar.  ataṉ piṉ, muṟkūṟiya mutu nampiyār aṅku naṭipeṟuvatu iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyāta ciṟuvaṉik koṇṭu  oru kaṭṭi eṭuppittu, ati aviḻttu vēṟoru kuṭattiliṭṭuk kulukki, avaṟṟuḷ ōr ōliyi acciṟuvaṉi eṭukkac colli vāṅki, aṅkuḷḷa kaṇakkaṉ kiyiṟ koṭuppar. avaṉ taṉ aintu viraliyum akala virittu atip peṟṟu, atiluḷḷa peyari aṅkuḷḷār aṉivarkkum kēṭkumāṟu urakkap paṭippāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, aṅkuḷḷa nampimār ellārum ov voruvarāy ati avvāṟē paṭippar. ataṉ piṉṉarē appeyar ēṭṭiṟ patiyap peṟum, iṅṅaṉam ellāk kuṭumpiṉiṉṟum terinteṭukkap peṟṟavarē ūraviyuṟuppiṉarāvar.
ūravik kaṇakkaṉ nalloḻukkamum nalvaḻiyil īṭṭiya poruḷum uṭiyavaṉāyiruttal vēṇṭum. avaṉ avi yārkkuk kaṇakkuk kāṭṭumpōtu, paḻukkak kāycciya iruppu maḻuvik kiyil ēntik koṇṭu, tāṉ kāṭṭum kaṇakku uṇmiyāṉa teṉṟu uṟuti kūṟavēṇṭum. avaṉ ki cuṭapṭāviṭiṉ, avaṉ āṭṭi naṉṉar (Annual Bonus) ākiya ēḻu kaḻañciṟkumēl kāṟpaṅku poṉ cērttuk koṭukkappeṟum; cuṭappaṭiṉ, pattuk kaḻañcu poṉ taṇṭamum vēṟu taṇṭaṉiyum avaṉ aṭivāṉ.
vaḻakku maṉṟaṅkaḷil, vaḻakkāḷikaḷ tam kūṟṟukkaḷip paḻukkak kāycciya irumpip piṭittal, naccup pāmpukkuṭattiṟkuḷ kiviṭutal mutaliya teyvaccāṉṟu  koṇṭu meypikkum vaḻakkamum akkālattil iruntatu.
cīvakacintāmaṇi nūlāciriyarāṉa tiruttakkatēvar, paḻukkak kāycciya irumpuk kampiyi ēnti, tam kaṟpip paṟṟip piṟarkkirunta aiyatti akaṟṟiṉār eṉṉum katiyum, iṅkuk karutattakkatu. kaṟpuṭi makaḷir tam kaṟpi meypikka immuṟiyiyē perumpālum kiyāṇṭaṉar.
paḻiyaṉūr vēḷāḷar eḻupatiṉmar, avvūr vaṇikaṉukku uṟuti kūṟiyati niṟivēṟṟumāṟu taṟkoli ceytukoṇṭatu, ilakkiyap pukaḻpeṟṟa ceytiyākum.
tāḷāṇmi, vēḷāṇmi, taṉmāṉam, naṉṟi maṟavāmi eṉpaṉa tamiḻariṉ piṟa paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
	viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāḷnutal
	maṉiyuṟi makaḷirk kāṭavar uyir   		           (kuṟun.135)
	vēḷāṇmi ceytu viruntōmpi veñcamattu
	vāḷāṇmi yālum valiyarāyt- tāḷāṇmi
	tāḻkkum maṭikōḷ ilarāy vāḻātār
	vāḻkki tiruntuta liṉṟu  		    (paḻamoḻi.151)
	ennaṉṟi koṉṟārkkumuyvuṇṭām uyvilli
	ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku. 	                         (kuṟaḷ.110)
palvēṟu vakuppār paṇpāṭu
2. antaṇar paṇpāṭu :
(1) aiyaratu:
maṇapparuvam vantapiṉ āṇum peṇṇum tāmākak kūṭi vāḻnta mutaṟ kālattil, maṇakkavilli yeṉṟu poy colliyum, maṇantatāka oppukkoṇṭaviṭattum kiviṭṭum, āṭavar peṇṭirkkut tīṅku ceytu vantatāl, avi perumpālum nērāvaṇṇam, palarkku muṉ uṟuti kūṟi maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷi maṇakkumāṟu, makkaḷ mītu aṉpum aruḷum koṇṭa irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōlum periyōr karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅki ēṟpaṭuttiṉar.
	poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
	aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.''  		       (tol. kaṟ.4)
aṟiyāmi ellāt tīmikkum vērākiyāl ati akaṟṟum poruṭṭup pala tuṟikaḷil mutaṉūli muṉivar aruḷic ceytaṉar.
	viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
	muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūl ākum''  	     (tol. mara. 95)
	uṭampār aḻiyil uyirāraḻivar
	tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉam cēravum māṭṭār
	uṭampiṉi vaḷarkkum upāyam aṟintē
	uṭampi vaḷarttēṉ uyirvaḷart tēṉē''
eṉṟu tirumūlar (tirumantiram,724) kūṟiyapaṭi, uṭampāṟ peṟakkūṭiya payaṉi muṟṟum peṟumāṟu vāḻnāḷi nīṭṭikkum maruttuvak kaliyi, cittar kaṇṭu illaṟattārk kutavi yaruḷiṉar.
iṟivaṉukku uṇmi aṟivu iṉpam mutaliya piṟa paṇpukaḷum vaṭivākumēṉum, aṉpē ciṟantatākak kaṇṭaṟintu iṟivaṉiyaṭiya atuvē vaḻiyākak kūṟiyavar nāvalan tēyattil tamiḻa antaṇarē.
	aṉpum civamumiraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
	aṉpē civamāva tārumaṟikilār
	aṉpē civamāva tārumaṟintapiṉ	
	aṉpē civamāy amarntirun tārē.
eṉṟār tirumūlar (tirumantiram, 270).
	aṉpeṉum piṭiyu ḷakappaṭu maliyē
		aṉpeṉuṅ kuṭipukum aracē
	aṉpeṉum valikkuṭ paṭuparam poruḷē
		aṉpeṉum karattama ramutē
	aṉpeṉuṅ kaṭattu ḷaṭaṅkiṭuṅ kaṭalē
		aṉpeṉum uyiroḷir aṟivē
	aṉpeṉum aṇuvuḷ amintapē roḷiyē
		aṉpuru vāmpara civamē 
eṉṉumirāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭṭum atuvē.
iṟivaṉiṭattil aṭiyār ciṟṟiṉpatti vēṇṭuvatiṉum pēriṉpatti vēṇṭuvatē takka teṉpati,
..........ah amirappavi
	poruḷum poṉṉum pōkamum alla niṉpāl
	aruḷumaṉpum aṟaṉum mūṉṟum
	uruḷiṇark kaṭampiṉ olitā rōyē.........
eṉṉum paripāṭalaṭikaḷ (5:78-81) uṇarttum kārikkālammiyār vēṇṭiyatum atuvē.
	tammi yikaḻntami tāmpoṟuppa taṉṟimaṟ
	ṟemmi yikaḻnta viṉippayattāl - cmmi	vrivāyÃrayattuåḻvarkolv‹ṟugÇvatūumcahṉṟōrkalṉ.''v‹gJ« (nālaṭi. 58) antaṇar paṇpāṭṭi yuṇarttum.
	antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
	centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.
eṉṟu (kuṟaḷ. 30) antaṇar ciṟappiyalpākak kūṟappaṭum aruḷuṭimi, mūlaṉ eṉṉum iṭiyaṉ kāṭṭil iṟantapiṉ avaṉ manti māṭukaḷ kataṟiyatik kaṇṭiraṅki, tirumūlar avaṉuṭampiṟ pukuntu avaṟṟi vīṭukaṭkuk koṇṭupōyc cērttamiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
(2) pārppāratu:
pulavar, āciriyar, pūcāriyār, kaṇiyar, ōtuvār, kaṇakkar eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟuk kalvit toḻil puriyum illaṟa vakuppār pārppār āvar. nūlkaḷip pārppavar pārppār, allatu pārppaṉar. pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉumcol pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripaṉṟu. pārttaṉaṉ, pārkiṉṟaṉaṉ, pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉum aṉaṉīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ; pārttāṉ, pārkkiṉṟāṉ, pārppāṉ eṉṉum āṉ īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ maṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākavē yiruttal kāṇka.
	yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir
	tītum naṉṟum piṟartara vārā
	nōtalum taṇitalumavaṟṟō raṉṉa
	cātalum putuva taṉṟē vāḻtal
	iṉiteṉa makiḻntaṉṟum ilamē muṉiviṉ
	iṉṉāteṉṟalumilamē
	nīrvaḻip paṭūum puṇipōl āruyir
	muṟivaḻip paṭūum eṉpatu tiṟavōr
	kāṭciyiṉ teḷintaṉam ākaliṉ māṭciyiṉ
	periyōri viyattalum ilamē
	ciṟiyōri yikaḻtal ataṉiṉum ilamē.
itu (puṟam.192), entavūrumcontavūr; ellārum uṟaviṉar; oruvariṉ iṉpa tuṉpattiṟku avarē karaṇiyam (kāraṇam); vāḻvilum tāḻvilum ottirukka vēṇṭum; ellām ūḻāl naṭappatāl periyōr ciṟiyōr eṉṉum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭak kūṭātu. eṉṟu cila ciṟanta paṇpāṭṭuk karuttukaḷik kaṇiyaṉ pūṅkuṉṟaṉār terivittatu. ūḻi viṉippayaṉeṉṟum iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭeṉṟum iyaṟkiyeṉṟum, mūvēṟu vakiyiṟ koḷvar.
	maṇkeḻutāṉi oṇpūṇ vēntar
	veṇkuṭic celvam viyattalō ilamē
	emmāl viyakkap paṭūu mōrē
	iṭumuṭ paṭappi maṟimēyn toḻinta
	kuṟunaṟu muññik koḻuṅkaṇ kuṟṟaṭaku
	puṉpala varakiṉ coṉṟiyoṭu peṟūum
	cīṟūr maṉṉa rāyiṉum evvayiṉ
	pāṭaṟin toḻukum paṇpi ṉārē
	mikappē revvam uṟiṉum eṉittum	
	uṇarcci yillōr uṭimi uḷḷēm
	nallaṟi vuṭiyōr nalkuravu
	uḷḷutum perumayām uvantunaṉi peritē.
itu (puṟam.197), nāṟperumpaṭip perunāṭṭu vēnta rāyiṉum paṇpillāri matiyōm; ciṟṟūr maṉṉarāyiṉum paṇpuṭiyāri matippōm; aṟivilikaḷ celvatti niṉiyōm; aṟivuṭiyār vaṟumiyiyē niṉippōm. eṉṟu māṭalaṉ maturik kumaraṉār pāṭiyatu.
	īyeṉa irattal iḻintaṉ ṟataṉ etir
	īyēṉ eṉṟal aṟaṉiṉumiḻintaṉṟu
	koḷḷeṉak koṭuttal uyarntaṉ ṟataṉetir
	koḷḷēṉ eṉṟal ataṉiṉum uyarntaṉṟu
	teṇṇīrp parappiṉ imiḻttirip peruṅkaṭal
	uṇṇā rākupa nīrvēṭ ṭōrē
	āyum māvum ceṉṟuṇak kalaṅkic
	cēṟṟoṭu paṭṭa ciṟumit tāyiṉum
	uṇṇīr maruṅkiṉ atarpala vākum
	puḷḷum poḻutum paḻitta lallati
	uṉṉic ceṉṟōrp paḻiyilar ataṉāl
	pulavēṉ vāḻiyar ōri vicumpiṉ
	karuvi vāṉam pōla
	variyātu curakkum vaḷḷiyōr niṉṉē.
itu (puṟam.204), irattal iḻivāṉatu; īyāmi ataṉiṉum iḻivāṉatu; koṭuttal uyarntatu; koṭuttati vāṅkāmi ataṉiṉum uyarntatu. taṇṇīr kuṭikka virumpiyavar periya kaṭalukkuc cellār; ciṟiya ūṟṟiṟkē celvar. nī oru kuṟunila maṉṉaṉāyiruntālum koṭi
yāḷi eṉṟu vantēṉ. nī koṭā viṭṭālum nāṉ uṉṉi veṟukka māṭṭēṉ.atu nāṉ puṟappaṭṭu vanta vēḷiyiṉ kuṟṟamēyaṉṟi uṉ kuṟṟamaṉṟu. eṉṟu kaḻitiṉ yāṉiyār valvil ōriyiṭam kūṟiyatu.
	muṟṟiya tiruviṉ mūvarāyiṉum
	peṭpiṉ ṟītal yāmvēṇ ṭalamē
itu (puṟam.205; 1-2),niṟinta celvattiyuṭiya mūvēntartariṉum emmiṭam ārvamiṉṟit taruvati yām virumpa villi eṉṟu perun
taliccāttaṉār kaṭiya neṭuvēṭṭuvaṉiṭam kūṟiyatu.
	kuṉṟum maliyum palapiṉ ṉoḻiya
	vantaṉeṉ paricil koṇṭaṉeṉ celaṟkeṉa
	niṉṟa eṉnayan taruḷiītu koṇ
	ṭīṅkaṉañ celka tāṉeṉa eṉṉi
	yāṅkaṟin taṉaṉō tāṅkaruṅ kāvalaṉ
	kāṇā tītta ipporuṭ kiyāṉōr
	vāṇikap paricilaṉ allēṉ pēṇit
	tiṉiyaṉit tāyiṉum iṉitavar
	tuṇiyaḷa vaṟintu nalkiṉar viṭiṉē.
itu (puṟam. 208), peruñcittiraṉār atikamāṉ neṭu māṉañci
yiṭam paricilpeṟac ceṉṟapōtu, avaṉ avarik kāṇāmalē paricu koṭuttaṉuppa, avar ati vāṅkātu,
pala kuṉṟukaḷiyum malikaḷiyum kaṭantu paricil peṟa vanta eṉmītu aṉpu koṇṭu ipporuḷik koṇṭu ippaṭic celka eṉṟu colvataṟku, avaṉ eṉṉi eppaṭi aṟintāṉ? eṉṉik kāṇāmaṟ koṭutta ipparicilip peṟuvataṟku nāṉ oru vaṇikap paricilaṉ allēṉ. tiṉiyaḷavāyiṉum eṉ takutiyaṟintu matittuk koṭuttāl nāṉ peṟuvēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟiyatu.
cāṉṟōr pukaḻu muṉṉar nāṇupa 
eṉpatu (kuṟun.252),aṟiñar pukaḻcciyi virumpāmiyit terivikkum.
	pullā veḻuttiṟ poruḷil vaṟuṅkōṭṭi
	kallā voruvaṉ urippavuṅ kaṇṇōṭi
	nallār varuntiyuṅkēṭparē maṟṟavaṉ
	pallāruḷ nāṇal patintu.
eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷ (155), kallāta oruvaṉ ōr aviyil poruḷaṟṟa vuri nikaḻttiṉālum, avaṉ palar muṉ veṭkap paṭuvataṟku iraṅki, varuttattōṭum aṟiñar kēṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum paṇpāṭṭit terivikkiṉṟatu.
cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷiyiṭamiruntu uṟiyūr pukunta iḷantattaṉ eṉṉum pulavaṉi, kāriyaṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi oṟṟaṉāka vantāṉeṉṟu kollappukum pōtu, kōvūr kiḻār,
vaḷḷiyōrp paṭarntu puḷḷiṟ pōki
eṉṉum pāṭṭip pāṭi (puṟam.47),appulavaṉit tappivittār.
cōḻaṉ kuḷamaṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, maliyamāṉiṉ iru kuḻantikiḷi yāṉi mitittuk kollumpaṭi iṭap pukuntapōtum, kōvūrkiḻārē nīyē, puṟaviṉ alla laṉṟiyum eṉṉum pāṭṭip pāṭi (puṟam.46), akkuḻantikaḷit tappuvittār,
akkālattup pulavar aracariyaṭuttu,araciyal tavaṟukiḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi avaṟṟit tiruttavum, potunalat tiṭṭaṅkaḷi  vakuttuk koṭuttu avaṟṟi niṟivēṟṟavum, aṟivuri kūṟi vantaṉar.
	kāynel laṟuttuk kavaḷam koḷiṉē
	māniṟi villatum paṉṉāṭ kākum
	nūṟuceṟu vāyiṉum tamittuppuk kuṇiṉē
	vāypuku vataṉiṉum kālperitu keṭukkum
	aṟiviṭi vēntaṉ neṟiyaṟintu koḷiṉē
	kōṭi yāttu nāṭuperitu nantum
	melliyaṉ kiḻava ṉāki vikalum
	varici yaṟiyāk kalleṉ cuṟṟamoṭu	
	parivutapa eṭukkum  piṇṭam nacciṉ	
	yāṉi pukka pulampōlat
	tāṉum uṇṇāṉ ulakamum keṭumē.
itu (puṟam. 184), picirāntiyār pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟiviṭi nampikkuk  kūṟiya aṟivuri.
itaṉ poruḷ : viḷinta nelliyaṟuttu yāṉikkuk kavaḷaṅ koṭuttāl, orumāviṟkuk kuṟinta nilamum pala nāḷikku varum. nūṟu cey aḷavu naṉceyāṉālum yāṉi tāṉē tiṉṉumpaṭi viṭṭu
viṭṭāl,ataṉ vāykkuḷ pukuvati viṭa atikam ataṉ kālpaṭṭuc cētamākum. aṟivuṭiya aracaṉ nalla vaḻiyil varitaṇṭiṉāl, avaṉ nāṭu kōṭi poruḷit tokuttuk koṭuttut tāṉum mikak taḻikkum. avaṉ aṟivu keṭṭu nāḷtōṟum takutiyaṟiyāta vīṇ āravārak kūṭṭattōṭu kūṭi, valintu kavarum perum poruḷtiraḷi virumpiṉāl, yāṉi pukunta naṉcey pōlat tāṉum uṇṇāṉ; nāṭum keṭum eṉpatu.
cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi āvūri muṟṟiyirunta kālattil kōṭṭi vāyili aṭittukkeṇṭu uḷḷē cummāyirunta neṭuṅkiḷḷiyi nōkkik kōvūrkkiḻār,
kariya peṇyāṉik kūṭṭattōṭu periya kuḷattiṟ pōyp paṭiyāmalum, neṟ kavaḷattōṭu neym mitik kavaḷamum peṟāmalum, tiruntiya pakkatti yuṭiya valiya kampam keṭaccāyttu nilattiṉ mēṟ puraḷum kiyi yuṭiyaṉavāy, veppamāka mūccuviṭṭu varuntum yāṉi iṭiyōci pōla muḻaṅkavum, pālillāta kuḻavi aḻavum, peṇṭir pūvillāta veṟun taliyi muṭikkavum, nīrillāta aḻakiya vēlip
pāṭuḷḷa nalla  maṉikaḷiluḷḷavar varuntik kūkkuraliṭutal kēṭkavum, nī iṅku iṉitāka iruttal tīyatākum. kiṭṭutaṟkariya valimiyuḷḷa kutiriyiyuṭiya aracē! nī aṟatti mēṟkoṇṭāl itō nakar uṉataṉṟō eṉṟu collit tiṟantuviṭu; maṟatti mēṟkoṇṭāl pōr ceytu koṇṭu tiṟantuviṭu. ivviraṇṭumaṉṟi, uṟutiyāṉa niliyoṭu kūṭiya kataviṉiyuṭiya matilukkuḷ oru pakkattil oṭuṅkik kiṭattal, ārāyuṅ kālattu, veṭkappaṭattakka ceytiyākum. eṉṟu añcātu iṭitturittār. (puṟam.44).
cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi iṉṉoru camiyam uṟiyūri muṟṟuki ceytiruntapōtum, neṭuṅkiḷḷi kōṭṭi vāyili aṭittu uḷḷēyiruntāṉ. aṉṟum kōvūrkiḻār taliyiṭṭu avviruvar ceyalum avar kuṭikkup poruntāmiyik kāṭṭip pōri niṟuttiṉār.
kōpperuñcōḻaṉ taṉṉoṭu māṟupaṭṭa taṉ makkaḷ mēṟ pōrukkuc ceṉṟapōtu, pullāṟṟūr eyiṟṟiyaṉār ataṉ iḻiviyum payaṉiṉmiyiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭit taṭuttu, avaṉi nalvaḻip paṭuttiṉār. (puṟam. 213).
eḻuperu vaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākiya viyāvik kōpperum pēkaṉ taṉ tēviyākiya kaṇṇakiyit taḷḷiviṭṭapiṉ,avaḷ poruṭṭuk kapilar, paraṇar, aricilkiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar nālvar,kalluṅ kariyak kaṉintu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. (puṟam. 143-7).
kuṭapulaviyaṉār eṉṉum pulavar pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻi 
yaṉiṭam ceṉṟu,
 	mallalmutūr vayavēntē
	cellum ulakattuc celvam vēṇṭiṉum
	ñālaṅ kāvalar tōḷvali murukki
	orunī yākal vēṇṭiṉum ciṟanta
	nallici niṟuttal vēṇṭiṉum maṟṟataṉ
	takuti kēḷ iṉi mikuti yāḷa
	nīriṉ ṟamiyā yākkik kellām
	uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē
	uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam
	uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē
	nīrum nilaṉum puṇari yōrīṇṭu
	uṭampum uyirum paṭittici ṉōrē
	vittivāṉ nōkkum puṉpalam kaṇṇakaṉ
	vippiṟ ṟāyiṉum naṇṇi āḷum
	iṟivaṉ tāṭkuta vātē ataṉāl
	aṭupōrc ceḻiya ikaḻātu valalē
	nilaṉ neḷi maruṅkiṉ nīrnili perukat
	taṭṭōramma ivaṇtaṭ ṭōrē
	taḷḷā tōr ivaṇ taḷḷā tōrē. 
eṉṟu (puṟam.18), ōr uṇavup perukkat tiṭṭatti viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ: vaḷamuḷḷa paḻamiyāṉa ūriyuṭiya valiya
vēntē! nī maṟumiyiṟ cellakkūṭiya ulakattil nukarum (aṉupavikkum)  celvatti virumpiṉālum, ulaka aracariṉ tōḷ valimiyik keṭuttu nī oruvaṉē talivaṉāka virumpiṉālum, mikunta nalla pukaḻi ivvulakattil niṟutta virumpiṉālum, ataṟkuttakka ceyali ippōtu kēṭpāyāka. periyōṉē! nīriyiṉṟi yamiyāta uṭampiṟ kellām uṇavu koṭuttavar uyir koṭuttavarāvar. uṇavi mutaṟ karuviyākavuṭiyatu avvuṇavāl uḷḷatākum uṭampu. ātalāl, uṇaveṉṟu collappaṭuvatu nilattōṭu kūṭiya nīr.annīriyum nilattiyum oruvaḻic cērttavar ivvulakattil uṭampiyum uyiriyum paṭittavarāvar. nel mutaliyavaṟṟi vitittu maḻiyi etirpapārkkum nīrvaḷamaṟṟa nilam iṭamakaṉṟa parappuḷḷatāyiṉum, porunti yāḷum aracaṉatu muyaṟcikkum payaṉpaṭātu. ātalāl, kollum pōric ceyyum pāṇṭiyaṉē! iti ikaḻāmal virintu nilaṅ kuḻinta viṭaṅkaḷil nīr perukat tēkkiṉōr, tām cellum ulakattuc celvam mutaliya mūṉṟiyum ivvulakattut tēkkiṉōrāvar. annīrit tēkkā tōravaṟṟiyum tēkkātōrē yāvar. eṉpatu.
3. aracar paṇpāṭu
paṇṭit tamiḻaracar tammi āḷum talivar eṉakkarutātu, kākkum tantiyar pōṉṟē karutiṉar. ataṉāl kāvalar eṉṟum pulavalar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟār.
aracaṉālum araciyalatikārikaḷālum pakivarālum kaḷvar koḷḷikkārarālum, kāṭṭuvilaṅkālum nērakkūṭiya, aivakit tuṉpamākiya veyiliṉiṉṟum kuṭikaḷik kāttu, iṉpamākiya niḻalit tarupavaṉ eṉṉum karuttilēyē, kuṭi aracac ciṉṉaṅ kaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
ñāyiṟu cumanta kōṭu tiraḷ koṇmū
māka vicumpiṉ naṭuvuniṉ ṟāṅkuk
kaṇpora viḷaṅkum niṉviṇporu viyaṉkuṭi
veyilil maṟik koṇṭaṉṟō aṉṟē varuntiya
kuṭimaṟippatuvē kūrvēl vaḷava
eṉṟu (puṟam. 35), veḷḷikkuṭi nākaṉār pāṭutal kāṇka. veṇmati pōṉṟa taṇmiyiyum kuṟṟamaṟṟa tūymiyiyum kuṟikka, aracaṉ kuṭi veṇpaṭṭiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu veṇ koṟṟak kuṭi eṉap paṭṭatu. koṟṟam veṟṟi. aracāṭci niḻal eṉṟum, aracāṭcik kuṭpaṭṭa nāṭu kuṭi niḻal eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
	uṟutup pañcā tuṭalciṉam cerukkic
	ciṟucoṟ colliya ciṉaṅkeḻu vēntari
	aruñcamam citiyat tākki muracamoṭu
	oruṅkakap paṭēe ṉāyiṉ poruntiya
	eṉniḻal vāḻnar celniḻal kāṇātu
	koṭiyaṉem iṟiyeṉak kaṇṇīr parappik
	kuṭipaḻi tūṟṟam kōlēṉ ākuka.
eṉpatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ vañciṉam (puṟam.72).
	palakuṭi nīḻalum taṅkuṭikkīḻk kāṇpar	
	alakuṭi nīḻa lavar.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (kuṟaḷ. 1034).
āṭci nērmiyāyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati uṇartta aracaṉ kiyil oru ceṅkōl iruntatu. ceṅkōl nērāṉa kōl. kuṭikaḷit tuṉpuṟuttiya oru cilar ceṅkōl piṭippiṉum, koṭuṅkōlar eṉap paḻikkappaṭṭār.
aracaṉ muṟi (nīti) tavaṟi āṇṭāl avaṉ nāṭṭil maḻi peyyāteṉṟum, aracaṉum kuṭikaḷum uyirum uṭampum pōla neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyava reṉṟum, iru karuttukkaḷ akkālattu makkaḷ uḷḷattil vērūṉṟiyiruntaṉa.
	iyalpuḷik kōlōccum maṉṉavaṉ nāṭṭa
	peyalum viḷiyuḷum tokku.		          (kuṟaḷ. 545)
	muṟikōṭi maṉṉavaṉ ceyyiṉ uṟikōṭi
	ollātu vāṉam peyal. 			          (kuṟaḷ.559)
	kōlnili tirintiṭiṉ kōḷnili tiriyum
	kōḷnili tirintiṭiṉ mārivaṟaṅ kūrum
	mārivaṟaṅ kūriṉ maṉṉuyir illi
	maṉṉuyir ellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ
	taṉṉuyir eṉṉum takutiyiṉ ṟākum.	       (maṇi 7;8-12)
	maḻivaḷam karappiṉ vāṉpē raccam
	piḻiuyir eytiṉ perumpē raccam
	kuṭipura vuṇṭum koṭuṅkōl añci
	maṉpati kākkum naṉkuṭip piṟattal	
	tuṉpa mallatu toḻutaka vil.	          (cilap. 215 100-109)
	māri poyppiṉ vāri kuṉṟiṉum
	iyaṟki yallaṉ ceyaṟkiyil tōṉṟiṉum
	kāvalarp paḻikkum ik kaṇṇakaṉ ñālam		(puṟam.35).
aracar kuṭikaḷiṭattu aṉpu koṇṭiruntataṉāl, kāṭcik keḷi yarāyum kaṭuñcollar allarāyum iruntaṉar. itu kaṇṇaki vaḻakkāṭṭiṉiṉṟu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
ceṅkōl aracar tam āṭciyum uyirum iḻakkiṉum, vāyc 
col tavaṟuvatilli yeṉpatu, māvali eṉṉum cēramāvēntaṉ 
ceytiyiṉiṉṟu aṟiyalām.
naṭuniliyākak kuṭikaḷiṉ vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟi vaḻaṅkum poruṭṭu, vaḻakkukkaḷiṉ uṇmi kāṇpataṟku, māṟukōlam pūṇṭu nakar muḻutuñ cuṟṟip potumakkaḷ pēccik kavaṉittaṟivatum, vaḻak
kiḻantavar tammiṭam muṟiyiṭa araṇmaṉi vāyilil ārāyccimaṇi kaṭṭi vippatum,teyvaccāṉṟu vāyilāyt tam kūṟṟi meyppippāri ūkkuvippatum, evvakiyilum tupput tulaṅkāta vaḻakkiṉ uṇmiyi aṟivikkumāṟu iṟivaṉi vēṇṭuvatum, paṇṭit tamiḻaracar kiyāṇṭa vaḻikaḷākum.
kuṟṟaṅkaṭkut taṇṭaṉi, irappōṉeṉṟum purappō ṉeṉṟum vēṟupāṭukāṭṭātu naṭuniliyāy niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. koṟkip pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉki kuṟittatum, maṉumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ taṉ makaṉ
mēl tēric celuttik koṉṟatum, iti valiyuṟuttum.
nāḷtōṟum kāliyil muracaṟivittuk koṭi vaḻaṅki yatuṭaṉ, avvappōtu ciṟu cōṟṟu viḻāvum aracar naṭatti vantaṉar. ūṉ cōṟ ṟuruṇṭi vantavarkkellām vaḻaṅkuvatu ciṟu cōṟṟu viḻāvākum.
aracar tam piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭamākiya veḷḷaṇi viḻā vaṉṟu, ciṟiyāḷikaḷi viṭutali ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
aṭittaḷi nīkkum veḷḷaṇiyām (cilap. 27. 229)
aracar aṟiñariṉ aṟivurikaṭkuc cevicāyttatōṭu, avar coṉṉa kuṟikaḷiyum nīkkivantaṉar.
maṉumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ talinakarākiya tiruvārūril kaṭṭap
peṟṟirunta ārāycci maṇiyi, kaṉṟiyiḻanta ōr āvum payaṉ
paṭuttiṟṟu. ati avvaracaṉum ōr uyartiṇic ceyal pōṉṟē ēṟṟu, taṉ orē makaṉmītu tērōṭṭi muṟi ceytāṉ. ittakiya ceyti vēṟennāṭṭu varalāṟṟilumilli. paṇṭit tamiḻ vēntar mūvarum iṅṅaṉamē tammiyun tam makkaḷiyum naṭuniliyāyt taṇṭaṉik
kuḷḷākki vantaṉar.
veḷḷikkuṭi nākaṉār cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉippāṭi, tam nilavari niluvi nīkkappeṟṟār.
kūṉar kuṟaḷar ūmiyar  ceviṭar mutaliya eccappiṟaviyar araṇmaṉikaḷil araciyar irukkum uvaḷakaṅkaḷil kuṟṟēval ceyya amarttap peṟṟaṉar.
	kūṉum kuṟaḷum ūmum kūṭiya
	kuṟuntoḻil iḷiñar ceṟintucūḻtara

kōpperun tēvi ceṉṟutaṉ tīkkaṉāt tiṟamurippa 
(cilap. 20: 17-21)
palvēṟu camayak kuravar tattam camayamē uṇmiyeṉṟu 
makkaḷi mayakki vantatāl, avaṟṟiṉ uṇmi kāṇpataṟku aracar paṭṭi maṇṭapam eṉṉum tarukka maṇṭapatti amittu, ellāc camaya āciriyariyum atil ēṟit tattam camaya uṇmiyi nāṭṭa ēṟpāṭu ceytiruntaṉar.
	oṭṭiya camayat tuṟuporuḷ vātikaḷ
	paṭṭimaṇ ṭapattup pāṅkaṟin tēṟumiṉ'' 
eṉṟu, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil vēntaṉ (intira) viḻāvi aracaṉ ēvalāṟpaṟiyaṟintaṟivitta vaḷḷuvaṉ kūṟiṉami kāṇka. (maṇi.1:61).
pōrviṉiyil mutaliliruntu muṭivuvari palvēṟu aṟaṅkaḷ kaṭippiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. vēṟṟu nāṭṭik koṭuṅkōl aracar āṭci
yiṉiṉṟu viṭuvikkac ceyyum aṟappōrāyiṉum, maṇṇāci yāṟ piṟa nāṭṭiṉ mēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu tākkum maṟappōrāyiṉum, vēṟṟu nāṭṭu mulli nilattiluḷḷaṉavum tīṅkaṟṟaṉavum tīṅku ceyyat tākataṉavumāṉa āviṉ mantikaḷi, pōriṟcēta muṟātapaṭi tappuvit
taṟku, pōrtoṭaṅkum aracaṉ taṉ paṭiyi ēvik kaḷavākak kavarntu koṇṭu  varac ceytu kāppatu vaḻakkam. itu poruḷilakkaṇattil puṟapporuṭ pakutiyil veṭcittiṇi yeṉappaṭum.
	vēntuviṭu muṉiñar vēṟṟuppulak kaḷaviṉ
	ātan tōmpal mēvaṟ ṟākum.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam veṭcit tiṇiyilakkaṇam kūṟutal kāṇka. (puṟam. 2). nōyiṉṟuyttal (ākkaḷic cētamiṉṟi ōṭṭik koṇṭu varal eṉṉum veṭcit tuṟiyum iti valiyuṟuttum). `pātīṭu eṉṉum tuṟi, paṭittalivaṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi maṟavar ākkaḷit tamakkuṭ pakuttuk kāttalik kuṟikkum. pātukāttal eṉṉum col itiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṟṟu. pātu paṅku.
kuṟitta iṭattil pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ, akkam pakkattuḷḷa taṉippaṭṭa ākkaḷiyum āvippōl aminta iyalpuḷḷa aṟiñariyum, peṇṭiriyum nōyāḷikaḷiyum piḷḷi peṟāta makaḷiriyum, avviṭatti viṭṭakaṉṟu pātukāppāṉa iṭattiṟ cērntukoḷḷumāṟu muṉṉaṟivippatu marapu.
	āvum āṉiyaṟ pārppaṉa mākkaḷum
	peṇṭirum piṇiyuṭi yīrum pēṇit
	teṉpula vāḻnark karuṅkaṭaṉ iṟukkum
	poṉpōṟ putalvarp peṟāatīrum
	emampu kaṭiviṭutum numaraṇ cērmiṉeṉa
	aṟattāṟu nuvalum pūṭki maṟattiṉ
eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ (9) kūṟutal kāṇka. pārppār eṉpatu, āriyar varumuṉ tamiḻp pārppaṉariyum, avar vantapiṉ pirāmaṇariyum, kuṟittatu.
eḷiya paṭikkalamuḷḷavaṉ, kīḻē viḻuntavaṉ, muṭikulin tavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭukiṉṟavaṉ ākiyōr mītu paṭikkalatti ēvāmiyum, iravil pōri niṟuttutalum, pācaṟi pukuntu pakivarit tākkāmiyum, tōṟṟa aracaṉik kollātu tiṟi celuttac ceytalum, iyalumāyiṉ avaṉoṭu maṇavuṟavu koḷḷutalum, piṟa pōraṟaṅkaḷām.
akkālattiṟ paṭittalivar pōṉṟē aracarum pōrkkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itu avar poṟuppuṇarcciyiyum maṟattiyum kāṭṭum.
pōril mutukiṟ puṇpaṭṭapōtum, vāḻkkiyil taṉmāṉaṅ keṭa ētēṉum nērnta pōtum, aracar vaṭakku nōkki uṇṇā nōṉpiruntu uyir tuṟantaṉar. itu vaṭakkiruttal eṉappaṭum. cēramāṉ peruñ
cēralātaṉ cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉoṭu porutupaṭṭa puṟappuṇ nāṇiyum, kōpperuñ cōḻaṉ taṉ makkaḷ taṉṉoṭu  poravanta nikaḻccipaṟṟiya akappuṇṇālum vaṭakkiruntaṉar.
 cēramāṉ kaṇikkā lirumpoṟi cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉōṭu porutu ciṟippaṭ ṭiruntapōtu, taṇṇīr kēṭṭuk kālan tāḻttup peṟṟatiṉāṟ parukātu iṟantāṉ.
akkālattaracar ciṟanta pulavarāyum pāvalarāyum iruntaṉar. āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ, cēramāṉ kaṇikkālirumpoṟi, pālipāṭiyaperuṅ kaṭuṅkō mutaliyōr pāṭiyuḷḷavi ciṟanta pāmaṇi
kaḷākum. cēramāṉ takaṭūreṟinta peruñ cēralirumpoṟi, muracu kaṭṭiliṉ mēl aṟiyātu ēṟittuyiṉṟa mōcikīraṉārkku, avar eḻumaḷavum kavari vīciyatum paṇpāṭṭuc ceyalē.
muttamiḻ vēntaruḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉ ceṅkōṟkuc ciṟantavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ nāṭu tīṅkaṟṟateṉṟum, nāṟṟiciyum pērum pukaḻum paraviyiruntatu.
	maṉpati kākkum nīḷ kuṭic ciṟanta
	teṉpulaṅ kāvaliṉ ōrīippiṟar
	vaṉpulaṅ kāvaliṉ māṟiyāṉ piṟakkē
eṉpatu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ vañciṉam. (puṟam. 71).
	teṉṉavaṉ nāṭṭuc ciṟappum ceykiyum
	kaṇmaṇi kuḷirppak kaṇṭēṉ ātaliṉ	
	vāḻttivan tiruntēṉ ituveṉ varavu
eṉpatu māṅkāṭṭu maṟiyōṉ kūṟṟu. (cilap. 11:54-56).
	kaṭuṅkatir vēṉilik kāriki poṟāaḷ
	paṭintila cīṟaṭi paralveṅ kāṉattuk
	kōḷval uḷiyamum koṭumpuṟ ṟakaḻā
	vāḷvari vēṅkiyum māṉkaṇam maṟalā
	aravum cūrum iritēr mutaliyum
	urumum cārntavark kuṟukaṇ ceyyā
	ceṅkōl teṉṉavar kākkum nāṭeṉa
	eṅkaṇum pōkiya iciyō peritē
	pakaloḷi taṉṉiṉum palluyir ōmpum
	nilavoḷi viḷakkiṉ nīḷiṭi maruṅkiṉ
	iraviṭik kaḻitaṟ kētam il. 
eṉpatu kōvalaṉ kūṟṟu (cilap. 13: 3-12).
	poṉcey kollaṉ taṉcoṟ kēṭṭa
	yāṉō aracaṉ yāṉē kaḷvaṉ
	maṉpati kākkum teṉpulaṅ kāval	
	eṉmutaṟ piḻittatu keṭukaveṉ āyuḷeṉa
	maṉṉavaṉ mayaṅkivīḻn taṉaṉē. 
eṉṟatu āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉi(cilap. 20: 74-78).
4. vaṇikar paṇpāṭu
akkālattu  vāṇikar,
	vāṇikam ceyvārkku vāṇikam pēṇip
	piṟavum tamapōṟ ceyiṉ.
eṉṉum (kuṟaḷ. 120) neṟiyik kaṭip piṭittu, naṭunili pūṇṭu nalluḷḷattiṉarāyp piṟar poruḷiyum tamatu pōṟpēṇi, tām koḷḷum carakkiyum tām koṭukkum vilikku mikutiyākak koḷḷātu tām koṭukkum carakkiyum tām koṇṭa vilikkuk kuṟiyak koṭātu, kuṟṟattiṟ kañci uṇmiyāṉa vili collip palvēṟu nāṭṭu arumporuḷkaḷiviṟṟu, pērāciyiṉṟi nērmiyākap perum poruḷīṭṭip palvēṟu aṟañceytu, vāḻntu vantaṉar.

5. vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu
vēḷāḷar uḻutuṇpārum uḻuvittuṇpārum eṉa iru vakiyar. muṉṉavar ciṟu nilakkiḻār; piṉṉavar peru nilakkiḻār.
vēḷāḷariṉ ciṟanta paṇpu viruntōmpal, ataṉālēyē uḻavaṉ vēḷāḷaṉ eṉappeṟṟāṉ. uḻavut toḻilum vēḷāṇmi eṉappaṟṟatu.
vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka vuṇṇātāṉ 
(tirikaṭukam, 1 12)
virunteṉṟatu uṟaviṉarum naṇparumaṉṟip putitāy varupavari.
	uraṉkeḻu nōṉpakaṭ ṭuḻavar taṅki
	
	toṭikki makaṭūṭa makamuṟi taṭuppa
eṉṟatu (ciṟupāṇ: 190-2) putiyōriyē.
	ceṭṭi makkaḷ vācalvaḻic cellōmē cekkārap
	poṭṭimakkaḷ vācalvaḻip pōkōmē - muṭṭipukum
	pārppār akatti yeṭṭip pārōmē ennāḷum
	kāppārē vēḷāḷar kāṇ.

	puytteṟi karumpiṉ viṭukaḻi tāmarip
	pūmpōtu citiya vīḻnteṉa
