6. piṟavakuppār paṇpāṭu
	uṟuvarum ciṟuvarum ūḻmā ṟuykkum
	 aṟattuṟi yampiyiṉ māṉa
eṉṟu naṉṉākaṉār kūṟuvatāl (puṟam. 381), ōṭak kūli koṭukka  iyalāta eḷiyāriyum irappōriyum, ilavacamāka āṟu kaṭattum aṟavōṭaṅkaḷum akkālattiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
kaḷvar paṇpāṭu
pāli nilattil vāḻnta kaḷvar maṟavar eyiṉar vēṭṭuvar            mutuliya vakuppiṉar, ōruṇavum viḷiyāta kaṭuṅ kōṭikkālattil vaḻippaṟiyiyum koḷḷiyiyum kaḷaviyum mēṟkoḷḷa nērntatu. aṅṅaṉam nēriṉum, paṇpāṭṭik kiviṭavilli. teyva vaṇakkam, eḷiyār poruḷikkavarāmi, pulavariyum muṉivariyum pōṟṟal, viruntōmpal, aracappaṟṟu, īkiyāḷarik kāttal mutaliya naṟkoḷkikaḷik kaṭippiṭittaṉar.
paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ tuḷuvanāṭṭuk kōkaraṇattil piḷḷiyār kōyilil ōkattiliruntapōtu, annakara maṉṉarāṉa pattirakiriyāriṉ araṇmaṉiyil pala aṇikalaṅkaḷik kavarnta kaḷvar, tām muṉpu vaṇaṅki nērntukoṇṭavāṟē piḷḷiyār kōyiṟkuc ceṉṟu, oru viliyuyarnta patakkattip paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ kaḻuttiṟpiḷḷi yāreṉṟu karuti iṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉar.
	koṭuvil eyiṉak kuṟumpiṟ cēppiṉ
	kaḷarvaḷar īntiṉ kāḻkaṇ ṭaṉṉa
	cuvalviḷi nelliṉ cevvaviḻc coṉṟi
	ñamali tanta maṉavuccūl uṭumpiṉ
	vaṟikāl yāttatu vayiṉtoṟum peṟukuvir.
eṉṉum perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭip pakuti. (129-133), eyiṉariṉ virun
tōmpaṟ paṇpi uṇarttum.
kōvalaṉ viliyēṟap peṟṟa poṟcilam paṇintirunta kaṇṇaki
yōṭu kāṭṭuvaḻiyāyc ceṉṟatumaṉṟi, vaḻippaṟittuk koḷḷi koṇṭuṇṇum vēṭṭuvaccēriyilum taṅkiṉataṟkuk kāvalāyiruntatu, kaṇṇaki kaṟpumaṭṭumaṉṟu, kavuntiyaṭikaḷiṉ tuṟavu niliyumākum. māṭalaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṭattum, parācaraṉ palyāṉic celkeḻu
kuṭṭuvaṉiṭattum, ērāḷamāyp poṉṉum aṇikalamum peṟṟu vaḻippaṟi koṭāmal ūr tirumpiyataṟkum, maṟiyōr (pirāmaṇar) muṉivar pōṟ karutappaṭṭatē karaṇiyamām.
pulavarum pāṇarum kūttarum porunarum, avvappōtu vaḷḷal
kaḷiṭam peṟṟa paricilik kāṭṭu vaḻikaḷiṟ paṟi koṭātu vīṭukoṇṭu vantu cērttatum kaḷvariṉ paṇpāṭṭut taṉmiyiṉālēyē.
poyyā moḻip pulavar tañcivāṇaṉ mītu kōvi pāṭi araṅkēṟṟi, avaṉ tēviyāriṭam nāṉūṟu poṟṟēṅkāy peṟṟup pālinila vaḻiyāyc ceṉṟapōtu vaḻimaṟitta muṭṭi eṉṉum vēṭṭuvaṉ, pulavaṉāyiruntatiṉāl poyyā moḻiyāriṉ pulamiyi āyntatum, avarip poruḷoṭu viṭṭatum,
	viḻunta tuḷi antarattē vēmeṉṟum vīḻiṉ
	eḻuntu cuṭarcuṭumeṉ ṟēṅkic-ceḻuṅkoṇṭal
	peyyāta kāṉakattē peyvaḷiyum pōyiṉāṉ
	poyyā moḻippakivar pōl.
eṉṉum viḻumiya veṇpāvip pāṭiyatum, karuti makiḻattakkaṉa. kuṭṭi, moṭṭi, eṉpaṉapōl muṭṭi eṉpatum ōr iyaṟpeyar.
pīcappūr eṉṉum vicayapurac culuttāṉiṉ paṭikaḷ 1659-ām āṇṭu vallattiṉ mēṟ ceṉṟa pōtu, vicayarākava nāyakkarum avar paṭiñarum kōṭṭiyi viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭip pōyviṭṭaṉar. aṉṟu vallattik keḷḷiyaṭittup perum poruḷ kavarnta kaḷvar, culuttāṉ paṭikaḷ tirumpiyapiṉ, tām kavarntatil oru pakutiyi vicayarākava nāyakkarukku mīḷak koṭuttuviṭṭaṉar.
innūṟṟāṇṭilum kaḷvarum koḷḷikkārarum pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ paṇpāṭu kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. maṉṉār kuṭiyilirunta oru paṇṇiyār (mirācutār) tam peruṅ kuṭumpattuṭaṉ irāmēcuvaram pōyp pala vaṇṭikaḷil tirumpumpōtu, vaḻippaṟitta koḷḷik kūṭṭat talivaṉ, appaṇṇiyāriṉ maṉiviyār īkiyāṭṭiyarā yiruntatiṉāl, avar irunta vaṇṭiyi maṭṭum tāṉē kāvalceytu kavarātu viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ.
vaṟaṇṭa nilattup pālivāṇar ikkālattum iṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ, vaḷamikka akkālattil ēṉi nilavāṇar eṅṅaṉam iruntiruppar eṉpati oruvāṟu uyttuṇaralām.
7. peṇṭir paṇpāṭu
uyarkuṭip peṇṭir koṇṭirunta kaṟpuk kuṇaṅkaḷuḷ, nāṇamum payirppum, paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷām. kaṇavaṉ peyar collāmi, avaṉ collit taṭṭāmi, avaṉ kuṟṟatti maṟittal, piṉṉuṇṭal, piṉtūṅki muṉṉeḻutal, viruntu mika variṉum caliyāmi, kaṇavaṉillāk kālattut taṉṉi aṇi ceyyāmi, avaṉ iṟappiṉ uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟal allatu vēṟu vakiyil uyir tuṟattal, avaṉukkuppiṉ uyir vāḻa nēriṉ maṟu maṇam ceyyāmi ākiyavi, kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyar paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
maṟakkula makaḷir maṟappaṇpu, taṉmāṉam, aracap paṟṟu, nāṭṭup paṟṟu mutaliya paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷi uṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar.
potu makaḷirōṭu cērtteṇṇappaṭum nāṭakak kaṇikiyar kūṭa, oru kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻvatik koḷkiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. aracarkkum peruñcelvarkkum kāmakkiḻatti yarātaṟkuriyavar avarē. mātavi kōvalaṉiṉ kāmakkiḻatti yāṉamiyum, avaṉ koliyuṇṭapiṉ makaḷoṭu tuṟuvu pūṇṭamiyum, kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻar mutaṉ mutal nākarikap paṇpāṭṭik kaṇṭum, āriyar varumuṉ ellāt tuṟiyilum taliciṟanta nākarikamum paṇpāṭum uṭiyavarāyiruntum, piṟkālattil ayalāri  nampi aṭimippaṭṭum aṟiyāmippaṭṭum vaṟumippaṭṭum ciṟumippaṭṭum pōyiṉar. itaṉ viḷakkatti eṉ tamiḻar varalāṟu eṉṉum nūluṭ kāṇka.
piṉṉiṇippu-1
mantiram eṉṉum col varalāṟu
teṉcollā vaṭacollā eṉṉum aiyuṟaviṟ kiṭamāṉa teṉ
coṟkaḷuḷ mantiram eṉpatum oṉṟākum. atu vaṭa moḻiyil vaḻaṅku
vatālum, atu vaṭacollēyeṉa āriyar valippatālum, polivoli yuṟṟiruppataṉālum, vāymoḻi yeṉṉum (eḷḷaḷavum aiyuṟaviṟ kiṭantarāta) vēṟoru teṉcol luṇmiyālum, talimip patavi tāṅkum tamiḻp pērāciriyarum tamiḻ varalāṟṟaṟivum moḻiyārāycciyu
miṉmiyāl atu vaṭa collēyeṉa mayaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.
vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷaviyellām vaṭacol allaveṉṟum, vaṭa coṟkaḷuṭ kuṟinta pakkam aintiliru pakuti teṉcol eṉṟum, muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. tamiḻil taṉippaṭṭa polivoli allatu eṭuppoliyiṉṟēṉum, melivali yiṇintuvariṉ poli voliyām eṉṉum uṇmiyi, taṅkam, neñcam, paṇṭam. muntiri, pamparam, kuṉṟam eṉṉum tūyateṉ coṟkaḷi olittuk kāṇka.
(tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟa mutupaḻan toṉmoḻi yeṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, māperuñ colvaḷa moḻi yeṉpatum, karuttil iruttutal vēṇṭum. ataṉ col vaḷatti viḷakkutaṟku, tamiḻ taṉakkēyuriya vīṭu eṉṉum collōṭu, teluṅkiṟkuriya il(lu) eṉṉum colliyum, kaṉṉaṭat
tiṟkuriya maṉiyeṉṉum colliyum, vaṭa moḻikkum piṉṉiya moḻikaṭkum uriya kuṭi eṉṉum colliyum, taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷa
teṉṟu kālṭuvelār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyatiyum, nōkkutal vēṇṭum.) ī,tā, koṭu eṉṉum oru poruṭ coṟkaḷuḷ, ī eṉṉum collit teluṅku koṇṭuḷḷatu pōṉṟē, tā eṉṉum colli āriya moḻikaḷellām koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tāymoḻikkuriya oru poruṭ coṟkaḷuḷ, ovvoṉṟik kiḷimoḻikaḷ koṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu kiḷimoḻit terippu (Dialectic Selection) eṉappaṭum. itu tāy vīṭṭiluḷḷa oru vakip palvēṟu kalaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi 7makaḷir eṭuttukkoḷvatu pōṉṟatu. ākavē, vāy moḻi eṉṉum vēṟoru colluṇmiyāl mantiram eṉpatu vaṭa collāki viṭātu.
oru col emmoḻikkuriyateṉṟu kāṇṭaṟku, varalāṟṟoṭu poruntiya collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum ceyvatē ciṟanta vaḻiyām.
mantiram eṉṉum col maṉ tiram eṉap piriyum. maṉṉutal karututal. tiram eṉpatu tiṟam eṉpataṉ paḻiya vaṭivam; tiraḷ eṉṉum colloṭu toṭarpuṭiyatu. ṟakaram rakarattiṉum pintit tōṉṟiyatu. ataṉālēyē, atu ataṟkiṉamāṉa ṉakarattuṭaṉ neṭuṅkaṇakki ṉiṟutiyil vikkap
paṭṭuḷḷatu.
rakaram muntiyateṉpatum ṟakarattiṟku mūlameṉpatum, muri - muṟi (vaḷi), pār - pāṟi, oḷir - oḷiṟu mutaliya colliṇikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum.
maṉ eṉṉum col muṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. muṉṉutal = karututal. muṉṉam = karuttu, maṉam. muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam. āṅkilattiṟku mūlamāṉa āṅkila cākacaṉiyattilum (Anglo-Saxon) mun eṉṟa ukara vuyir vaṭivamē uḷḷatu. Munnan, to think, an eṉpatu nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca īṟu.
āriya moḻik kuṭumpap pirivukaḷuḷ, āṅkila cākaṉiyatti allatu kīḻc cerumāṉiyatti (Low German) uṭkoṇṭa tiyūt
tāṉiyam (Teutonic), kīḻiyāriyattiṉum muntiyatum vēta  moḻiyiṉum allatu camaṟkirutattiṉum tamiḻukku neruṅkiyatum ākumeṉpatu, eṉ Primary Classical Language of the World eṉṉum āṅkila nūliṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
uraka mutaṟ coṟkaḷ akara mutalaṉavāka māṟuvatu iyalpē.

kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi     	nuri - nari

tuḷir taḷir
immuṟiyilēyē muṉ eṉṉum viṉiyum maṉ eṉat tirin tuḷḷatu. muṉ eṉṉum mūla vaṭivam tamiḻilum āṅkila cākacaṉiyattilum irukka, ataṉuyiriṉam, māntaṉ; maṉpati = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
Man = thinking animal.
kd« - kd°(t.), L. mens, E. mind.
maṉ - manu(va.) - manukṣya(va.) = māntaṉ.
eṇṇum tiṇmi allatu karutuntiṟaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum maṉtiram eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col, mantiram eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
o.nō: maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - manti.
(maṉṉutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, maṉ - maṉi)
muṉ - muṉṟu - muntu
piṉ - piṉṉu - pintu
uṟutiyāṉa eṇṇam (piṅ.) eṉpati aṭippaṭik karuttākak koṇṭuḷḷa mantiram eṉṉum col, piṉvarumāṟu pala poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
1. cūḻviṉi
2. cūḻviṉikkuḻu.
maṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉakkunāyēṉ mantirattuḷḷēṉ
(kamparā. urukkāṭ.31)
3. tirumaṉṟāṭṭu.
tiruvinteḻuttum. tiruvāymoḻiyum pōṉṟavi tirumaṉṟāṭṭām. vāymoḻi = vāykkum moḻi. vāyttal = uṇmiyātal, niṟivēṟutal.
4. camayak koṇmuṭipu nūl.
e-ṭu: tirumūlar tirumantiram.
5. paṭippu moḻi.
illati uṇṭākkuvatum tīyati nallatākkuvatum paṭippu moḻiyām.
6. kaṭṭu moḻi.
iyaṟkip pūtaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkattiyum tīyavuyirikaḷiṉ tīṅkiyum kaṭṭuppaṭuttuvatu kaṭṭu moḻiyām.
7. cāvippu moḻi.
irutiṇip poruḷkaḷiyum aḻippatu cāvippu moḻiyām.
mantiram - Skt. mantra.
cūḻviṉi ceyyum amiccaṉ mantiri eṉṟum, pēykaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭu paṭippum kaṭṭum cāvippum ceypavaṉ mantirakkāraṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟuvar.
kªâÇ-Skt. mantrin, Hind. mantri, Port. mandarim, Ch. mandarin.
mantirattāl tirumaṉṟāṭṭuc ceytal mantiratti uruvēṟṟutal allatu uruppōṭutal, eṉṟum, ataṉāl nōy nīkkutal mantirittal eṉṟum, ataṉāl tīṅku ceytal mantiram paṇṇutal eṉṟum, collap
paṭum.
āriya vēta mantiraṅkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭumuṉṉarē, tamiḻil mantira nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. tolkāppiyam kūṟum eḻuvakic ceyyuḷ 
nilikkaḷaṅkaḷuḷ mantiramum oṉṟu.
	pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
	aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
	vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
	nāṟpeyar elli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
	yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar. 		       (tol: 1336)
vāymoḻiyiyē tolkāppiyar vēṟiṭattil mantiram eṉak kuṟippar.
	niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
	maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa. 		       (tol. 1434)
